internal static class SR
{
	private static List<ResourceManager> _resourceManagers;

	private static int _customResourcesVersion;

	internal static int CustomResourcesVersion => _customResourcesVersion;

	static SR()
	{
		Assembly assembly = typeof(SR).Assembly;
		object[] customAttributes = assembly.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(StringResourceLocationAttribute), inherit: true);
		if (customAttributes == null || customAttributes.Length == 0)
		{
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Assembly is missing the StringResourceLocation attribute.");
		}
		string[] array = ((customAttributes[0] as StringResourceLocationAttribute) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Error retrieving the StringResourceLocation attribute.")).Location.Split(';');
		int num = 0;
		string[] array2 = array;
		foreach (string text in array2)
		{
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		_resourceManagers = new List<ResourceManager>();
		array2 = array;
		foreach (string text2 in array2)
		{
			if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
			{
				_resourceManagers.Add(new ResourceManager(text2, assembly));
			}
		}
	}

	internal static string GetString(string resourceName)
	{
		foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in _resourceManagers)
		{
			string text = resourceManager.GetString(resourceName, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture);
			if (text != null)
			{
				return text;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	internal static void AddResource(string name, Assembly assembly)
	{
		ResourceManager resourceManager = new ResourceManager(name, assembly);
		resourceManager.GetString("DummyValue");
		_resourceManagers.Insert(0, resourceManager);
		ResourceStringBase.InvalidateCachedResources();
		_customResourcesVersion++;
	}

	internal static void RemoveResource(string name)
	{
		ResourceManager resourceManager = null;
		int num = 0;
		foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager2 in _resourceManagers)
		{
			if (resourceManager2.BaseName == name)
			{
				resourceManager = resourceManager2;
				break;
			}
			num++;
		}
		if (resourceManager != null)
		{
			_resourceManagers.Remove(resourceManager);
			ResourceStringBase.InvalidateCachedResources();
			_customResourcesVersion++;
		}
	}

	internal static string GetString(string resourceName, params object[] args)
	{
		string text = GetString(resourceName);
		if (text != null && args != null && args.Length != 0 && text.Length > 2)
		{
			try
			{
				text = string.Format(null, text, args);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		return text;
	}
}

	public class StringToCharTypeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			return sourceType == typeof(string);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			return destinationType == typeof(string);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			string text = (string)value;
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				return text[0];
			}
			return ' ';
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			return ((char)value).ToString();
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Controls.Editors
{
	public class ComboEditorItem : ComboEditorItemBase<ComboEditorItemControl>
	{
		public XamComboEditor ComboEditor { get; private set; }

		protected internal override bool MeasureRaised
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Control != null)
				{
					return base.Control.MeasureRaised;
				}
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Control != null)
				{
					base.Control.MeasureRaised = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal ComboEditorItem(object data, XamComboEditor xamWebComboEditor)
			: base(data)
		{
			ComboEditor = xamWebComboEditor;
		}

		internal void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}

		protected override void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (base.IsSelected)
			{
				ComboEditor.SelectItem(this, clearRestOfSelection: false);
			}
			else
			{
				ComboEditor.UnselectItem(this);
			}
		}

		protected override ComboEditorItemControl CreateInstanceOfRecyclingElement()
		{
			return new ComboEditorItemControl();
		}

		protected override void OnElementAttached(ComboEditorItemControl element)
		{
			base.OnElementAttached(element);
			element.OnAttached(this);
		}

		protected override void OnElementReleased(ComboEditorItemControl element)
		{
			base.OnElementReleased(element);
			element.OnReleased(this);
		}

		internal override void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (base.Control != null)
			{
				base.Control.EnsureVisualStates();
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class ComboEditorItemBase<T> : RecyclingContainer<T>, IBindableItem where T : FrameworkElement
	{
		private bool _isSelected;

		private bool _isFocused;

		private bool _isEnabled;

		private T _control;

		private Style _style;

		public object Data { get; private set; }

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return _isSelected;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isSelected != value)
				{
					_isSelected = value;
					OnSelectionChanged();
					OnPropertyChanged("IsSelected");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsFocused
		{
			get
			{
				return _isFocused;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isFocused != value)
				{
					_isFocused = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsFocused");
				}
			}
		}

		public T Control
		{
			get
			{
				return _control;
			}
			protected internal set
			{
				_control = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return _isEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isEnabled != value)
				{
					_isEnabled = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsEnabled");
				}
			}
		}

		public Style Style
		{
			get
			{
				return _style;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_style != value)
				{
					_style = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Style");
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual bool MeasureRaised
		{
			get
			{
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		protected bool IsDataBound { get; set; }

		bool IBindableItem.IsDataBound
		{
			get
			{
				return IsDataBound;
			}
			set
			{
				IsDataBound = value;
			}
		}

		protected ComboEditorItemBase(object data)
		{
			Data = data;
			IsEnabled = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
		}

		internal void SetSelected(bool isSelected)
		{
			_isSelected = isSelected;
			OnPropertyChanged("IsSelected");
		}

		internal virtual void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
		}

		protected override void OnElementAttached(T element)
		{
			Control = element;
		}

		protected override void OnElementReleased(T element)
		{
			Control = null;
		}
	}
	public class ComboEditorItemCollection : BindableItemCollection<ComboEditorItem>
	{
		public ComboEditorItemCollection(IProvideDataItems<ComboEditorItem> provider)
			: base(provider)
		{
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "MouseOver", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Disabled", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Unselected", GroupName = "SelectionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Selected", GroupName = "SelectionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Focused", GroupName = "FocusStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Unfocused", GroupName = "FocusStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Standard", GroupName = "MultipleSelectionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "CheckBox", GroupName = "MultipleSelectionStates")]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "SelectedCheckbox", Type = typeof(CheckBox))]
	public class ComboEditorItemControl : ContentControl, IRecyclableElement, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private CheckBox _elementCheckBox;

		private ComboEditorItem item;

		public ComboEditorItem Item
		{
			get
			{
				return item;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (item != value)
				{
					item = value;
					ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool DelayRecycling { get; set; }

		protected virtual Panel OwnerPanel { get; set; }

		internal bool MeasureRaised { get; set; }

		bool IRecyclableElement.DelayRecycling
		{
			get
			{
				return DelayRecycling;
			}
			set
			{
				DelayRecycling = value;
			}
		}

		Panel IRecyclableElement.OwnerPanel
		{
			get
			{
				return OwnerPanel;
			}
			set
			{
				OwnerPanel = value;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		static ComboEditorItemControl()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ComboEditorItemControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ComboEditorItemControl)));
		}

		public ComboEditorItemControl()
		{
			base.Loaded += ComboEditorItemControl_Loaded;
			KeyboardNavigation.SetTabNavigation(this, KeyboardNavigationMode.Local);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_elementCheckBox = GetTemplateChild("SelectedCheckbox") as CheckBox;
			AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox();
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			if (e.Property == UIElement.IsMouseOverProperty)
			{
				EnsureVisualStates();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			e.Handled = true;
			if (Item != null)
			{
				Item.ComboEditor.OnComboEditorItemClicked(Item);
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new ComboEditorItemControlAutomationPeer<ComboEditorItem, ComboEditorItemControl>(this);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			MeasureRaised = true;
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		internal void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (Item != null && Item.ComboEditor != null)
			{
				if (!Item.IsEnabled)
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: false, (base.Content is Control) ? "Normal" : "Disabled");
				}
				else if (base.IsMouseOver)
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "MouseOver");
				}
				else
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Normal");
				}
				if (Item.IsSelected)
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Selected");
				}
				else
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Unselected");
				}
				if (Item.IsFocused)
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Focused");
				}
				else
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Unfocused");
				}
				if (Item.ComboEditor.CheckBoxVisibility.Equals(Visibility.Visible))
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: false, "CheckBox");
				}
				else
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: false, "Standard");
				}
			}
		}

		private void GoToState(bool useTransitions, string stateName)
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions);
		}

		private void AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox()
		{
			if (_elementCheckBox != null && Item != null)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding("IsSelected");
				binding.Source = Item;
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
				_elementCheckBox.SetBinding(ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAttached(ComboEditorItem comboEditorItem)
		{
			base.IsEnabled = comboEditorItem.IsEnabled;
			Item = comboEditorItem;
			if (Item.ComboEditor.ItemTemplate != null)
			{
				base.Content = comboEditorItem.Data;
				base.ContentTemplate = comboEditorItem.ComboEditor.ItemTemplate;
			}
			else
			{
				string text = comboEditorItem.ComboEditor.DisplayMemberPathResolved;
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = "";
				}
				Binding binding = new Binding(text);
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
				binding.Source = comboEditorItem.Data;
				SetBinding(ContentControl.ContentProperty, binding);
			}
			Style style = ((Item.Style == null) ? Item.ComboEditor.ItemContainerStyle : Item.Style);
			if (base.Style != style)
			{
				if (style != null)
				{
					base.Style = style;
				}
				else
				{
					ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
				}
			}
			Item.PropertyChanged += Item_PropertyChanged;
			EnsureVisualStates();
			AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnReleased(ComboEditorItem comboEditorItem)
		{
			Item.PropertyChanged -= Item_PropertyChanged;
			Item = null;
			base.Content = null;
		}

		private void ComboEditorItemControl_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void Item_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			EnsureVisualStates();
			if (e.PropertyName == "Style")
			{
				base.Style = ((Item.Style == null) ? Item.ComboEditor.ItemContainerStyle : Item.Style);
			}
			else if (e.PropertyName == "Template" && Item != null && Item.ComboEditor.ItemTemplate != null)
			{
				base.Content = Item.Data;
				base.ContentTemplate = Item.ComboEditor.ItemTemplate;
			}
			if (e.PropertyName == "IsEnabled")
			{
				base.IsEnabled = Item.IsEnabled;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}
	}

	[ContentProperty("Conditions")]
	public class ComboItemFilter : IRecordFilter, IExpressConditions, INotifyPropertyChanged, IDisposable
	{
		private ConditionCollection _conditions;

		private LogicalOperator _logicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or;

		public ConditionCollection Conditions => _conditions;

		public LogicalOperator LogicalOperator
		{
			get
			{
				return _logicalOperator;
			}
			set
			{
				_logicalOperator = value;
				_conditions.LogicalOperator = value;
			}
		}

		public Type ObjectType { get; protected internal set; }

		public string FieldName { get; set; }

		public Type FieldType
		{
			get
			{
				if (Field != null)
				{
					return Field.FieldType;
				}
				Type result = null;
				if (ObjectType != null && FieldName != null)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					Type type = null;
					object defaultValue = null;
					if (ObjectTypedInfo != null && ObjectTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors != null && ObjectTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors.Count > 0)
					{
						type = ObjectTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[FieldName].PropertyType;
						defaultValue = DataManagerBase.GetDefaultValue(type);
					}
					try
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FieldName))
						{
							result = ObjectType;
						}
						else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FieldName))
						{
							result = ObjectType;
							flag = true;
						}
						else
						{
							System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression = DataManagerBase.BuildPropertyExpressionFromPropertyName(FieldName, System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Parameter(ObjectType, "param"), ObjectTypedInfo, type, defaultValue);
							if (expression != null)
							{
								result = expression.Type;
								flag = true;
							}
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (!flag && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(FieldName))
					{
						throw new Exception(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidMember"), FieldName, ObjectType.Name));
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public CachedTypedInfo ObjectTypedInfo { get; set; }

		protected internal DataField Field { get; set; }

		string IRecordFilter.FieldName => FieldName;

		ConditionCollection IRecordFilter.Conditions => Conditions;

		Type IRecordFilter.ObjectType => ObjectType;

		Type IRecordFilter.FieldType => FieldType;

		CachedTypedInfo IRecordFilter.ObjectTypedInfo => ObjectTypedInfo;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public ComboItemFilter()
		{
			_conditions = new ConditionCollection();
			_conditions.CollectionItemChanged += Conditions_CollectionItemChanged;
			_conditions.CollectionChanged += Conditions_CollectionChanged;
			_conditions.PropertyChanged += Conditions_PropertyChanged;
			_conditions.LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator;
			_conditions.Parent = this;
		}

		protected virtual System.Linq.Expressions.Expression GetCurrentExpression()
		{
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression result = null;
			if (ObjectTypedInfo != null && FieldType != null)
			{
				FilterContext context = FilterContext.CreateGenericFilter(ObjectTypedInfo, FieldType, caseSensitive: false, fromDateColumn: false);
				result = GetCurrentExpression(context);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual System.Linq.Expressions.Expression GetCurrentExpression(FilterContext context, object dataItem = null)
		{
			return context.CreateExpression(Conditions);
		}

		System.Linq.Expressions.Expression IExpressConditions.GetCurrentExpression(FilterContext context, object dataItem = null)
		{
			return GetCurrentExpression(context);
		}

		System.Linq.Expressions.Expression IExpressConditions.GetCurrentExpression()
		{
			return GetCurrentExpression();
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (_conditions != null)
			{
				_conditions.CollectionItemChanged -= Conditions_CollectionItemChanged;
				_conditions.CollectionChanged -= Conditions_CollectionChanged;
				_conditions.PropertyChanged -= Conditions_PropertyChanged;
				_conditions.Dispose();
				_conditions = null;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private void Conditions_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "LogicalOperator")
			{
				_logicalOperator = _conditions.LogicalOperator;
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("Conditions");
		}

		private void Conditions_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("ConditionsChanged");
		}

		private void Conditions_CollectionItemChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("ConditionsItemChanged");
		}
	}
	public class ComboCell : ComboCellBase
	{
		internal class ComboCellBindingConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public virtual object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				ComboCellControl comboCellControl = (ComboCellControl)parameter;
				ComboCell comboCell = (ComboCell)comboCellControl.Cell;
				if (comboCell != null)
				{
					bool num = comboCell.Control == null;
					if (num)
					{
						comboCell.Control = comboCellControl;
					}
					ComboColumn column = comboCell.Column;
					if (column.ValueConverter != null)
					{
						value = column.ValueConverter.Convert(value, targetType, column.ValueConverterParameter, culture);
					}
					else if (comboCellControl.ContentProvider != null)
					{
						value = comboCellControl.ContentProvider.ApplyFormatting(value, column, culture);
					}
					if (value == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.DataField.NullDisplayText))
					{
						value = column.DataField.NullDisplayText;
					}
					if (num)
					{
						comboCell.Control = null;
					}
				}
				return value;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				ComboCell comboCell = (ComboCell)((ComboCellControl)parameter).Cell;
				if (comboCell != null)
				{
					ComboColumn column = comboCell.Column;
					if (column.ValueConverter != null)
					{
						value = column.ValueConverter.ConvertBack(value, targetType, column.ValueConverterParameter, culture);
					}
					if (column.DataField.ConvertEmptyStringToNull && value is string { Length: 0 })
					{
						value = null;
					}
				}
				return value;
			}
		}

		protected override Style ResolveStyle
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Style != null)
				{
					return base.Style;
				}
				if (base.Row != null && base.Row.CellStyle != null)
				{
					return base.Row.CellStyle;
				}
				return base.Column.CellStyle;
			}
		}

		public ComboCell(ComboRowBase row, ComboColumn column)
			: base(row, column)
		{
		}

		protected override ComboCellControlBase CreateInstanceOfRecyclingElement()
		{
			return new ComboCellControl();
		}

		protected internal override IValueConverter CreateCellBindingConverter()
		{
			return new ComboCellBindingConverter();
		}
	}
	public abstract class ComboCellBase : RecyclingContainer<ComboCellControlBase>
	{
		private Style _style;

		public ComboRowBase Row { get; internal set; }

		public ComboColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public ComboCellControlBase Control { get; protected internal set; }

		public Style Style
		{
			get
			{
				return _style;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_style != value)
				{
					_style = value;
					ApplyStyle();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual Style ResolveStyle => Style;

		protected internal virtual BindingMode BindingMode => BindingMode.OneWay;

		internal Size MeasuringSize { get; set; }

		protected override Type RecyclingElementType => null;

		protected override string RecyclingIdentifier => Row.ToString() + "_" + Column.Key;

		public ComboCellBase(ComboRowBase row, ComboColumn column)
		{
			Row = row;
			Column = column;
		}

		protected internal void ApplyStyle()
		{
			if (Control == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Style resolveStyle = ResolveStyle;
			if (Control.Style != resolveStyle)
			{
				EnsureCurrentState();
				if (resolveStyle != null)
				{
					Control.Style = resolveStyle;
				}
				else
				{
					Control.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual IValueConverter CreateCellBindingConverter()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected internal virtual void EnsureCurrentState()
		{
			if (Control != null)
			{
				Control.EnsureVisualStates();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnElementAttached(ComboCellControlBase element)
		{
			Control = element;
			element.OnAttached(this);
		}

		protected override void OnElementReleased(ComboCellControlBase element)
		{
			Control = null;
			element.OnReleased(this);
		}
	}
	public class ComboCellControl : ComboCellControlBase
	{
		private ComboColumnContentProviderBase _content;

		private IValueConverter _currentConveter;

		public override ComboColumnContentProviderBase ContentProvider => _content;

		static ComboCellControl()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ComboCellControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ComboCellControl)));
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			e.Handled = true;
			base.Cell.Row.ComboEditor.OnComboEditorItemClicked((ComboRow)base.Cell.Row);
		}

		protected override void AttachContent()
		{
			if (_content == null)
			{
				_content = base.Cell.Column.GenerateContentProvider();
				if (_content != null)
				{
					_currentConveter = base.Cell.Column.ValueConverter;
					base.Content = _content.ResolveDisplayElement(base.Cell, _content.ResolveBinding(base.Cell));
				}
			}
			if (_content != null && _currentConveter != base.Cell.Column.ValueConverter)
			{
				_content.ResolveDisplayElement(base.Cell, _content.ResolveBinding(base.Cell));
			}
		}

		protected internal override void EnsureContent()
		{
			if (_content != null)
			{
				_content.AdjustDisplayElement(base.Cell);
			}
		}

		protected internal override void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (base.Cell.Row == null || base.Cell.Row.ComboEditor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!base.Cell.Row.IsEnabled)
			{
				if (base.Content is Control)
				{
					GoToState(useTransitions: false, "Disabled");
				}
			}
			else if (base.IsMouseOver || base.Cell.Row.IsMouseOver)
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "MouseOver");
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Normal");
			}
			if (base.Cell.Row.IsSelected)
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Selected");
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Unselected");
			}
			if (base.Cell.Row.IsFocused)
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Focused");
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: true, "Unfocused");
			}
			if (base.Cell.Row.ComboEditor.CheckBoxVisibility.Equals(Visibility.Visible))
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: false, "CheckBox");
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState(useTransitions: false, "Standard");
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class ComboCellControlBase : ContentControl
	{
		public virtual ComboColumnContentProviderBase ContentProvider => null;

		public ComboCellBase Cell { get; protected set; }

		internal bool IsCellLoaded { get; set; }

		internal bool IsMeasureRaised { get; set; }

		protected ComboCellControlBase()
		{
			base.Loaded += ComboCellControlBase_Loaded;
		}

		protected virtual void AttachContent()
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void EnsureContent()
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
		}

		protected internal void GoToState(bool useTransitions, string stateName)
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAttached(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			ClearValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty);
			Cell = cell;
			AttachContent();
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnReleased(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			cell?.EnsureCurrentState();
			base.DataContext = null;
		}

		protected virtual void OnLoaded()
		{
			IsCellLoaded = true;
		}

		private void ComboCellControlBase_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnLoaded();
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			IsMeasureRaised = true;
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			if (e.Property != UIElement.IsMouseOverProperty)
			{
				return;
			}
			Cell.Row.IsMouseOver = base.IsMouseOver;
			if (Cell.Row.Control != null)
			{
				if (base.IsMouseOver)
				{
					Cell.Row.Control.InternalCellMouseEnter();
				}
				else
				{
					Cell.Row.Control.InternalCellMouseLeave();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class ComboHeaderCell : ComboCellBase
	{
		protected override Style ResolveStyle
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Style != null)
				{
					return base.Style;
				}
				return base.Column.HeaderStyle;
			}
		}

		public ComboHeaderCell(ComboRowBase row, ComboColumn column)
			: base(row, column)
		{
		}

		protected override ComboCellControlBase CreateInstanceOfRecyclingElement()
		{
			return new ComboHeaderCellControl();
		}

		protected internal override void EnsureCurrentState()
		{
			if (base.Control != null && base.Row != null)
			{
				if (base.Row.IsSelected)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(base.Control, "Selected", useTransitions: true);
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(base.Control, "Normal", useTransitions: true);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class ComboHeaderCellControl : ComboCellControlBase
	{
		private DataTemplate _currentTemplate;

		private FrameworkElement _contentElement;

		static ComboHeaderCellControl()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ComboHeaderCellControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ComboHeaderCellControl)));
		}

		protected override void AttachContent()
		{
			EnsureContent();
		}

		protected internal override void EnsureContent()
		{
			ComboColumn column = base.Cell.Column;
			if (column == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = true;
			string text = null;
			if (column.Key != null)
			{
				string[] array = column.Key.Split('.');
				text = array[array.Length - 1];
			}
			if (column.HeaderTemplate != null)
			{
				if (_currentTemplate != column.HeaderTemplate)
				{
					_currentTemplate = column.HeaderTemplate;
					_contentElement = column.HeaderTemplate.LoadContent() as FrameworkElement;
				}
				base.Content = _contentElement;
				base.DataContext = text;
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag || base.Content == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.HeaderText))
				{
					base.Content = text;
				}
				else
				{
					base.Content = column.HeaderText;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}
	}

	[TemplatePart(Name = "Checkbox", Type = typeof(CheckBox))]
	public class RowSelectionCheckBoxCellControl : ComboCellControlBase
	{
		private CheckBox _elementCheckBox;

		private static int measureCount;

		static RowSelectionCheckBoxCellControl()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RowSelectionCheckBoxCellControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RowSelectionCheckBoxCellControl)));
		}

		protected override void AttachContent()
		{
			base.DataContext = base.Cell.Row;
			AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox();
		}

		protected internal override void OnReleased(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			base.OnReleased(cell);
			if (_elementCheckBox != null)
			{
				_elementCheckBox.ClearValue(ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty);
			}
			base.DataContext = null;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			measureCount++;
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			e.Handled = true;
			base.Cell.Row.ComboEditor.OnComboEditorItemClicked((ComboRow)base.Cell.Row);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_elementCheckBox = GetTemplateChild("Checkbox") as CheckBox;
			AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox();
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void AttachIsSelectedToCheckBox()
		{
			if (_elementCheckBox != null)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding("IsSelected");
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
				_elementCheckBox.SetBinding(ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, binding);
			}
		}
	}

	public abstract class ComboColumn : DependencyObjectNotifier
	{
		private double _actualWidth;

		private DataTemplate _headerTemplate;

		private Style _headerStyle;

		private bool _isFixable;

		private ComboColumnFixedState _isFixed;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty KeyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Key", typeof(string), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(KeyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueConverterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueConverter", typeof(IValueConverter), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(ValueConverterChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueConverterParameterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueConverterParameter", typeof(object), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(ValueConverterParameterChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinimumWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimumWidth", typeof(double), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, MinimumWidthChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaximumWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximumWidth", typeof(double), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity, MaximumWidthChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HeaderText", typeof(string), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(HeaderTextChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty WidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Width", typeof(ComboColumnWidth?), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(null, WidthChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Visibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(ComboColumn), new PropertyMetadata(VisibilityChanged));

		public string Key
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(KeyProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(KeyProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public virtual Type DataType { get; protected internal set; }

		public bool IsAutoGenerated { get; protected internal set; }

		public IValueConverter ValueConverter
		{
			get
			{
				return (IValueConverter)GetValue(ValueConverterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueConverterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object ValueConverterParameter
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ValueConverterParameterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueConverterParameterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Style CellStyle { get; set; }

		public double ActualWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _actualWidth;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (_actualWidth != value)
				{
					_actualWidth = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("ActualWidth");
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual double MinimumWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MinimumWidthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MinimumWidthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public virtual double MaximumWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaximumWidthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaximumWidthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string HeaderText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(HeaderTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HeaderTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string HeaderTextResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(HeaderText))
				{
					return Key;
				}
				return HeaderText;
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate HeaderTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _headerTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				_headerTemplate = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("HeaderTemplate");
			}
		}

		public Style HeaderStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _headerStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_headerStyle != value)
				{
					_headerStyle = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("HeaderStyle");
				}
			}
		}

		[TypeConverter(typeof(ComboColumnWidthTypeConverter))]
		[Bindable(true)]
		public virtual ComboColumnWidth? Width
		{
			get
			{
				return (ComboColumnWidth?)GetValue(WidthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(WidthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal virtual ComboColumnWidth WidthResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Width.HasValue)
				{
					return ComboEditor.ColumnWidth;
				}
				return Width.Value;
			}
		}

		public Visibility Visibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)GetValue(VisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VisibilityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal DataField DataField { get; set; }

		protected internal XamMultiColumnComboEditor ComboEditor { get; set; }

		internal ComboColumnFixedState IsFixed
		{
			get
			{
				return _isFixed;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isFixed != value)
				{
					SetFixedColumnState(value);
				}
			}
		}

		internal virtual bool IsFixable
		{
			get
			{
				return _isFixable;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isFixable != value)
				{
					_isFixable = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsFixable");
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInitialAutoSet { get; set; }

		private static void KeyChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void ValueConverterChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void ValueConverterParameterChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void MinimumWidthChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboColumn)obj).OnPropertyChanged("MinimumWidth");
		}

		private static void MaximumWidthChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboColumn)obj).OnPropertyChanged("MaximumWidth");
		}

		private static void HeaderTextChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboColumn)obj).OnPropertyChanged("HeaderText");
		}

		private static void WidthChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboColumn obj2 = (ComboColumn)obj;
			obj2.ActualWidth = 0.0;
			obj2.OnPropertyChanged("Width");
		}

		private static void VisibilityChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboColumn comboColumn = (ComboColumn)obj;
			comboColumn.OnVisibilityChanged();
			if (comboColumn.ComboEditor != null)
			{
				comboColumn.ComboEditor.Columns.InvalidateColumnsCollections(fullInvalidate: true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibilityChanged()
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("Visibility");
		}

		protected internal virtual ComboCellBase GenerateCell(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			RowType rowType = row.RowType;
			if (rowType == RowType.HeaderRow)
			{
				return GenerateHeaderCell(row);
			}
			return GenerateDataCell(row);
		}

		protected virtual ComboHeaderCell GenerateHeaderCell(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			return new ComboHeaderCell(row, this);
		}

		protected virtual ComboCell GenerateDataCell(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			return new ComboCell(row, this);
		}

		protected internal abstract ComboColumnContentProviderBase GenerateContentProvider();

		internal bool SetFixedColumnState(ComboColumnFixedState state)
		{
			_ = _isFixed;
			_isFixed = state;
			if (ComboEditor != null)
			{
				switch (state)
				{
				case ComboColumnFixedState.Left:
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsLeft.AddItemSilently(this);
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsRight.RemoveItemSilently(this);
					break;
				case ComboColumnFixedState.NotFixed:
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsRight.RemoveItemSilently(this);
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsLeft.RemoveItemSilently(this);
					break;
				case ComboColumnFixedState.Right:
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsRight.AddItemSilently(this);
					ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsLeft.RemoveItemSilently(this);
					break;
				}
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("IsFixed");
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class ComboColumnCollection : ObservableCollection<ComboColumn>
	{
		private List<ComboColumn> _internalFixedAdornerColumns;

		private ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn> _fixedAdornerColumns;

		private List<ComboColumn> _internalVisibleColumns;

		private ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn> _visibleColumns;

		private Collection<ComboColumn> _starColumns;

		private ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn> _starColumnsReadOnly;

		private RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn _rowSelectionCheckBoxColumn;

		private FixedComboColumnsCollection _fixedColumnsCollectionLeft;

		private FixedComboColumnsCollection _fixedColumnsCollectionRight;

		private FillerComboColumn _fillerColumn;

		private XamMultiColumnComboEditor _comboEditor;

		public ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn> FixedAdornerColumns => _fixedAdornerColumns;

		[Browsable(false)]
		internal FixedComboColumnsCollection FixedColumnsLeft
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fixedColumnsCollectionLeft == null)
				{
					_fixedColumnsCollectionLeft = new FixedComboColumnsCollection(ComboColumnFixedState.Left);
				}
				return _fixedColumnsCollectionLeft;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		internal FixedComboColumnsCollection FixedColumnsRight
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fixedColumnsCollectionRight == null)
				{
					_fixedColumnsCollectionRight = new FixedComboColumnsCollection(ComboColumnFixedState.Right);
				}
				return _fixedColumnsCollectionRight;
			}
		}

		public ComboColumn this[string key]
		{
			get
			{
				ComboColumn result = null;
				foreach (ComboColumn item in base.Items)
				{
					if (item.Key == key)
					{
						result = item;
						break;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public FillerComboColumn FillerColumn => _fillerColumn;

		protected internal ReadOnlyCollection<ComboColumn> VisibleColumns => _visibleColumns;

		protected internal ReadOnlyCollection<ComboColumn> StarColumns => _starColumnsReadOnly;

		internal RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn
		{
			get
			{
				if (_rowSelectionCheckBoxColumn == null)
				{
					_rowSelectionCheckBoxColumn = new RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn();
				}
				return _rowSelectionCheckBoxColumn;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInitialized { get; set; }

		public ComboColumnCollection(XamMultiColumnComboEditor comboEditor)
		{
			_comboEditor = comboEditor;
			_internalFixedAdornerColumns = new List<ComboColumn>();
			_fixedAdornerColumns = new ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn>(_internalFixedAdornerColumns);
			_internalVisibleColumns = new List<ComboColumn>();
			_visibleColumns = new ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn>(_internalVisibleColumns);
			_fillerColumn = new FillerComboColumn();
			_fillerColumn.ComboEditor = _comboEditor;
			RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn.ComboEditor = _comboEditor;
			_starColumns = new Collection<ComboColumn>();
			_starColumnsReadOnly = new ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<ComboColumn>(_starColumns);
		}

		protected virtual void RegisterFixedAdornerColumn(ComboColumn column, bool first)
		{
			if (!_internalFixedAdornerColumns.Contains(column))
			{
				if (first)
				{
					_internalFixedAdornerColumns.Insert(0, column);
				}
				else
				{
					_internalFixedAdornerColumns.Add(column);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UnregisterFixedAdornerColumn(ComboColumn column)
		{
			_internalFixedAdornerColumns.Remove(column);
		}

		private void InvalidateCollection(IList list)
		{
			for (int num = list.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (!base.Items.Contains((ComboColumn)list[num]))
				{
					list.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void InvalidateColumnsCollections(bool fullInvalidate)
		{
			_internalVisibleColumns.Clear();
			_starColumns.Clear();
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (ComboColumn item in base.Items)
			{
				if (item.IsFixed == ComboColumnFixedState.NotFixed && item.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					_internalVisibleColumns.Add(item);
				}
				if (item.WidthResolved.WidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
				{
					flag = true;
					_starColumns.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				_internalVisibleColumns.Add(_fillerColumn);
			}
			if (fullInvalidate)
			{
				InvalidateCollection(FixedColumnsLeft);
				InvalidateCollection(FixedColumnsRight);
			}
			_comboEditor.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: true);
		}

		internal void InternalRegisterFixedAdornerColumn(ComboColumn column, bool first)
		{
			RegisterFixedAdornerColumn(column, first);
		}

		internal void InternalUnregisterFixedAdornerColumn(ComboColumn column)
		{
			UnregisterFixedAdornerColumn(column);
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewItems != null)
			{
				if (IsInitialized && _comboEditor != null)
				{
					DataManagerBase dataManager = _comboEditor.DataManager;
					if (dataManager == null)
					{
						base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
						return;
					}
					PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptors = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors;
					foreach (ComboColumn newItem in e.NewItems)
					{
						newItem.ComboEditor = _comboEditor;
						if (_comboEditor.DataKeys != null)
						{
							XamMultiColumnComboEditor.ValidateColumn(newItem, _comboEditor.DataKeys, dataManager, propertyDescriptors);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (ComboColumn newItem2 in e.NewItems)
					{
						newItem2.ComboEditor = _comboEditor;
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			InvalidateColumnsCollections(fullInvalidate: true);
		}
	}
	public class ComboColumnTypeMapping
	{
		public Type ColumnType { get; set; }

		public Type DataType { get; set; }
	}
	public class ComboColumnTypeMappingsCollection : CollectionBase<ComboColumnTypeMapping>
	{
		private Dictionary<Type, ComboColumnTypeMapping> _usedDataTypes;

		public ComboColumnTypeMappingsCollection()
		{
			_usedDataTypes = new Dictionary<Type, ComboColumnTypeMapping>();
		}

		protected override void OnItemAdded(int index, ComboColumnTypeMapping item)
		{
			if (item == null || item.ColumnType == null || item.DataType == null)
			{
				throw new Exception("Invalid ColumnType Mapping");
			}
			if (_usedDataTypes.ContainsKey(item.DataType))
			{
				ComboColumnTypeMapping item2 = _usedDataTypes[item.DataType];
				Items.Remove(item2);
				_usedDataTypes[item.DataType] = item;
			}
			else
			{
				_usedDataTypes.Add(item.DataType, item);
			}
			base.OnItemAdded(index, item);
		}

		protected override bool RemoveItem(int index)
		{
			ComboColumnTypeMapping comboColumnTypeMapping = Items[index];
			_usedDataTypes.Remove(comboColumnTypeMapping.DataType);
			return base.RemoveItem(index);
		}

		protected override void ResetItems()
		{
			_usedDataTypes.Clear();
			base.ResetItems();
		}
	}
	public struct ComboColumnWidth
	{
		private static ComboColumnWidth _sizeToCells = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, ComboColumnWidthType.SizeToCells);

		private static ComboColumnWidth _auto = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, ComboColumnWidthType.Auto);

		private static ComboColumnWidth _initialAuto = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, ComboColumnWidthType.InitialAuto);

		private static ComboColumnWidth _sizeToHeader = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, ComboColumnWidthType.SizeToHeader);

		private static ComboColumnWidth _star = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, ComboColumnWidthType.Star);

		private double _value;

		private ComboColumnWidthType _type;

		public static ComboColumnWidth SizeToHeader => _sizeToHeader;

		public static ComboColumnWidth SizeToCells => _sizeToCells;

		public static ComboColumnWidth Auto => _auto;

		public static ComboColumnWidth InitialAuto => _initialAuto;

		public static ComboColumnWidth Star => _star;

		public double Value => _value;

		public ComboColumnWidthType WidthType => _type;

		private ComboColumnWidth(double value, ComboColumnWidthType type)
		{
			if (value < 0.0)
			{
				value = 0.0;
			}
			_value = value;
			_type = type;
		}

		public ComboColumnWidth(double value, bool isStar)
			: this(value, isStar ? ComboColumnWidthType.Star : ComboColumnWidthType.Numeric)
		{
		}
	}
	public sealed class FillerComboColumn : ComboColumn
	{
		private ComboColumnWidth _width;

		internal override ComboColumnWidth WidthResolved => _width;

		internal override bool IsFixable => false;

		internal FillerComboColumn()
		{
			_width = new ComboColumnWidth(1.0, isStar: true);
		}

		protected internal override ComboColumnContentProviderBase GenerateContentProvider()
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class FixedComboColumnsCollection : CollectionBase<ComboColumn>
	{
		protected ComboColumnFixedState Direction { get; set; }

		public FixedComboColumnsCollection(ComboColumnFixedState direction)
		{
			Direction = direction;
		}

		protected override void AddItem(int index, ComboColumn item)
		{
			if (item == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (item.IsFixed == Direction)
			{
				if (Items.IndexOf(item) != -1)
				{
					if (index < Items.Count)
					{
						Items.Remove(item);
						Items.Insert(index, item);
						if (item.ComboEditor != null)
						{
							item.ComboEditor.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					base.AddItem(index, item);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				item.SetFixedColumnState(Direction);
			}
		}

		protected override void InsertItem(int index, ComboColumn item)
		{
			AddItem(index, item);
		}

		protected override bool RemoveItem(int index)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (index >= 0 && index < Items.Count)
			{
				result = Items[index]?.SetFixedColumnState(ComboColumnFixedState.NotFixed) ?? false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void ResetItems()
		{
			for (int num = Items.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				RemoveItem(num);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void AddItemSilently(ComboColumn item)
		{
			if (!Items.Contains(item))
			{
				AddItemSilently(base.Count, item);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void RemoveItemSilently(ComboColumn item)
		{
			int num = Items.IndexOf(item);
			if (num != -1)
			{
				RemoveItemSilently(num);
			}
		}
	}

	public class ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection<T> : ReadOnlyCollection<T> where T : ComboColumn
	{
		public T this[string key]
		{
			get
			{
				T result = null;
				foreach (T item in base.Items)
				{
					if (item.Key == key)
					{
						result = item;
						return result;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyKeyedComboColumnCollection(IList<T> list)
			: base(list)
		{
		}
	}

	public abstract class ComboRowBase : ComboEditorItemBase<ComboCellsPanel>
	{
		private ComboCellsCollection _cells;

		private double _actualHeight;

		public double ActualHeight
		{
			get
			{
				return _actualHeight;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (_actualHeight != value)
				{
					_actualHeight = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("ActualHeight");
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual ComboCellsCollection Cells
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cells == null)
				{
					_cells = new ComboCellsCollection(ComboEditor.Columns, this);
				}
				return _cells;
			}
		}

		public XamMultiColumnComboEditor ComboEditor { get; private set; }

		public Style CellStyle { get; set; }

		public abstract RowType RowType { get; }

		public bool IsMouseOver { get; protected internal set; }

		protected internal virtual bool CanScrollHorizontally => true;

		protected internal override bool MeasureRaised
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Control != null)
				{
					return base.Control.MeasureRaised;
				}
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Control != null)
				{
					base.Control.MeasureRaised = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal ComboRowBase(object data, XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamWebComboEditor)
			: base(data)
		{
			ComboEditor = xamWebComboEditor;
		}

		protected internal virtual ComboCellBase ResolveCell(ComboColumn column)
		{
			return Cells[column];
		}

		protected override ComboCellsPanel CreateInstanceOfRecyclingElement()
		{
			return new ComboCellsPanel();
		}

		protected override void OnElementAttached(ComboCellsPanel element)
		{
			base.OnElementAttached(element);
			element.OnAttached(this);
		}

		protected override void OnElementReleased(ComboCellsPanel element)
		{
			base.OnElementReleased(element);
			element.OnReleased(this);
			IsMouseOver = false;
		}

		protected override void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (base.IsSelected)
			{
				ComboEditor.SelectItem(base.Data, !ComboEditor.AllowMultipleSelection);
			}
			else
			{
				ComboEditor.UnselectItem(base.Data);
			}
		}
	}
	public class XamComboEditor : ComboEditorBase<ComboEditorItem, ComboEditorItemControl>
	{
		private UltraLicense _license;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowFilteringProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AutoCompleteProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsEditableProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OpenDropDownOnTypingProperty;

		public bool AllowFiltering
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowFilteringProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowFilteringProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AutoComplete
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AutoCompleteProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AutoCompleteProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsEditable
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsEditableProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsEditableProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool OpenDropDownOnTyping
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(OpenDropDownOnTypingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OpenDropDownOnTypingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<CancelEventArgs> DropDownClosing
		{
			add
			{
				base.DropDownClosing += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.DropDownClosing -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler DropDownClosed
		{
			add
			{
				base.DropDownClosed += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.DropDownClosed -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<ComboItemAddingEventArgs<ComboEditorItem>> ItemAdding
		{
			add
			{
				base.ItemAdding += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.ItemAdding -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<ComboItemAddedEventArgs<ComboEditorItem>> ItemAdded
		{
			add
			{
				base.ItemAdded += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.ItemAdded -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<CancelEventArgs> DropDownOpening
		{
			add
			{
				base.DropDownOpening += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.DropDownOpening -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler DropDownOpened
		{
			add
			{
				base.DropDownOpened += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.DropDownOpened -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<SelectionChangedEventArgs> SelectionChanged
		{
			add
			{
				base.SelectionChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.SelectionChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		public new event EventHandler<HandleableObjectGenerationEventArgs> DataObjectRequested
		{
			add
			{
				base.DataObjectRequested += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				base.DataObjectRequested -= value;
			}
		}

		static XamComboEditor()
		{
			AllowFilteringProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowFiltering", typeof(bool), typeof(XamComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(true, AllowFilteringChanged));
			AutoCompleteProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoComplete", typeof(bool), typeof(XamComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(false, AutoCompleteChanged));
			IsEditableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsEditable", typeof(bool), typeof(XamComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(true, IsEditableChanged));
			OpenDropDownOnTypingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OpenDropDownOnTyping", typeof(bool), typeof(XamComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(true, OpenDropDownOnTypingChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamComboEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(XamComboEditor)));
			ThemeManager.RegisterControl(typeof(XamComboEditor));
			ControlTemplate controlTemplate = new ControlTemplate();
			controlTemplate.Seal();
			Validation.ErrorTemplateProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamComboEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(controlTemplate));
		}

		public XamComboEditor()
		{
			_license = Utilities.ValidateLicense(typeof(XamComboEditor), this);
			base.FilterModeResolved = FilterMode.FilterOnPrimaryColumnOnly;
		}

		protected override ComboEditorItem GenerateNewObject(object data)
		{
			return new ComboEditorItem(data, this);
		}

		protected override void OnDataManagerUnhooked()
		{
			base.OnDataManagerUnhooked();
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (!(propertyName == "ItemContainerStyle"))
			{
				if (propertyName == "ItemTemplate")
				{
					RaiseItemPropertyChanged("Template");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RaiseItemPropertyChanged("Style");
			}
		}

		private void RaiseItemPropertyChanged(string itemPropertyName)
		{
			foreach (ComboEditorItem allocatedItem in base.Items.GetAllocatedItems())
			{
				allocatedItem.RaisePropertyChanged(itemPropertyName);
			}
		}

		private static void AllowFilteringChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as XamComboEditor).AllowFilteringResolved = (bool)e.NewValue;
		}

		private static void AutoCompleteChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as XamComboEditor).AutoCompleteResolved = (bool)e.NewValue;
		}

		private static void IsEditableChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as XamComboEditor).IsEditableResolved = (bool)e.NewValue;
		}

		private static void OpenDropDownOnTypingChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as XamComboEditor).OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved = (bool)e.NewValue;
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "MouseOver", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Disabled", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Unfocused", GroupName = "FocusStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Focused", GroupName = "FocusStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Selectable", GroupName = "ModeStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Editable", GroupName = "ModeStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WaterMarkHidden", GroupName = "WaterMarkStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WaterMarkVisible", GroupName = "WaterMarkStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Closed", GroupName = "DropDownStates")]

	[ComplexBindingProperties("ItemsSource")]

		public bool IsEditableResolved
		{
			get
			{
				return _isEditableResolved;
			}
			internal set
			{
				_isEditableResolved = value;
				if (_toggleButton != null)
				{
					if (!_isEditableResolved)
					{
						_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else if (DropDownButtonDisplayMode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Always)
					{
						_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				ClearFilters();
				EnsureVisualStates();
				InvalidateSelectedContent();
				SetValue(IsEditableResolvedPropertyKey, _isEditableResolved ? KnownBoxes.TrueBox : KnownBoxes.FalseBox);
			}
		}

		internal bool AllowFilteringResolved
		{
			get
			{
				return _allowFilteringResolved;
			}
			set

				_allowFilteringResolved = value;
			}
		}

					EnsureVisualStates();
				}

		internal string DisplayMemberPathResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPath))
				{
					return _firstFieldName;
				}
				return DisplayMemberPath;
			}
		}

		internal string IsSelectedMemberPathResolved { get; set; }

		internal string SelectedValuePathResolved { get; set; }

		internal bool HasJustOpened => _hasJustOpened;

		protected int DataCount => DataManager?.RecordCount ?? 0;

		protected internal virtual bool? IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataManager != null)
				{
					Type cachedType = DataManager.CachedType;
					if (cachedType.IsPrimitive || cachedType.IsEnum || cachedType == typeof(DateTime) || cachedType == typeof(string))
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private List<T> StartsWithItems
		{
			get
			{
				if (_startsWithList == null)
				{
					_startsWithList = new List<T>();
				}
				return _startsWithList;
			}
		}

		private bool IsEditorTextValidInItemSource
		{
			get
			{
				DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
				if (ItemFilters != null && ItemFilters.Count > 0 && dataManager != null && dataManager.Filters != null && dataManager.Filters.Count > 0)
				{
					return Items.Count > 0;
				}
				if (SupportsAlternateFilters)
				{
					return Items.Count > 0;
				}
				return StartsWithItems.Count > 0;
			}
		}

		private TextBox Editor { get; set; }

		private double DropDownHeight
		{
			get
			{
				return _ddHeight;
			}
			set
			{
				_ddHeight = value;
			}
		}

		private double DropDownWidth
		{
			get
			{
				if (_panel != null)
				{
					return Math.Max(MinDropDownWidth, Math.Min(MaxDropDownWidth, _panel.DesiredSize.Width));
				}
				return double.NaN;
			}
		}

		int IProvideDataItems<T>.DataCount => DataCount;

		List<string> IProvidePropertyPersistenceSettings.PropertiesToIgnore => PropertiesToIgnore;

		protected virtual List<string> PriorityProperties => null;

		List<string> IProvidePropertyPersistenceSettings.PriorityProperties => PriorityProperties;

		double ISupportScrollHelper.HorizontalMax
		{
			get
			{
				if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					return HorizontalScrollBar.Maximum;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		ScrollType ISupportScrollHelper.HorizontalScrollType
		{
			get
			{
				if (this is XamComboEditor)
				{
					return ScrollType.Pixel;
				}
				return ScrollType.Item;
			}
		}

		double ISupportScrollHelper.HorizontalValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					return HorizontalScrollBar.Value;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					HorizontalScrollBar.Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		double ISupportScrollHelper.VerticalMax
		{
			get
			{
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					return VerticalScrollBar.Maximum;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		ScrollType ISupportScrollHelper.VerticalScrollType => ScrollType.Item;

		double ISupportScrollHelper.VerticalValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					return VerticalScrollBar.Value;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					VerticalScrollBar.Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsDPIScaled => Utilities.ConvertToLogicalPixels(100) != 100.0;
