		static ComboEditorBase()
		{
			AllowDropDownResizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowDropDownResizing", typeof(bool), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, AllowDropDownResizingChanged));
			IsDropDownOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDropDownOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsDropDownOpenChanged));
			IsTouchSupportEnabledProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("IsTouchSupportEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, OnIsTouchSupportEnabledChanged));
			ItemContainerStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemContainerStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(ItemContainerStyleChanged));
			MaxDropDownHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxDropDownHeight", typeof(double), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity, MaxDropDownHeightChanged));
			MinDropDownHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinDropDownHeight", typeof(double), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, MinDropDownHeightChanged));
			MinDropDownWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinDropDownWidth", typeof(double), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(double.NaN, OnMinDropDownWidthChanged));
			MaxDropDownWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxDropDownWidth", typeof(double), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity, OnMaxDropDownWidthChanged));
			SelectedIndexProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("SelectedIndex", typeof(int), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(-1, SelectedIndexChanged, DependencyPropertyUtilities.MetadataOptionFlags.BindsTwoWayByDefault));
			SelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("SelectedItem", typeof(object), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, SelectedItemChanged, DependencyPropertyUtilities.MetadataOptionFlags.BindsTwoWayByDefault));
			SelectedItemsProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("SelectedItems", typeof(ObservableCollection<object>), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, SelectedItemsChanged, DependencyPropertyUtilities.MetadataOptionFlags.BindsTwoWayByDefault));
			IsSelectedMemberPathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsSelectedMemberPath", typeof(string), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(OnSelectedMemberPathChanged));
			ItemFiltersProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemFilters", typeof(ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter>), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(ItemFiltersChanged));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(ItemTemplateChanged));
			ItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(OnItemsSourceChanged));
			DisplayMemberPathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayMemberPath", typeof(string), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(OnDisplayMemberPathChanged));
			SelectedValuePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedValuePath", typeof(string), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(SelectedValuePathChanged));
			SelectedValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedValue", typeof(object), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(SelectedValueChanged));
			SelectedValuesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedValues", typeof(object[]), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(SelectedValuesChanged));
			AllowMultipleSelectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowMultipleSelection", typeof(bool), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(false, AllowMultipleSelectionChanged));
			CheckBoxVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CheckBoxVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, CheckBoxVisibilityChanged));
			MultiSelectValueDelimiterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MultiSelectValueDelimiter", typeof(char), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(',', MultiSelectValueDelimiterChanged));
			AutoCompleteDelayProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoCompleteDelay", typeof(int), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(0, OnAutoCompleteDelayChanged));
			CustomValueEnteredActionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CustomValueEnteredAction", typeof(CustomValueEnteredActions), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore, CustomValueEnteredActionChanged));
			EmptyTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EmptyText", typeof(string), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(EmptyTextChanged));
			EditAreaBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EditAreaBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Colors.White), EditAreaBackgroundChanged));
			DropDownButtonDisplayModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DropDownButtonDisplayMode", typeof(DropDownButtonDisplayMode), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), new PropertyMetadata(DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Always, DropDownButtonDisplayModeChanged));
			IsEditableResolvedPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("IsEditableResolved", typeof(bool), typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>), KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null);
			IsEditableResolvedProperty = IsEditableResolvedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			try
			{
				Type type = typeof(Button).Assembly.GetType("MS.Internal.Helper");
				if (type != null)
				{
					MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("IsComposing", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic, null, new Type[1] { typeof(TextBoxBase) }, null);
					PropertyInfo property = typeof(TextComposition).GetProperty("Source", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
					PropertyInfo property2 = typeof(TextComposition).GetProperty("Stage", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
					if (property2 != null && property2.PropertyType.IsEnum)
					{
						_textCompositionStageDone = Enum.Parse(property2.PropertyType, "Done");
						_textCompositionStageProp = property2;
						_textCompositionSourceProp = property;
						_isComposingMethod = method;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		protected ComboEditorBase()
		{
			_reflectChangesOverModel = true;
			_cachedRows = new Dictionary<object, T>();
			base.Loaded += XamWebComboEditor_Loaded;
			base.Unloaded += XamComboEditor_Unloaded;
			base.SizeChanged += XamWebComboEditor_SizeChanged;
			base.IsEnabledChanged += XamWebComboEditor_IsEnabledChanged;
			_timeoutTracker = new DispatcherTimer();
			_timeoutTracker.Interval = _timeoutTimeSpan;
			_timeoutTracker.Tick += TimeoutTracker_Tick;
			_delayTracker = new DispatcherTimer();
			_delayTracker.Interval = new TimeSpan(0, 0, AutoCompleteDelay / 1000);
			_delayTracker.Tick += DelayTracker_Tick;
			_duplicateObjectValidator = new Dictionary<object, int>();
			_valueProxy = new ComboEditorItemValueProxy();
			_backupSelectedItems = new ObservableCollection<object>();
			if (SelectedItems == null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemsProperty, new ObservableCollection<object>());
			}
			if (ItemFilters == null)
			{
				ItemFilters = new ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter>();
			}
			base.SizeChanged += ComboEditorBase_SizeChanged;
			_lastVerticalAlignment = base.VerticalAlignment;
			base.LayoutUpdated += ComboEditorBase_LayoutUpdated;
			AddHandler(UIElement.KeyDownEvent, new KeyEventHandler(XamWebComboEditor_KeyDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(XamWebComboEditor_MouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			AddHandler(UIElement.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(XamWebComboEditor_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			KeyboardNavigation.SetTabNavigation(this, KeyboardNavigationMode.Once);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				Editor.PreviewTextInput -= Editor_PreviewTextInput;
				Editor.TextChanged -= Editor_TextChanged;
				Editor.LostFocus -= Editor_LostFocus;
				Editor.GotFocus -= Editor_GotFocus;
				Editor.RemoveHandler(UIElement.KeyDownEvent, new KeyEventHandler(Editor_KeyDown));
				Editor.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(Editor_MouseLeftButtonDown));
			}
			if (ContentBorder != null)
			{
				ContentBorder.GotFocus -= ContentBorder_GotFocus;
				ContentBorder.LostFocus -= ContentBorder_LostFocus;
				ContentBorder.MouseEnter -= ContentBorder_MouseEnter;
				ContentBorder.MouseLeave -= ContentBorder_MouseLeave;
			}
			Editor = GetTemplateChild("TextBoxPresenter") as TextBox;
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				Editor.PreviewTextInput += Editor_PreviewTextInput;
				Editor.TextChanged += Editor_TextChanged;
				Editor.LostFocus += Editor_LostFocus;
				Editor.GotFocus += Editor_GotFocus;
				Editor.AddHandler(UIElement.KeyDownEvent, new KeyEventHandler(Editor_KeyDown), handledEventsToo: true);
				Editor.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(Editor_MouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			}
			if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
			{
				VerticalScrollBar.ValueChanged -= VerticalScrollBar_ValueChanged;
				VerticalScrollBar.MouseEnter -= VerticalScrollBar_MouseEnter;
			}
			VerticalScrollBar = GetTemplateChild("VerticalScrollBar") as ScrollBar;
			if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
			{
				VerticalScrollBar.ValueChanged += VerticalScrollBar_ValueChanged;
				VerticalScrollBar.MouseEnter += VerticalScrollBar_MouseEnter;
				VerticalScrollBar.Orientation = Orientation.Vertical;
				VerticalScrollBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				HorizontalScrollBar.ValueChanged -= HorizontalScrollBar_ValueChanged;
			}
			HorizontalScrollBar = GetTemplateChild("HorizontalScrollBar") as ScrollBar;
			if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				HorizontalScrollBar.ValueChanged += HorizontalScrollBar_ValueChanged;
				HorizontalScrollBar.Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal;
				HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			ContentBorder = GetTemplateChild("ContentPresenterBorder") as Border;
			if (ContentBorder != null)
			{
				ContentBorder.GotFocus += ContentBorder_GotFocus;
				ContentBorder.LostFocus += ContentBorder_LostFocus;
				ContentBorder.MouseEnter += ContentBorder_MouseEnter;
				ContentBorder.MouseLeave += ContentBorder_MouseLeave;
			}
			if (_panel != null)
			{
				RecyclingManager.Manager.ReleaseAll(_panel);
				_panel.Children.Clear();
				_panel.ComboEditor = null;
				_touchScrollHelper = null;
			}
			_panel = GetTemplateChild("ItemsPanel") as ItemsPanelBase<T, TControl>;
			if (_panel != null)
			{
				_panel.ComboEditor = this;
				_panel.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent);
				_touchScrollHelper = new TouchScrollHelper(_panel, this);
				_touchScrollHelper.IsEnabled = IsTouchSupportEnabled;
				_panel.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty, IsTouchSupportEnabled);
			}
			_rootPopupElement = GetTemplateChild("RootPopupElement") as FrameworkElement;
			if (_rootPopupElement != null)
			{
				_rootPopupParent = _rootPopupElement.Parent as FrameworkElement;
				if (_rootPopupParent != null)
				{
					if (_resizer != null)
					{
						_resizer.PopupResizing -= Resizer_PopupResizing;
						_resizer.PopupResized -= Resizer_PopupResized;
					}
					_resizer = _rootPopupParent.FindName("Resizer") as PopupResizer;
					if (_resizer != null)
					{
						_resizer.PopupResizing += Resizer_PopupResizing;
						_resizer.PopupResized += Resizer_PopupResized;
					}
				}
			}
			if (_rootPopupElement != null)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.Unloaded += _rootPopupElement_Unloaded;
			}
			_multiSelectPanel = GetTemplateChild("MultiSelectContentPanel") as Panel;
			if (_dropDownPopup != null)
			{
				_dropDownPopup.Opened -= DropDownPopup_Opened;
			}
			if (_dropDownPopup != null && _dropDownPopup.Child != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)_dropDownPopup.Child).Loaded -= dropdownPopupChildElement_Loaded;
			}
			_dropDownPopup = GetTemplateChild("Popup") as Popup;
			if (_dropDownPopup != null)
			{
				EnsurePopupsAllowsTransparencyInSync();
				_dropDownPopup.PlacementTarget = this;
				_dropDownPopup.Placement = PlacementMode.Relative;
				_dropDownPopup.Opened += DropDownPopup_Opened;
				if (_dropDownPopup.Child != null)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)_dropDownPopup.Child).Loaded += dropdownPopupChildElement_Loaded;
				}
			}
			base.MouseWheel += xamWebComboEditor_MouseWheel;
			if (SelectedItem != null)
			{
				InvalidateSelectedContent();
			}
			_toggleButton = GetTemplateChild("ToggleButton") as ToggleButton;
			if (_toggleButton != null)
			{
				_toggleButton.ClickMode = ClickMode.Press;
			}
			if ((DropDownButtonDisplayMode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.MouseOver || DropDownButtonDisplayMode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Focused || DropDownButtonDisplayMode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Never) && IsEditableResolved)
			{
				_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			_lastSetHeight = double.NaN;
			if (_rootPopupElement != null)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.MinWidth = ((double.IsInfinity(MinDropDownWidth) || double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth)) ? base.ActualWidth : MinDropDownWidth);
			}
			EnsureVisualStates();
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(MultiColumnComboEditorClearSelectionCommand));
			_flowDirectionTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(this, "FlowDirection", FlowDirectionChanged);
		}

		private void Editor_PreviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_isCompositionUpdatePending && _textCompositionSourceProp.GetValue(e.TextComposition, null) == Editor && object.Equals(_textCompositionStageProp.GetValue(e.TextComposition, null), _textCompositionStageDone))
			{
				_isCompositionUpdatePending = false;
				ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: false);
			}
		}

		private void VerticalScrollBar_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_hasJustOpened = false;
		}

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				if (IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					IsDropDownOpen = false;
				}
				RestoreIsTabStopValue();
			}
			ApplyTemplate();
			OnIsFocusWithinChangedHelper((bool)e.NewValue);
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		protected override void OnLostFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Unfocused", useTransitions: true);
			RestoreIsTabStopValue();
			base.OnLostFocus(e);
		}

		private void RestoreIsTabStopValue()
		{
			if (_isTabingBackwards)
			{
				_isTabingBackwards = false;
				DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, Control.IsTabStopProperty, _originalIsTabStopValue);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnGotFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Focused", useTransitions: true);
			base.OnGotFocus(e);
			OnIsFocusWithinChangedHelper(gotFocus: true);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new XamComboEditorAutomationPeer<T, TControl>(this);
		}

		private static void AllowDropDownResizingChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> obj2 = obj as ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>;
			obj2.AllowDropDownResizingResolved = (bool)e.NewValue;
			obj2._invalidateHeight = true;
		}

		private static void OnIsDropDownOpenChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d;
			if (comboEditorBase.IsInDesignMode)
			{
				comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
			else if (!comboEditorBase._isLoaded && (bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = true;
				comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = false;
				comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = false;
			}
			else
			{
				if (comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging)
				{
					return;
				}
				if ((bool)e.NewValue)
				{
					comboEditorBase._hasBeenOpened = true;
					comboEditorBase._hasJustOpened = true;
					comboEditorBase.SetFocusToControlOrEditor();
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = true;
					comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = false;
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = false;
					if (comboEditorBase.OnDropDownOpening())
					{
						return;
					}
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = true;
					comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = true;
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = false;
					comboEditorBase.EnsureVisualStates();
					if (comboEditorBase.TextEditor != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(comboEditorBase.TextEditor.Text))
					{
						comboEditorBase.ClearFilters();
					}
					comboEditorBase.EnableCloseUpTriggerTrackers();
					comboEditorBase.EnsurePopupsAllowsTransparencyInSync();
					if (comboEditorBase._rootPopupElement != null)
					{
						if (comboEditorBase.AllowDropDownResizingResolved && !double.IsNaN(comboEditorBase._lastDropdownWidth) && comboEditorBase._lastDropdownWidth != 0.0)
						{
							comboEditorBase._rootPopupElement.Width = comboEditorBase._lastDropdownWidth;
						}
						else
						{
							comboEditorBase._rootPopupElement.Width = comboEditorBase.ActualWidth;
						}
					}
					if (comboEditorBase._focusItem == null && comboEditorBase.Items.Count > 0)
					{
						comboEditorBase.SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(comboEditorBase.Items[0], forward: true, scrollToTop: true);
					}
					else if (comboEditorBase.DataManager != null && comboEditorBase.Items.Count > 0)
					{
						int num = -1;
						if (comboEditorBase.SelectedItem != null)
						{
							num = comboEditorBase.DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(comboEditorBase.SelectedItem);
						}
						if (num == -1 && comboEditorBase._focusItem != null)
						{
							num = comboEditorBase.DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(comboEditorBase._focusItem.Data);
						}
						if (num == -1)
						{
							comboEditorBase.SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(comboEditorBase.Items[0], forward: true, scrollToTop: true);
						}
						else
						{
							T val = comboEditorBase.Items[num];
							if (!val.IsFocused)
							{
								comboEditorBase.SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(val, forward: true, scrollToTop: true);
							}
							comboEditorBase.ScrollItemIntoView(val, toTop: true);
						}
					}
					comboEditorBase.InvalidateDropDownPosition(adjustHeight: false);
					comboEditorBase.OnDropDownOpened();
					if (!comboEditorBase.IsEditableResolved)
					{
						comboEditorBase.Focus();
					}
					return;
				}
				if (comboEditorBase._isLoaded)
				{
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = true;
					comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = true;
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = false;
				}
				if (!comboEditorBase._isLoaded || !comboEditorBase.OnDropDownClosing())
				{
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = true;
					comboEditorBase.IsDropDownOpen = false;
					comboEditorBase._ignoreIsDropDownChanging = false;
					comboEditorBase.EnsureVisualStates();
					comboEditorBase.DisableCloseUpTriggerTrackers();
					comboEditorBase.ClearFilters();
					comboEditorBase.OnDropDownClosed();
					if (comboEditorBase._panel != null)
					{
						comboEditorBase._panel.DropDownClosed();
					}
					comboEditorBase.CurrentSearchText = string.Empty;
					if (comboEditorBase._rootPopupElement != null)
					{
						comboEditorBase._lastDropdownWidth = comboEditorBase._rootPopupElement.ActualWidth;
					}
					comboEditorBase.LostMouseCapture -= comboEditorBase._xamWebComboEditor_LostMouseCapture;
					if (comboEditorBase._elementThatStoleMouseCapture != null)
					{
						comboEditorBase._elementThatStoleMouseCapture.LostMouseCapture -= comboEditorBase._elementThatStoleMouseCapture_LostMouseCapture;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public double GetRootBorderWidth()
		{
			if (GetRootPopupElement() is Border { BorderThickness: { Left: var left }, BorderThickness: var borderThickness2 })
			{
				return left - borderThickness2.Right;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		public double GetRootBorderHeight()
		{
			if (GetRootPopupElement() is Border { BorderThickness: { Top: var top }, BorderThickness: var borderThickness2 })
			{
				return top + borderThickness2.Bottom;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		private static void OnIsTouchSupportEnabledChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d;
			if (comboEditorBase._touchScrollHelper != null)
			{
				comboEditorBase._touchScrollHelper.IsEnabled = (bool)e.NewValue;
			}
			if (comboEditorBase._panel != null)
			{
				comboEditorBase._panel.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty, KnownBoxes.FromValue((bool)e.NewValue));
			}
		}

		private static void ItemContainerStyleChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj).OnPropertyChanged("ItemContainerStyle");
		}

		private static void MaxDropDownHeightChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj).DropDownSizePropertyChanged((double)e.NewValue, "MaxDropDownHeight");
		}

		private static void MinDropDownHeightChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj).DropDownSizePropertyChanged((double)e.NewValue, "MinDropDownHeight");
		}

		private static void OnMinDropDownWidthChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> obj2 = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			obj2.DropDownSizePropertyChanged((double)e.NewValue, "MinDropDownWidth");
			obj2.OnMinDropDownWidthChanged((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
		}

		private void OnMinDropDownWidthChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			if (_rootPopupElement != null)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.MinWidth = ((double.IsInfinity(MinDropDownWidth) || double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth)) ? base.ActualWidth : MinDropDownWidth);
			}
		}

		private static void OnMaxDropDownWidthChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj).DropDownSizePropertyChanged((double)e.NewValue, "MaxDropDownWidth");
		}

		private static void SelectedIndexChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (!comboEditorBase._ignoreSelectionChanging)
			{
				comboEditorBase.SelectItem((int)e.NewValue, clearRestOfSelection: true);
			}
			if (comboEditorBase.ItemsSource == null)
			{
				comboEditorBase._applySelectedIndexWhenItemsSourceChanges = true;
			}
		}

		private static void SelectedItemChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (!comboEditorBase._ignoreSelectionChanging)
			{
				comboEditorBase.SelectItem(e.NewValue, clearRestOfSelection: true);
			}
		}

		private static void SelectedItemsChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> obj2 = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			ObservableCollection<object> oldSelectedItemsCollection = e.OldValue as ObservableCollection<object>;
			ObservableCollection<object> newSelectionItemsCollection = e.NewValue as ObservableCollection<object>;
			obj2.OnSelectedItemsChanged(oldSelectedItemsCollection, newSelectionItemsCollection);
		}

		private void OnSelectedItemsChanged(ObservableCollection<object> oldSelectedItemsCollection, ObservableCollection<object> newSelectionItemsCollection)
		{
			if (oldSelectedItemsCollection != null)
			{
				oldSelectedItemsCollection.CollectionChanged -= SelectedItems_CollectionChanged;
			}
			if (newSelectionItemsCollection != null)
			{
				newSelectionItemsCollection.CollectionChanged += SelectedItems_CollectionChanged;
				if (!_ignoreSelectionChanging && ItemsSource != null)
				{
					List<object> itemsToSelect = newSelectionItemsCollection.ToList();
					SelectItems(itemsToSelect, clearRestOfSelection: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void NotifySelectedItemsCollectionAfterItemsAdded(IList itemsAdded)
		{
			SelectedItemsChangedPostProcess(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add, itemsAdded, null, 0);
		}

		private static void OnSelectedMemberPathChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d).OnSelectedMemberPathChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private void OnSelectedMemberPathChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			if (_isLoaded)
			{
				ClearFilters();
				InvalidateSelectedContent();
				InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false, resetItems: true);
			}
			_reflectChangesOverModel = string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldValue);
			IsSelectedMemberPathResolved = newValue;
			FillSelectedItemsBySelectedMemberPath();
			_reflectChangesOverModel = true;
		}

		private static void ItemFiltersChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter> observableCollection = (ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter>)e.OldValue;
			ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter> observableCollection2 = (ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter>)e.NewValue;
			if (observableCollection != null)
			{
				observableCollection.CollectionChanged -= comboEditorBase.ItemsFilter_CollectionChanged;
			}
			if (observableCollection2 == null)
			{
				observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<ComboItemFilter>();
				DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(comboEditorBase, ItemFiltersProperty, observableCollection2);
			}
			observableCollection2.CollectionChanged += comboEditorBase.ItemsFilter_CollectionChanged;
			comboEditorBase.InvalidateFiltering();
		}

		private void ItemsFilter_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateFiltering();
		}

		private void InvalidateFiltering()
		{
			if (_isLoaded)
			{
				ValidateItemsFilters();
				ClearFilters();
			}
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: false);
			}
		}

		private void ValidateItemsFilters()
		{
			foreach (ComboItemFilter itemFilter in ItemFilters)
			{
				if (!CheckMemberPath(itemFilter.FieldName) && DataManager != null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("IvalidItemsFilterFieldName"), itemFilter.FieldName, DataManager.CachedType.Name));
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ItemTemplateChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> obj2 = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			obj2.OnPropertyChanged("ItemTemplate");
			obj2.InvalidateSelectedContent();
			obj2.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false, resetItems: true);
		}

		private static void OnItemsSourceChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> combo = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (e.OldValue != null)
			{
				combo.AttachDetachPropertyChanged(attach: false);
			}
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				combo.AttachDetachPropertyChanged(attach: true);
			}
			if (combo._isLoaded)
			{
				combo.ClearDataProperties();
				combo.ApplyItemSource((IEnumerable)e.NewValue);
				combo._lastSetHeight = double.NaN;
				combo._heightRestrictionsChanged = true;
				combo._hasElementBeenResized = false;
				if (combo.IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					if (combo.ItemsSource == null)
					{
						combo.IsDropDownOpen = false;
					}
					else
					{
						combo._rootPopupElement.Height = double.NaN;
						combo.InvalidateDropDownPosition(adjustHeight: true);
					}
				}
			}
			if (e.OldValue == null && combo.SelectedIndex >= 0 && combo._applySelectedIndexWhenItemsSourceChanges)
			{
				combo._applySelectedIndexWhenItemsSourceChanges = false;
				combo.SelectItem(combo.SelectedIndex, clearRestOfSelection: false);
			}
			obj.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(combo.IsSelectedMemberPathResolved))
				{
					combo.FillSelectedItemsBySelectedMemberPath();
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
			combo.FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValue(combo.GetValue(SelectedValueProperty));
			combo.ValidateItemsFilters();
		}

		private static void OnDisplayMemberPathChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (comboEditorBase._isLoaded)
			{
				comboEditorBase.ClearFilters();
				comboEditorBase.InvalidateSelectedContent();
				comboEditorBase.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false, resetItems: true);
				comboEditorBase.CheckDisplayMemberPath();
			}
		}

		private static void SelectedValuePathChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d;
			if (comboEditorBase.SelectedItems != null)
			{
				comboEditorBase.SelectedItems.Clear();
			}
			comboEditorBase.SelectedValuePathResolved = e.NewValue as string;
			comboEditorBase.FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValue(d.GetValue(SelectedValueProperty));
		}

		private static void SelectedValueChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d;
			if (!comboEditorBase._ignoreSelectionChanging)
			{
				comboEditorBase.FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValue(e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void SelectedValuesChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)d;
			if (!comboEditorBase._ignoreSelectionChanging)
			{
				comboEditorBase.FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValues(e.NewValue as object[]);
			}
		}

		private static void AllowMultipleSelectionChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (comboEditorBase.SelectedItems != null && comboEditorBase.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				comboEditorBase.ResetSelection();
			}
			comboEditorBase.EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private static void CheckBoxVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (obj is XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamMultiColumnComboEditor)
			{
				if ((Visibility)e.NewValue == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns.InternalRegisterFixedAdornerColumn(xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns.RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn, first: true);
				}
				else
				{
					xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns.InternalUnregisterFixedAdornerColumn(xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns.RowSelectionCheckBoxColumn);
				}
				xamMultiColumnComboEditor.EnsureVisualStates();
			}
		}

		private static void MultiSelectValueDelimiterChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			if (comboEditorBase.Editor != null)
			{
				if (comboEditorBase.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					comboEditorBase.SelectedItems.Clear();
				}
				comboEditorBase.Editor.Text = "";
			}
		}

		private static void OnAutoCompleteDelayChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> comboEditorBase = d as ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>;
			comboEditorBase._delayTracker.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(comboEditorBase.AutoCompleteDelay);
		}

		{
		}

			((ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj).OnPropertyChanged("EmptyText");
		}
