		}

		private static void DropDownButtonDisplayModeChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> obj2 = (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)obj;
			DropDownButtonDisplayMode dropDownButtonVisibiltyMode = (DropDownButtonDisplayMode)e.NewValue;
			obj2.SetDropDownButtonVisibiltyMode(dropDownButtonVisibiltyMode);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataManagerUnhooked()
		{
		}

		private void UpdatePosition()
		{
			if (!IsPopupJustResized && !IsPopupResizing && !double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Height))
			{
				if (double.IsNaN(_aboveHeight) || double.IsNaN(_belowHeight))
				{
					Tuple<double, double> tuple = CalcAvailableSpace();
					_aboveHeight = tuple.Item1;
					_belowHeight = tuple.Item2;
				}
				_isAboveState = DropDownHeight > _belowHeight && _belowHeight < _aboveHeight;
			}
		}

		private double GetResizerHeight()
		{
			if (!AllowDropDownResizing || _rootPopupParent == null)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			if (_resizer == null)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			if (_resizer.DesiredSize.Width == 0.0 || _resizer.DesiredSize.Height == 0.0)
			{
				_resizer.Measure(new Size(double.MaxValue, double.MaxValue));
			}
			return _resizer.DesiredSize.Height;
		}

		private double CalculateMaxPopupHeight(bool isAbove, double aboveHeight, double belowHeight)
		{
			double val = (isAbove ? aboveHeight : belowHeight);
			return Math.Min(Math.Max(MaxDropDownHeight, MinDropDownHeight), val);
		}

		private Tuple<double, double> CalcAvailableSpace_NonPrimaryMonitor(Rect screenRect)
		{
			Point point = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(this, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			double num = Utilities.ConvertFromLogicalPixels(base.ActualHeight);
			double num2 = Math.Abs(point.Y - screenRect.Top);
			double num3 = Math.Abs(screenRect.Bottom - (point.Y + num));
			double item = Utilities.ConvertToLogicalPixels((int)num2);
			num3 = Utilities.ConvertToLogicalPixels((int)num3);
			return new Tuple<double, double>(item, num3);
		}

		private Tuple<double, double> CalcAvailableSpace()
		{
			_currentWorkAreaRect = Utilities.GetWorkArea(this);
			if (IsControlOnNonPrimaryMonitor())
			{
				return CalcAvailableSpace_NonPrimaryMonitor(_currentWorkAreaRect);
			}
			Point point;
			Point point2;
			try
			{
				point = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(this, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
				point2 = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(this, new Point(0.0, SystemParameters.PrimaryScreenHeight));
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (!(ex is InvalidOperationException))
				{
					throw ex;
				}
				point2 = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				point = point2;
			}
			point.Y = Math.Abs(point.Y);
			point2.Y = Math.Abs(point2.Y);
			double num = point.Y + point2.Y;
			double num2 = ((num != 0.0) ? (SystemParameters.PrimaryScreenHeight / num) : 1.0);
			double item = SystemParameters.PrimaryScreenHeight / num2 - (point.Y + base.ActualHeight);
			return new Tuple<double, double>(point.Y, item);
		}

		private void Resizer_PopupResizing(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsPopupResizing)
			{
				_originalResizeHeight = _rootPopupElement.Height;
			}
			IsPopupResizing = true;
			IsPopupJustResized = false;
		}

		private void Resizer_PopupResized(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (Math.Abs(_originalResizeHeight - _rootPopupElement.Height) > double.Epsilon)
			{
				_hasElementBeenResized = true;
				_lastSetHeight = _rootPopupElement.Height - GetResizerHeight();
			}
			IsPopupResizing = false;
			IsPopupJustResized = true;
		}

		private void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (IsEditableResolved)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Editable", useTransitions: false);
				IInputElement inputElement;
				if (Editor == null || !Editor.IsFocused)
				{
					inputElement = FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(this);
				}
				else
				{
					IInputElement editor = Editor;
					inputElement = editor;
				}
				object obj = inputElement;
				if (Editor != null && Editor.Text.Length == 0 && obj != Editor)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "WaterMarkVisible", useTransitions: false);
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "WaterMarkHidden", useTransitions: false);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Selectable", useTransitions: false);
				if (SelectedItems != null && SelectedItems.Count == 0)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "WaterMarkVisible", useTransitions: false);
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "WaterMarkHidden", useTransitions: false);
				}
			}
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Open", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Closed", useTransitions: false);
			}
			if (base.IsEnabled)
			{
				string stateName = (base.IsMouseOver ? "MouseOver" : "Normal");
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Disabled", useTransitions: false);
			}
			if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Focused", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Unfocused", useTransitions: false);
			}
			bool num = _rootPopupElement != null && _rootPopupElement.DesiredSize.Height == 0.0 && _hasBeenOpened;
			bool hasError = Validation.GetHasError(this);
			if (!num && hasError)
			{
				if (base.IsKeyboardFocused || base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "InvalidFocused", useTransitions: false);
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "InvalidUnfocused", useTransitions: false);
				}
				return;
			}
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Valid", useTransitions: false);
			if (hasError)
			{
				InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		private void ProcessEditorText(bool allowDropDown)
		{
			ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown, attemptAutoComplete: true);
		}

		private void ProcessEditorText(bool allowDropDown, bool attemptAutoComplete)
		{
			if (_isTextCommitted || !IsEditableResolved)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = Editor.Text;
			if (text.Length == 0)
			{
				VerticalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
				ClearFilters();
				CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: false, cancelSelection: true, moveSelectionToTheEnd: false, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
				if (SelectedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					SelectedItems.Clear();
				}
				CurrentSearchText = text;
			}
			else
			{
				_supressEditorTextUpdates = true;
				if (SelectedItem != null && ResolveText(SelectedItem) == text)
				{
					_isTextCommitted = true;
					SearchAndFilterItemsByText(text, performAutoComplete: true);
					if (OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved && allowDropDown && !IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						DropDownOpenedViaTyping = true;
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
						Editor.Focus();
					}
					return;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				string[] array = new string[1] { text };
				if (AllowMultipleSelection)
				{
					array = text.Split(MultiSelectValueDelimiter);
					if (array.Length != 0)
					{
						text = array[array.Length - 1];
					}
				}
				string text2 = Editor.Text;
				_ = SelectedItems.Count;
				int selectionStart = Editor.SelectionStart;
				ValidateSelection(Editor.Text);
				string text3 = Editor.Text;
				SetEditorText(text2, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: false);
				Editor.SelectionStart = selectionStart;
				SearchAndFilterItemsByText(text, performAutoComplete: true);
				if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
				{
					if (AutoCompleteResolved && attemptAutoComplete)
					{
						if (StartsWithItems.Count > 0)
						{
							if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore || !flag)
							{
								NavigateAutoCompleteOptions(increment: true, usePrevText: false, flag, ignoreFocusedItem: true);
								if (flag && Editor.SelectionLength == 0)
								{
									SetEditorText(Editor.Text + MultiSelectValueDelimiter, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
								}
							}
							else if (flag)
							{
								CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: false, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: false);
								SetEditorText(Editor.Text + MultiSelectValueDelimiter, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
							}
						}
					}
					else if (AllowMultipleSelection && array.Length > 1)
					{
						string text4 = text3;
						if (!text3.Contains(text))
						{
							text4 = ((text3.Length <= 0) ? text : (text3 + MultiSelectValueDelimiter + text));
						}
						SetEditorText(text4, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
						if (flag)
						{
							CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: false, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
							SetEditorText(Editor.Text + MultiSelectValueDelimiter, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						SetEditorText(text, silently: true, CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore);
					}
				}
				else if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore)
				{
					if (text.Length > 0)
					{
						string text5 = text.Remove(text.Length - 1, 1);
						while (text5.Length > 0)
						{
							SearchAndFilterItemsByText(text5, performAutoComplete: true);
							if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
							{
								string text6 = "";
								for (int i = 0; i < array.Length - 1; i++)
								{
									text6 += array[i];
									if (AllowMultipleSelection)
									{
										text6 += MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
									}
								}
								text6 += text5;
								SetEditorText(text6, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
								if (AutoCompleteResolved && StartsWithItems.Count > 0)
								{
									NavigateAutoCompleteOptions(increment: true, usePrevText: false, flag, ignoreFocusedItem: true);
								}
								break;
							}
							text5 = text5.Remove(text5.Length - 1, 1);
						}
						if (text5.Length == 0)
						{
							string text7 = "";
							for (int j = 0; j < array.Length - 1; j++)
							{
								text7 += array[j];
								if (AllowMultipleSelection)
								{
									text7 += MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
								}
							}
							SetEditorText(text7, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
							CurrentSearchText = text5;
							ClearFilters();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Add && _focusItem != null)
					{
						SetFocusedItem(null, scrollToTop: false);
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: false, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: false);
						SetEditorText(Editor.Text + MultiSelectValueDelimiter, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
					}
				}
				_supressEditorTextUpdates = false;
			}
			_previousText = Editor.Text.Substring(0, Editor.SelectionStart);
			_hasJustOpened = !IsDropDownOpen && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Editor.Text);
			if (OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved && allowDropDown && _hasJustOpened)
			{
				DropDownOpenedViaTyping = true;
				IsDropDownOpen = true;
				Editor.Focus();
			}
			_isTextCommitted = true;
		}

		private void SearchAndFilterItemsByText(string text, bool performAutoComplete)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager == null || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPathResolved) && IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData != true))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ClearFilters();
				StartsWithItems.Clear();
				return;
			}
			ComboItemFilter comboItemFilter = null;
			bool flag = false;
			DataField[] source = GetDataManagerDataFields().ToArray();
			comboItemFilter = new ComboItemFilter
			{
				ObjectType = dataManager.CachedType,
				FieldName = DisplayMemberPathResolved,
				Field = source.FirstOrDefault((DataField f) => f.Name == DisplayMemberPathResolved)
			};
			comboItemFilter.ObjectTypedInfo = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo;
			comboItemFilter.Conditions.LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or;
			CachedTypedInfo cachedTypedInfo = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo;
			if (dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors != null && dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors.Count > 0)
			{
				CustomComparisonCondition startsWithCondition = GetStartsWithCondition(dataManager.CachedType, DisplayMemberPathResolved, text, cachedTypedInfo);
				comboItemFilter.Conditions.Add(startsWithCondition);
			}
			else
			{
				CustomComparisonCondition startsWithCondition2 = GetStartsWithCondition(dataManager.CachedType, DisplayMemberPathResolved, text);
				comboItemFilter.Conditions.Add(startsWithCondition2);
			}
			if (performAutoComplete)
			{
				RecordFilterCollection recordFilterCollection = new RecordFilterCollection
				{
					LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or
				};
				recordFilterCollection.Add(comboItemFilter);
				if ((AllowFilteringResolved && ItemFilters != null && ItemFilters.Count > 0) || (AllowFilteringResolved && (ItemFilters == null || ItemFilters.Count <= 0) && SupportsAlternateFilters))
				{
					_willFilterBeSetAgain = true;
				}
				dataManager.Filters = recordFilterCollection;
				flag = true;
				StartsWithItems.Clear();
				StartsWithItems.AddRange(Items);
				if (!AllowFilteringResolved && !AutoCompleteResolved && StartsWithItems.Count > 0)
				{
					SetFocusedItem(StartsWithItems[0], scrollToTop: true);
				}
			}
			if (!AllowFilteringResolved)
			{
				ClearFilters();
				return;
			}
			if (ItemFilters != null && ItemFilters.Count > 0)
			{
				RecordFilterCollection recordFilterCollection2 = new RecordFilterCollection
				{
					LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or
				};
				foreach (ComboItemFilter itemsFilter in ItemFilters)
				{
					if (itemsFilter == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					itemsFilter.ObjectType = dataManager.CachedType;
					itemsFilter.ObjectTypedInfo = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(itemsFilter.FieldName))
					{
						itemsFilter.FieldName = DisplayMemberPathResolved;
					}
					itemsFilter.Field = source.FirstOrDefault((DataField f) => f.Name == itemsFilter.FieldName);
					if (itemsFilter.Field == null && !CheckMemberPath(itemsFilter.FieldName) && DataManager != null)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("IvalidItemsFilterFieldName"), itemsFilter.FieldName, DataManager.CachedType.Name));
					}
					bool flag2 = false;
					foreach (IFilterCondition condition in itemsFilter.Conditions)
					{
						object obj = text;
						Type dataType = null;
						if (itemsFilter.Field != null)
						{
							dataType = itemsFilter.Field.FieldType;
						}
						else if (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == true)
						{
							dataType = itemsFilter.ObjectType;
						}
						obj = ChangeType(text, dataType);
						if (obj == null)
						{
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
						((ComparisonCondition)condition).FilterValue = obj;
					}
					if (!flag2)
					{
						recordFilterCollection2.Add(itemsFilter);
					}
				}
				_willFilterBeSetAgain = false;
				dataManager.Filters = recordFilterCollection2;
			}
			else if (SupportsAlternateFilters)
			{
				_willFilterBeSetAgain = false;
				dataManager.Filters = GetAlternateFilters(text);
			}
			else if (!flag)
			{
				RecordFilterCollection recordFilterCollection3 = new RecordFilterCollection();
				recordFilterCollection3.Add(comboItemFilter);
				_willFilterBeSetAgain = false;
				dataManager.Filters = recordFilterCollection3;
			}
			if (_focusItem != null)
			{
				int num = DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(_focusItem.Data);
				if (num == -1 && DataManager.RecordCount > 0)
				{
					SetFocusedItem(Items[0], scrollToTop: true);
				}
				else
				{
					SetFocusedItem(Items[num], scrollToTop: true);
				}
			}
			else if (DataManager.RecordCount > 0)
			{
				SetFocusedItem(Items[0], scrollToTop: true);
			}
			else
			{
				SetFocusedItem(null, scrollToTop: true);
			}
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: true);
			}
			CurrentSearchText = text;
		}

		private void FillSelectedItemsBySelectedMemberPath()
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved))
			{
				return;
			}
			CheckSelectedMemberPath();
			Type type = DataManagerBase.ResolvePropertyTypeFromPropertyName(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved, dataManager.CachedTypedInfo);
			if (type == null || type != typeof(bool))
			{
				return;
			}
			Type type2 = null;
			PropertyInfo propertyInfo = null;
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
			{
				Type type3 = item.GetType();
				PropertyInfo propertyInfo2 = null;
				if (type3 == type2)
				{
					propertyInfo2 = propertyInfo;
				}
				else
				{
					type2 = type3;
					propertyInfo = (propertyInfo2 = type3.GetProperty(IsSelectedMemberPath));
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if ((!(propertyInfo2 == null)) ? ((bool)propertyInfo2.GetValue(item, null)) : ((bool)_dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[IsSelectedMemberPathResolved].GetValue(item)))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			SelectItems(list, clearRestOfSelection: true);
		}

		private void FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValue(object selectedValue, bool shouldClearRestOfSelection = true)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedValuePathResolved) && ItemsSource != null)
			{
				if (selectedValue == null)
				{
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, null);
					return;
				}
				List<object> itemsToSelect = FindItemsBySelectedValue(selectedValue).ToList();
				SelectItems(itemsToSelect, shouldClearRestOfSelection);
				VerifyProperSelectedValue();
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<object> FindItemsBySelectedValue(object selectedValue)
		{
			bool shouldContinue = false;
			foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
			{
				Type type = item.GetType();
				object currentItemValue = null;
				if (type == typeof(DataRowView))
				{
					currentItemValue = GetValueFromDataRow(SelectedValuePathResolved, item);
				}
				Type type2 = null;
				if (currentItemValue == null)
				{
					PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty(SelectedValuePathResolved);
					if (property == null && currentItemValue == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					type2 = property.PropertyType;
					currentItemValue = property.GetValue(item, null);
				}
				if (type2 != null && !selectedValue.GetType().Equals(type2))
				{
					object obj = CoreUtilities.ConvertDataValue(selectedValue, type2, null, string.Empty);
					if (obj == null)
					{
						if (!IsInDesignMode)
						{
							throw new FormatException(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidSelectedValue"));
						}
					}
					else if (currentItemValue.Equals(obj))
					{
						if (SelectedItems.Contains(item))
						{
							shouldContinue = true;
						}
						yield return item;
						if (!shouldContinue)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (currentItemValue.Equals(selectedValue))
				{
					if (SelectedItems.Contains(item))
					{
						shouldContinue = true;
					}
					yield return item;
					if (!shouldContinue)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void VerifyProperSelectedValue()
		{
			int num = ((SelectedValues != null) ? SelectedValues.Count() : 0);
			if ((SelectedValues == null && SelectedValue != null) || (num > 0 && !SelectedValues[num - 1].Equals(SelectedValue)))
			{
				ResetSelection();
			}
		}

		private void ResetSelection()
		{
			SelectedItems.Clear();
			DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValueProperty, null);
			DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, null);
		}

		private void FillSelectedItemsBySelectedValues(object[] selectedValues)
		{
			if (selectedValues == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedValuePathResolved) && ItemsSource != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<object> source = selectedValues.Where((object x) => x != null);
				if (!source.Any())
				{
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, null);
					return;
				}
				List<object> itemsToSelect = source.SelectMany((object x) => FindItemsBySelectedValue(x)).ToList();
				SelectItems(itemsToSelect, clearRestOfSelection: true);
				VerifyProperSelectedValue();
			}
			else if (selectedValues.Length != 0)
			{
				SelectedItems.Clear();
			}
		}

		private void SetDropDownButtonVisibiltyMode(DropDownButtonDisplayMode mode)
		{
			if (_toggleButton != null)
			{
				if ((mode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Focused || mode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.MouseOver || mode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Never) && IsEditableResolved)
				{
					_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else if (mode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Always)
				{
					_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
		}

		private CustomComparisonCondition GetStartsWithCondition(Type dataType, string fieldName, string filterText, CachedTypedInfo cachedTypedInfo)
		{
			Expression<Func<string, string, bool>> filterExpression = (string s, string f) => s != null && s.StartsWith(f, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			return CreateComplexPropertyCondition(dataType, fieldName, filterText, filterExpression, cachedTypedInfo);
		}

		private CustomComparisonCondition GetStartsWithCondition(Type dataType, string fieldName, string filterText)
		{
			return GetStartsWithCondition(dataType, fieldName, filterText, null);
		}

		internal virtual void ClearDataProperties()
		{
		}

		internal void ApplyItemSource(IEnumerable itemSource)
		{
			if (_dataManager != null)
			{
				UnhookDataManager();
			}
			else
			{
				EnsureDataManager();
			}
			if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				HorizontalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
			}
			if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
			{
				VerticalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
				VerticalScrollBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (SelectedItems != null && !_deferClearingSelectionOnItemsSourceChanged)
			{
				if (SelectedItems.Count == 0 && SelectedItem != null)
				{
					SelectItem(SelectedItem, clearRestOfSelection: true);
				}
				else if (AllowMultipleSelection && SelectedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					NotifySelectedItemsCollectionAfterItemsAdded(SelectedItems);
				}
			}
			SyncSelectionOnNewItemSourceApplied(itemSource);
			InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: true);
		}

		private void SyncSelectionOnNewItemSourceApplied(IEnumerable itemSource)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			_selectedItemsToSync = new ObservableCollection<object>();
			if (itemSource != null)
			{
				List<object> list = itemSource.Cast<object>().ToList();
				object[] array = null;
				if (SelectedItems != null && SelectedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					array = SelectedItems.ToArray();
				}
				else if (SelectedItem != null)
				{
					array = new object[1] { SelectedItem };
				}
				if (array != null)
				{
					object[] array2 = array;
					foreach (object item in array2)
					{
						flag = list.IndexOf(item) == -1;
						if (!flag && AllowMultipleSelection)
						{
							IEnumerable<object> source;
							if (SelectedItems == null)
							{
								IEnumerable<object> enumerable = list;
								source = enumerable;
							}
							else
							{
								source = SelectedItems.Except(list);
							}
							flag = source.Any();
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							_selectedItemsToSync.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_selectedItemsToSync != null && _selectedItemsToSync.Count > 0 && !flag)
			{
				if (!_selectedItemsToSync.SequenceEqual(SelectedItems))
				{
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemsProperty, _selectedItemsToSync);
				}
				else if (SelectedItem == null || SelectedIndex == -1)
				{
					SelectItems(_selectedItemsToSync, clearRestOfSelection: false);
				}
				return;
			}
			_deferClearingSelectionOnItemsSourceChanged = true;
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (_deferClearingSelectionOnItemsSourceChanged)
				{
					if (SelectedItem != null)
					{
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, null);
					}
					if (SelectedIndex != -1)
					{
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedIndexProperty, -1);
					}
					object defaultValueOfSelectedValueType = GetDefaultValueOfSelectedValueType();
					if (SelectedValue != null && !SelectedValue.Equals(defaultValueOfSelectedValueType))
					{
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValueProperty, defaultValueOfSelectedValueType);
					}
					if (SelectedValues != null)
					{
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, null);
					}
					if (SelectedItems != null && SelectedItems.Any())
					{
						SelectedItems.Clear();
					}
				}
			});
		}

		protected void EnsureDataManager()
		{
			if (ItemsSource != null && _dataManager == null)
			{
				SetupDataManager();
			}
		}

		private void SetupDataManager()
		{
			_dataManager = DataManagerBase.CreateDataManager(ItemsSource, null, unwrapCollectionView: false, preferEnumerableItemType: false, allowObjectItemType: false);
			if (_dataManager != null)
			{
				_equalsDataManager = DataManagerBase.CreateDataManager(ItemsSource, null, unwrapCollectionView: false, preferEnumerableItemType: false, allowObjectItemType: false);
				_equalsDataManager.AllowCollectionViewOverrides = false;
				_dataManager.AllowCollectionViewOverrides = false;
				_dataManager.CollectionChanged += DataManager_CollectionChanged;
				_dataManager.NewObjectGeneration += DataManager_NewObjectGeneration;
				_dataManager.DataUpdated += DataManager_DataUpdated;
				IEnumerable<DataField> dataManagerDataFields = GetDataManagerDataFields();
				string text = string.Empty;
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == false)
				{
					foreach (DataField item in dataManagerDataFields)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							text = item.Name;
						}
						if (item.FieldType == typeof(string) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text2 = item.Name;
						}
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					_firstFieldName = text;
				}
				else
				{
					_firstFieldName = text2;
				}
				InitializeData();
			}
			else
			{
				if (ItemsSource is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
				{
					notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
					notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged += ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
				}
				_firstFieldName = null;
			}
		}

		internal void UnhookDataManager()
		{
			OnDataManagerUnhooked();
			InvalidateItems();
			_cachedRows.Clear();
			if (_dataManager != null)
			{
				_dataManager.CollectionChanged -= DataManager_CollectionChanged;
				_dataManager.NewObjectGeneration -= DataManager_NewObjectGeneration;
				_dataManager.DataUpdated -= DataManager_DataUpdated;
				_dataManager.Filters = null;
				_dataManager = null;
				_dataFieldsCache = null;
			}
		}

		protected internal void InvalidateScrollPanel(bool resetScrollPosition)
		{
			InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition, resetItems: false);
		}

		internal virtual void InvalidateScrollPanel(bool resetScrollPosition, bool resetItems)
		{
			if (_panel == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (resetItems)
			{
				_panel.ResetItems();
				_panel.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			if (!IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				return;
			}
			_panel.InvalidateMeasure();
			if (resetScrollPosition)
			{
				if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					HorizontalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
				}
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					VerticalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
				}
			}
		}

		private string ResolveText(object dataItem)
		{
			string result = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPathResolved))
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding(DisplayMemberPathResolved);
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
				binding.Source = dataItem;
				_valueProxy.SetBinding(ComboEditorItemValueProxy.ValueProperty, binding);
				if (_valueProxy.Value != null)
				{
					result = _valueProxy.Value.ToString();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = dataItem.ToString();
			}
			return result;
		}

		private Type ResolveType(object dataItem)
		{
			Type result = typeof(string);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPathResolved))
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding(DisplayMemberPathResolved);
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
				binding.Source = dataItem;
				_valueProxy.SetBinding(ComboEditorItemValueProxy.ValueProperty, binding);
				if (_valueProxy.Value != null)
				{
					result = _valueProxy.Value.GetType();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetItemText(object dataItem, string text)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPathResolved))
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding(DisplayMemberPathResolved);
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
				binding.Source = dataItem;
				_valueProxy.SetBinding(ComboEditorItemValueProxy.ValueProperty, binding);
				_valueProxy.Value = text;
			}
		}

		private void ClearFilters()
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null && dataManager.Filters != null && dataManager.Filters.Count > 0)
			{
				dataManager.Filters.Clear();
				if (IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					_panel.InvalidateMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		private void CommitTextAsSelected(bool closeDropDown, bool cancelSelection, bool moveSelectionToTheEnd, bool allowUseOfFocusedItem)
		{
			if (cancelSelection)
			{
				SetEditorText(Editor.Text.Remove(Editor.SelectionStart, Editor.SelectionLength), silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: false);
			}
			else if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource && allowUseOfFocusedItem && CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow && !_isTextCommitted)
			{
				T focusItem = _focusItem;
				if (focusItem != null && !focusItem.IsSelected)
				{
					SelectItem(focusItem, clearRestOfSelection: false);
				}
			}
			else if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore)
			{
				string text = "";
				if (AllowMultipleSelection)
				{
					string[] array = Editor.Text.Split(MultiSelectValueDelimiter);
					text = array[array.Length - 1];
				}
				else
				{
					text = Editor.Text;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !AddOrAllowItem(text) && !_isTextCommitted && EqualsDataManager != null && EqualsDataManager.RecordCount > 0)
				{
					SelectItem(EqualsDataManager.GetRecord(0), clearRestOfSelection: false);
				}
			}
			if (closeDropDown)
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
			StartsWithItems.Clear();
			if (moveSelectionToTheEnd)
			{
				Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
			}
			ClearFilters();
			_isTextCommitted = true;
		}

		private void NavigateAutoCompleteOptions(bool increment, bool usePrevText, bool lookToCommit, bool ignoreFocusedItem)
		{
			if (!IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager == null || dataManager.RecordCount <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = -1;
			if (!ignoreFocusedItem && _focusItem != null)
			{
				num = StartsWithItems.IndexOf(_focusItem);
			}
			num = ((!increment) ? (num - 1) : (num + 1));
			if (num >= StartsWithItems.Count)
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			else if (num < 0)
			{
				num = StartsWithItems.Count - 1;
			}
			T val = null;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = (usePrevText ? _previousText : Editor.Text);
			string[] array = text2.Split(MultiSelectValueDelimiter);
			if (lookToCommit)
			{
				string text3 = array[array.Length - 1].ToLower();
				foreach (T startsWithItem in StartsWithItems)
				{
					string text4 = ResolveText(startsWithItem.Data);
					if (text4.ToLower() == text3)
					{
						text = text4;
						val = startsWithItem;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (val == null && StartsWithItems.Count > num && num != -1)
			{
				val = StartsWithItems[num];
				text = ResolveText(val.Data);
			}
			SetFocusedItem(val, scrollToTop: true);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && val != null)
			{
				array[array.Length - 1] = text;
				string text5 = "";
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text6 in array2)
				{
					text5 = text5 + text6 + MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
				}
				text5 = text5.Remove(text5.Length - 1, 1);
				SetEditorText(text5, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: false);
				Editor.SelectionStart = text2.Length;
				Editor.SelectionLength = Math.Max(0, Editor.Text.Length - text2.Length);
				if (lookToCommit && Editor.SelectionLength == 0)
				{
					SelectItem(val, clearRestOfSelection: false);
					Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
				}
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateItems()
		{
			Items.Clear();
			_duplicateObjectValidator.Clear();
			InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false);
		}

		private void SetEditorText(string text, bool silently, bool moveSelectionToEnd)
		{
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				if (silently)
				{
					Editor.TextChanged -= Editor_TextChanged;
				}
				if (Editor is SpecializedTextBox specializedTextBox && (specializedTextBox.AllowTextChanges || !_supressEditorTextUpdates))
				{
					Editor.Text = text;
				}
				if (silently)
				{
					Editor.TextChanged += Editor_TextChanged;
				}
				if (moveSelectionToEnd)
				{
					Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ValidateSelection(string text)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>(text.Split(MultiSelectValueDelimiter));
			List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			foreach (object selectedItem in SelectedItems)
			{
				string item = ResolveText(selectedItem);
				if (!list.Contains(item))
				{
					list2.Add(selectedItem);
				}
				else
				{
					list3.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (object item2 in list2)
				{
					SelectedItems.Remove(item2);
				}
			}
			list = new List<string>(Editor.Text.Split(MultiSelectValueDelimiter));
			string text2 = "";
			int count = list.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count - 1; i++)
			{
				string text3 = list[i];
				if (!list3.Contains(text3))
				{
					if (AddOrAllowItem(text3))
					{
						text2 = text2 + text3 + MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
						continue;
					}
					if (EqualsDataManager != null && EqualsDataManager.RecordCount > 0)
					{
						SelectItem(EqualsDataManager.GetRecord(0), clearRestOfSelection: false);
					}
					text2 = text2 + text3 + MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
				}
				else
				{
					text2 = text2 + text3 + MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
				}
			}
			text2 += list[list.Count - 1];
			SetEditorText(text2, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: false);
		}

		private void SetFocusedItem(T item, bool scrollToTop)
		{
			SetFocusedItem(item, scrollToTop, scroll: true);
		}

		private void SetFocusedItem(T item, bool scrollToTop, bool scroll)
		{
			if (_focusItem != null)
			{
				_focusItem.IsFocused = false;
			}
			if (item != null)
			{
				item.IsFocused = true;
			}
			_focusItem = item;
			if (scroll && IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				ScrollItemIntoView(_focusItem, scrollToTop);
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateSelectedContent()
		{
			if (IsEditableResolved)
			{
				if (SelectedItems == null)
				{
					SetEditorText("", silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: false);
					return;
				}
				string text = "";
				foreach (object selectedItem in SelectedItems)
				{
					text = text + ResolveText(selectedItem) + MultiSelectValueDelimiter;
				}
				if (text.Length > 0)
				{
					text = text.Remove(text.Length - 1, 1);
				}
				if (Editor != null)
				{
					SetEditorText(text, silently: true, SelectedItems.Count > 0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (_multiSelectPanel == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				_multiSelectPanel.Children.Clear();
				if (SelectedItems != null)
				{
					foreach (object selectedItem2 in SelectedItems)
					{
						if (ItemTemplate != null)
						{
							ContentControl contentControl = new ContentControl();
							contentControl.IsHitTestVisible = false;
							contentControl.IsTabStop = false;
							contentControl.Content = selectedItem2;
							contentControl.ContentTemplate = ItemTemplate;
							_multiSelectPanel.Children.Add(contentControl);
						}
						else
						{
							string text2 = DisplayMemberPathResolved;
							if (text2 == null)
							{
								text2 = "";
							}
							Binding binding = new Binding(text2);
							binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
							binding.Source = selectedItem2;
							TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock();
							textBlock.IsHitTestVisible = false;
							textBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, binding);
							_multiSelectPanel.Children.Add(textBlock);
						}
						_multiSelectPanel.Children.Add(new TextBlock
						{
							Text = MultiSelectValueDelimiter.ToString()
						});
					}
				}
				if (_multiSelectPanel.Children.Count > 0)
				{
					_multiSelectPanel.Children.RemoveAt(_multiSelectPanel.Children.Count - 1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CheckDisplayMemberPath()
		{
			if (!CheckMemberPath(DisplayMemberPathResolved) && DataManager != null && (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == false || string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayMemberPathResolved)))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidMember"), DisplayMemberPathResolved, DataManager.CachedType.Name));
			}
		}

		private void CheckSelectedMemberPath()
		{
			if (!CheckMemberPath(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved) && DataManager != null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidMember"), IsSelectedMemberPathResolved, DataManager.CachedType.Name));
			}
		}

		private bool CheckMemberPath(string memberPath)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(memberPath) && DataManager != null)
			{
				result = false;
				try
				{
					if (DataManagerBase.ResolvePropertyTypeFromPropertyName(memberPath, DataManager.CachedTypedInfo) != null)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool AddOrAllowItem(string itemText)
		{
			if (EqualsDataManager != null)
			{
				ComparisonOperator comparisonOperator = ComparisonOperator.Equals;
				IEnumerable<DataField> dataManagerDataFields = GetDataManagerDataFields();
				DataField field = null;
				foreach (DataField item2 in dataManagerDataFields)
				{
					if (item2.Name == DisplayMemberPathResolved)
					{
						field = item2;
						break;
					}
				}
				ComboItemFilter comboItemFilter = new ComboItemFilter
				{
					ObjectType = EqualsDataManager.CachedType,
					FieldName = DisplayMemberPathResolved,
					Field = field
				};
				comboItemFilter.ObjectTypedInfo = EqualsDataManager.CachedTypedInfo;
				object obj = null;
				obj = ((IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData != true) ? CoreUtilities.ConvertDataValue(itemText, comboItemFilter.FieldType, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, string.Empty) : CoreUtilities.ConvertDataValue(itemText, comboItemFilter.ObjectType, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, string.Empty));
				if (obj != null)
				{
					ComparisonCondition item = new ComparisonCondition
					{
						Operator = comparisonOperator,
						FilterValue = obj,
						CaseSensitive = true
					};
					comboItemFilter.Conditions.Add(item);
					RecordFilterCollection recordFilterCollection = new RecordFilterCollection();
					recordFilterCollection.Add(comboItemFilter);
					EqualsDataManager.Filters = recordFilterCollection;
					if (EqualsDataManager.RecordCount == 0)
					{
						if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Add)
						{
							if (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == true)
							{
								_newPrimitiveObjectToCreate = ChangeType(itemText, DataManager.CachedType);
							}
							T val = CreateItem();
							if (val != null)
							{
								SetItemText(val.Data, itemText);
								if (!OnItemAdding(val))
								{
									AddItem(val);
									SelectItem(val.Data, clearRestOfSelection: false);
									OnItemAdded(val);
									return true;
								}
							}
						}
						else if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
						{
							if (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == true)
							{
								_newPrimitiveObjectToCreate = ChangeType(itemText, DataManager.CachedType);
							}
							T val2 = CreateItem();
							if (val2 != null)
							{
								SetItemText(val2.Data, itemText);
								SelectItem(val2, clearRestOfSelection: false);
								return true;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnComboLostFocus()
		{
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && IsDropDownOpen && base.Focusable)
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnWindowSizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnWindowStateChanged(sender, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		private void OnWindowStateChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void EnableCloseUpTriggerTrackers()
		{
			if (_comboLostFocusTracker == null)
			{
				_comboLostFocusTracker = new LostFocusTracker(this, OnComboLostFocus);
			}
			if (!_windowEventsHooked)
			{
				_window = Window.GetWindow(this);
				if (_window != null)
				{
					_window.LocationChanged += OnWindowStateChanged;
					_window.Deactivated += OnWindowStateChanged;
					_window.SizeChanged += OnWindowSizeChanged;
					_window.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(RootVisual_MouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
					_windowEventsHooked = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void DisableCloseUpTriggerTrackers()
		{
			if (_comboLostFocusTracker != null)
			{
				_comboLostFocusTracker.Deactivate(forceLostFocusBindingUpdates: true);
				_comboLostFocusTracker = null;
			}
			if (_windowEventsHooked && _window != null && _window != null)
			{
				_window.LocationChanged -= OnWindowStateChanged;
				_window.Deactivated -= OnWindowStateChanged;
				_window.SizeChanged -= OnWindowSizeChanged;
				_window.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(RootVisual_MouseLeftButtonDown));
				_windowEventsHooked = false;
			}
		}

		private void EnsurePopupsAllowsTransparencyInSync()
		{
			if (_dropDownPopup != null && !_dropDownPopup.AllowsTransparency && DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(_dropDownPopup, Popup.AllowsTransparencyProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Default && _window != null && _window.AllowsTransparency && !NativeWindowMethods.IsWindows8orAbove)
			{
				_dropDownPopup.SetCurrentValue(Popup.AllowsTransparencyProperty, true);
			}
		}

		private FrameworkElement GetUltimateVisualParent()
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = this;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = null;
			while (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) as FrameworkElement;
				if (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					dependencyObject2 = dependencyObject;
				}
			}
			return dependencyObject2 as FrameworkElement;
		}

		private void SetFocusToControlOrEditor()
		{
			if (Editor == null || !IsEditableResolved)
			{
				Focus();
			}
			else
			{
				Editor.Focus();
			}
		}

		private void EnsureCaretPositionOnLostFocus()
		{
			_mouseButtonDownAfterGotFocus = false;
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				Editor.ScrollToHome();
				Editor.Select(0, 0);
			}
		}

		private void EnsureCaretPositionOnGotFocus()
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (Editor != null && !_mouseButtonDownAfterGotFocus)
				{
					Editor.CaretIndex = Editor.Text.Length;
					if (Editor is SpecializedTextBox specializedTextBox)
					{
						specializedTextBox.ScrollToRightEnd();
					}
				}
				_mouseButtonDownAfterGotFocus = false;
			});
		}

		internal void AttachDetachPropertyChanged(bool attach)
		{
			if (attach)
			{
				if (ItemsSource == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				{
					foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
					{
						if (item is INotifyPropertyChanged propertyChangedDataObject)
						{
							AttachDetachPropertyChanged(propertyChangedDataObject, attach: true);
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			DetachPropertyChanged();
		}

		internal void AttachDetachPropertyChanged(INotifyPropertyChanged propertyChangedDataObject, bool attach)
		{
			if (propertyChangedDataObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (attach)
			{
				WeakPropertyChangedHandler<ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>> weakPropertyChangedHandler = new WeakPropertyChangedHandler<ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>>(this, propertyChangedDataObject, delegate(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> instance, object s, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
				{
					instance.Item_PropertyChanged(s, e);
				});
				propertyChangedDataObject.PropertyChanged += weakPropertyChangedHandler.OnEvent;
				if (!_weakPropertyChangedHandlers.ContainsKey(propertyChangedDataObject))
				{
					_weakPropertyChangedHandlers.Add(propertyChangedDataObject, weakPropertyChangedHandler);
				}
			}
			else if (!attach)
			{
				WeakPropertyChangedHandler<ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>> value = null;
				if (_weakPropertyChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(propertyChangedDataObject, out value) && value != null)
				{
					value.Detach();
					_weakPropertyChangedHandlers.Remove(propertyChangedDataObject);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void DetachPropertyChanged()
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<INotifyPropertyChanged, WeakPropertyChangedHandler<ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>>> item in (IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<INotifyPropertyChanged, WeakPropertyChangedHandler<ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>>>>)_weakPropertyChangedHandlers)
			{
				item.Value?.Detach();
			}
			_weakPropertyChangedHandlers.Clear();
		}

		private void DropDownSizePropertyChanged(double newValue, string propertyName)
		{
			if (newValue < 0.0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidSize"), newValue, propertyName));
			}
			_invalidateHeight = true;
			_heightRestrictionsChanged = true;
			if (_rootPopupParent != null)
			{
				_rootPopupParent.Measure(new Size(1.0, 1.0));
			}
			OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}

		private object GetDefaultValueOfSelectedValueType()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedValuePathResolved) && CheckMemberPath(SelectedValuePathResolved) && _dataManager != null)
			{
				return DataManagerBase.GetDefaultValue(DataManagerBase.ResolvePropertyTypeFromPropertyName(SelectedValuePathResolved, _dataManager.CachedTypedInfo));
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected abstract T GenerateNewObject(object data);

		protected internal virtual T CreateItem(object data, DataManagerBase dataManager)
		{
			T val = null;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				if (!_dataManager.CachedType.IsAssignableFrom(data.GetType()))
				{
					throw new Exception(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, SRCombo.GetString("InvalidDataTypeException"), new object[2]
					{
						data.GetType(),
						dataManager.CachedType
					}));
				}
				return GenerateNewObject(data);
			}
			throw new Exception(SRCombo.GetString("AddWithOutItemSourceException"));
		}

		protected virtual T CreateItem()
		{
			T result = null;
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				return CreateItem(dataManager.GenerateNewObject(), dataManager);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual T CreateItem(object dataItem)
		{
			return CreateItem(dataItem, DataManager);
		}

		protected virtual T GetDataItem(int index)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				T val = null;
				object record = dataManager.GetRecord(index);
				if (record != null)
				{
					if (_cachedRows.ContainsKey(record))
					{
						if (!_duplicateObjectValidator.ContainsKey(record))
						{
							_duplicateObjectValidator.Add(record, index);
							return _cachedRows[record];
						}
						return GenerateNewObject(record);
					}
					val = GenerateNewObject(record);
					_cachedRows.Add(record, val);
					_duplicateObjectValidator.Add(record, index);
				}
				else
				{
					val = GenerateNewObject(record);
				}
				return val;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual void AddItem(T addedObject)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				dataManager.AddRecord(addedObject.Data);
				if (!_cachedRows.ContainsKey(addedObject.Data))
				{
					_cachedRows.Add(addedObject.Data, addedObject);
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool RemoveItem(T removedObject)
		{
			return RemoveItem(removedObject, DataManager);
		}

		protected virtual bool RemoveItem(T removedObject, DataManagerBase manager)
		{
			if (removedObject != null && manager != null)
			{
				manager.RemoveRecord(removedObject.Data);
				_cachedRows.Remove(removedObject.Data);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void RemoveRange(IList<T> itemsToRemove)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (itemsToRemove == null || itemsToRemove.Count == 0 || dataManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (T item in itemsToRemove)
			{
				RemoveItem(item, dataManager);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InsertItem(int index, T insertedObject)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				dataManager.InsertRecord(index, insertedObject.Data);
				if (!_cachedRows.ContainsKey(insertedObject.Data))
				{
					_cachedRows.Add(insertedObject.Data, insertedObject);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal static object ChangeType(object value, Type dataType)
		{
			if (dataType.IsGenericType && dataType.GetGenericTypeDefinition().Equals(typeof(Nullable<>)))
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				dataType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(dataType);
			}
			if (dataType.IsEnum && value != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return Enum.Parse(dataType, value.ToString(), ignoreCase: true);
				}
				catch (OverflowException)
				{
					return null;
				}
				catch (ArgumentException)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			try
			{
				return Convert.ChangeType(value, dataType, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			catch (OverflowException)
			{
				return null;
			}
			catch (InvalidCastException)
			{
				return null;
			}
			catch (FormatException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnEditorKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected internal IEnumerable<DataField> GetDataManagerDataFields()
		{
			if (_dataManager == null)
			{
				return new List<DataField>();
			}
			if (_dataFieldsCache != null)
			{
				return _dataFieldsCache;
			}
			IEnumerable<DataField> dataProperties = _dataManager.GetDataProperties();
			if (_dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.CachedType == typeof(DataRowView))
			{
				return dataProperties;
			}
			PropertyInfo[] properties = _dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.CachedType.GetProperties();
			List<DataField> list = new List<DataField>();
			foreach (DataField item in dataProperties)
			{
				PropertyInfo[] array = properties;
				foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in array)
				{
					if (propertyInfo.Name == item.Name && propertyInfo.GetIndexParameters().Length == 0)
					{
						list.Add(item);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			_dataFieldsCache = list;
			return list;
		}

		[Obsolete("The overload that takes an Expression<Func<string, string, bool>> should be used instead. The new 2nd string parameter is the filterText so a closure isn't needed.", false)]
		protected CustomComparisonCondition CreateComplexPropertyCondition(Type dataType, string fieldName, string filterText, Expression<Func<string, bool>> filterExpression, CachedTypedInfo cachedTypedInfo)
		{
			return CreateComplexPropertyCondition(dataType, fieldName, filterText, filterExpression, cachedTypedInfo, provideFilterTextToFilterExpression: false);
		}

		protected CustomComparisonCondition CreateComplexPropertyCondition(Type dataType, string fieldName, string filterText, Expression<Func<string, string, bool>> filterExpression, CachedTypedInfo cachedTypedInfo)
		{
			return CreateComplexPropertyCondition(dataType, fieldName, filterText, filterExpression, cachedTypedInfo, provideFilterTextToFilterExpression: true);
		}

		private CustomComparisonCondition CreateComplexPropertyCondition(Type dataType, string fieldName, string filterText, System.Linq.Expressions.Expression filterExpression, CachedTypedInfo cachedTypedInfo, bool provideFilterTextToFilterExpression)
		{
			ParameterExpression parameterExpression = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Parameter(dataType);
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression;
			if (typeof(DataRowView).IsAssignableFrom(dataType) || typeof(DataRow).IsAssignableFrom(dataType))
			{
				expression = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Call(typeof(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>).GetMethod("GetValueFromDataRow", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic), System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(fieldName), parameterExpression);
			}
			else
			{
				Type type = null;
				object defaultValue = null;
				if (cachedTypedInfo != null && cachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors != null && cachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors.Count > 0)
				{
					type = cachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[fieldName].PropertyType;
					defaultValue = DataManagerBase.GetDefaultValue(type);
				}
				expression = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fieldName)) ? DataManagerBase.BuildPropertyExpressionFromPropertyName(fieldName, parameterExpression, cachedTypedInfo, type, defaultValue) : parameterExpression);
			}
			MethodInfo method = expression.Type.GetMethod("ToString", new Type[0]);
			if (method == null)
			{
				method = typeof(object).GetMethod("ToString", Type.EmptyTypes);
			}
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression2 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Call(expression, method);
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression3 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(filterText);
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression[] arguments = ((!provideFilterTextToFilterExpression) ? new System.Linq.Expressions.Expression[1] { expression2 } : new System.Linq.Expressions.Expression[2] { expression2, expression3 });
			expression2 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Invoke(filterExpression, arguments);
			if (!expression.Type.IsValueType)
			{
				expression2 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Condition(System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Equal(expression, System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(null, expression.Type)), System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(false), expression2);
			}
			if (!dataType.IsValueType)
			{
				expression2 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Condition(System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Equal(parameterExpression, System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(null, dataType)), System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Constant(false), expression2);
			}
			System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression4 = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Lambda(expression2, parameterExpression);
			return new CustomComparisonCondition
			{
				Expression = expression4
			};
		}

		internal void SelectItem(int index, bool clearRestOfSelection)
		{
			int eventFiredCount = 0;
			SelectItem(index, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal void SelectItem(int index, bool clearRestOfSelection, ref int eventFiredCount)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				if (index == -1)
				{
					SelectItem(null, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
				}
				else
				{
					SelectItem(dataManager.GetRecord(index), clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SelectItem(T item, bool clearRestOfSelection)
		{
			int eventFiredCount = 0;
			SelectItem(item, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal void SelectItem(T item, bool clearRestOfSelection, ref int eventFiredCount)
		{
			SelectItem(item?.Data, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal void SelectItem(object data, bool clearRestOfSelection)
		{
			int eventFiredCount = 0;
			SelectItem(data, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal void SelectItem(object data, bool clearRestOfSelection, ref int eventFiredCount)
		{
			SelectItems(new List<object> { data }, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal IList<object> SelectItems(IList<object> itemsToSelect, bool clearRestOfSelection)
		{
			int eventFiredCount = 0;
			return SelectItems(itemsToSelect, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
		}

		internal IList<object> SelectItems(IList<object> itemsToSelect, bool clearRestOfSelection, ref int eventFiredCount)
		{
			if (SelectedItems == null)
			{
				return new List<object>();
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (SelectedItems.Count < 1 && itemsToSelect != null && itemsToSelect.Count > 0)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (itemsToSelect == null || itemsToSelect.Count == 0 || !itemsToSelect.Any((object obj2) => obj2 != null))
			{
				if (clearRestOfSelection)
				{
					SelectedItems.Clear();
				}
				return new List<object>();
			}
			if (itemsToSelect.Count > 1 && !AllowMultipleSelection)
			{
				object obj = itemsToSelect.Last();
				SelectItem(obj, clearRestOfSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
				foreach (object item2 in itemsToSelect.Take(itemsToSelect.Count - 1))
				{
					UpdateItemSelectedMemberPathValue(item2, isSelectedValue: false);
				}
				return new List<object> { obj };
			}
			IList<object> previouslySelected = _backupSelectedItems.ToList();
			if (DataManager != null)
			{
				bool supressSelectionChangedEvent;
				if ((clearRestOfSelection || !AllowMultipleSelection) && SelectedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					supressSelectionChangedEvent = _supressSelectionChangedEvent;
					_supressSelectionChangedEvent = true;
					SelectedItems.Clear();
					_supressSelectionChangedEvent = supressSelectionChangedEvent;
				}
				supressSelectionChangedEvent = _supressSelectionChangedEvent;
				_supressSelectionChangedEvent = true;
				_updatingSelectedItems = true;
				foreach (object item3 in itemsToSelect)
				{
					if (!SelectedItems.Contains(item3))
					{
						SelectedItems.Add(item3);
					}
				}
				_updatingSelectedItems = false;
				NotifySelectedItemsCollectionAfterItemsAdded((IList)itemsToSelect);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedValuePathResolved))
				{
					_ignoreSelectionChanging = true;
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValueProperty, DataManagerBase.ResolveValueFromPropertyPath(SelectedValuePathResolved, itemsToSelect.Last()));
					List<object> list = new List<object>();
					foreach (object selectedItem in SelectedItems)
					{
						object item = DataManagerBase.ResolveValueFromPropertyPath(SelectedValuePathResolved, selectedItem);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, list.ToArray());
					_ignoreSelectionChanging = false;
				}
				bool ignoreSelectionChanging = _ignoreSelectionChanging;
				_ignoreSelectionChanging = true;
				DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedIndexProperty, DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(itemsToSelect.Last()));
				DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, itemsToSelect.Last());
				_ignoreSelectionChanging = ignoreSelectionChanging;
				_supressSelectionChangedEvent = supressSelectionChangedEvent;
				if (flag)
				{
					SyncFocusedItem();
				}
				if (!_supressSelectionChangedEvent)
				{
					OnSelectionChanged(previouslySelected, itemsToSelect);
					eventFiredCount++;
				}
			}
			return itemsToSelect;
		}

		internal void UnselectItem(int index)
		{
			UnselectItem(DataManager.GetRecord(index));
		}

		internal void UnselectItem(T item)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				UnselectItem(item.Data);
				UnselectValue(item.Data);
			}
			else
			{
				UnselectItem(null);
			}
		}

		internal void UnselectItem(object data)
		{
			SelectedItems.Remove(data);
		}

		internal void UnselectValue(object data)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedValuePathResolved))
			{
				object item = DataManagerBase.ResolveValueFromPropertyPath(SelectedValuePathResolved, data);
				if (SelectedValues != null)
				{
					List<object> list = SelectedValues.ToList();
					list.Remove(item);
					_ignoreSelectionChanging = true;
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, list.ToArray());
					_ignoreSelectionChanging = false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal virtual void OnComboEditorItemClicked(T comboEditorItem)
		{
			if (!comboEditorItem.IsEnabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			int eventFiredCount = 0;
			List<object> list = new List<object>(SelectedItems);
			bool flag = (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control || CheckBoxVisibility == Visibility.Visible;
			if (comboEditorItem.IsSelected)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					UnselectItem(comboEditorItem);
				}
				else
				{
					bool supressSelectionChangedEvent = _supressSelectionChangedEvent;
					_supressSelectionChangedEvent = true;
					UnselectItem(comboEditorItem);
					SelectItem(comboEditorItem, clearRestOfSelection: true, ref eventFiredCount);
					_supressSelectionChangedEvent = supressSelectionChangedEvent;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				SelectItem(comboEditorItem, !flag || !AllowMultipleSelection, ref eventFiredCount);
			}
			if (!flag || !AllowMultipleSelection)
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
			else
			{
				_skipDropDownClosingOnMouseClick = true;
			}
			SyncFocusedItem(comboEditorItem);
			if (SelectedItems.Count != list.Count && eventFiredCount == 0)
			{
				List<object> newlySelected = new List<object>(SelectedItems);
				OnSelectionChanged(list, newlySelected);
			}
		}

			return null;
		}

		{
		}

		internal void ClearCurrentSelection()
		{
			SelectedItems.Clear();
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false, resetItems: false);
			}
		}

		internal void OnIsFocusWithinChangedHelper(bool gotFocus)
		{
			if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && (_comboLostFocusTracker == null || _comboLostFocusTracker.ElementWithFocus != Editor))
			{
				SetFocusToControlOrEditor();
			}
		}

		internal void InvalidateDropDownPosition(bool adjustHeight)
		{
			if (!_isLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = DropDownOpenedViaTyping && adjustHeight;
			if (_dropDownPopup != null && IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				bool flag2 = _rootPopupElement.DesiredSize.Height == 0.0;
				Tuple<double, double> tuple = CalcAvailableSpace();
				double item = tuple.Item1;
				double item2 = tuple.Item2;
				_aboveHeight = item;
				_belowHeight = item2;
				item2 += 10.0;
				_isAboveState = item > item2;
				double num = CalculateMaxPopupHeight(item > item2, item, _belowHeight);
				double num2 = num;
				MaxPanelHeight = 0.0;
				double num3 = 1.0;
				num3 = ((!double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Width)) ? (double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth) ? _rootPopupElement.Width : Math.Max(_rootPopupElement.Width, MinDropDownWidth)) : ((double.IsInfinity(MinDropDownWidth) || double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth)) ? 1.0 : MinDropDownWidth));
				bool flag3 = false;
				if (_rootPopupParent != null && (double.IsNaN(_lastSetHeight) || double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Height) || _rootPopupElement.Height == _lastSetHeight || flag || _invalidateHeight))
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					bool flag4 = true;
					if (adjustHeight || flag2 || flag || IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						MaxPanelHeight = num;
						double height = _rootPopupElement.Height;
						if (_heightRestrictionsChanged)
						{
							_rootPopupElement.Height = double.NaN;
							_heightRestrictionsChanged = false;
						}
						if (HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility.Equals(Visibility.Visible))
						{
							num -= HorizontalScrollBar.DesiredSize.Height;
						}
						if (_resizer != null)
						{
							num -= _resizer.DesiredSize.Height;
						}
						_rootPopupElement.Measure(new Size(num3, num));
						bool flag5 = !double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Height) && height != _lastSetHeight;
						bool flag6 = DataManager != null && Items.Count > 0 && flag;
						if ((!_invalidateHeight && (flag5 || (!flag6 && flag))) || (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore && _rootPopupElement.DesiredSize.Height < num && !flag6))
						{
							flag4 = false;
						}
						else
						{
							if (_invalidateHeight)
							{
								_invalidateHeight = false;
							}
							if (double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Height) || _rootPopupElement.Height < num)
							{
								Panel.InvalidateMeasure();
								Panel.Measure(new Size(num3, num2));
								double num4 = Math.Max(Panel.DesiredSize.Height, Panel.ActualHeight);
								num4 = (Panel.IsMeasureValid ? num4 : num2);
								num = Panel.UpdateHeightWithParentSizeProperties(num4);
								double num5 = num2 - HorizontalScrollBar.DesiredSize.Height - GetResizerHeight();
								if (num > num5)
								{
									num = num5;
								}
								num = Math.Min(num, num2);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num = DropDownHeight - GetResizerHeight();
					}
					if (flag4)
					{
						UpdateHeight(num);
					}
					UpdatePositionStates();
				}
				else
				{
					if (!double.IsNaN(_rootPopupElement.Height) && !IsPopupResizing)
					{
						UpdateHeight(_lastSetHeight);
					}
					UpdatePositionStates();
				}
			}
			if (_dropDownPopup == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (SystemParameters.IsMenuDropRightAligned)
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth))
				{
					_dropDownPopup.HorizontalOffset = Math.Max(_rootPopupElement.Width, MinDropDownWidth);
				}
				else
				{
					_dropDownPopup.HorizontalOffset = _rootPopupElement.Width;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_dropDownPopup.HorizontalOffset = 0.0;
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateHeight(double rootPopupHeight, bool shouldCheckHorizontalWidth = false)
		{
			if (IsPopupJustResized && !DropDownOpenedViaTyping)
			{
				if (_rootPopupElement.Height > MaxDropDownHeight)
				{
					_rootPopupElement.Height = MaxDropDownHeight;
				}
				DropDownHeight = _rootPopupElement.Height + GetResizerHeight();
				if (!_hasElementBeenResized)
				{
					_lastSetHeight = Panel.UpdateHeightWithParentSizeProperties(Panel.DesiredSize.Height);
				}
				return;
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(_rootPopupParent.Height))
			{
				_rootPopupParent.Height = double.NaN;
			}
			if (IsPopupResizing)
			{
				if (_rootPopupElement.Height > MaxDropDownHeight)
				{
					_rootPopupElement.Height = MaxDropDownHeight;
				}
				DropDownHeight = _rootPopupElement.Height + GetResizerHeight();
				if (_rootPopupElement.Height > MaxDropDownHeight)
				{
					UpdatePositionStates();
				}
				return;
			}
			bool flag = HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible;
			if ((Panel.FixedRowsTop == null || Panel.FixedRowsTop.Count <= 0) ? (flag & (Items.Count > 0)) : (flag & (Panel.FixedRowsTop[0].Control != null && Panel.FixedRowsTop[0].Cells.Count >= Panel.FixedRowsTop[0].Control.VisibleCells.Count)))
			{
				HorizontalScrollBar.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
				rootPopupHeight += HorizontalScrollBar.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			rootPopupHeight = Math.Min(Math.Max(rootPopupHeight, _rootPopupElement.MinHeight), Math.Max(_rootPopupElement.MaxHeight, _rootPopupElement.MinHeight));
			rootPopupHeight += 1.0;
			if (!_hasElementBeenResized)
			{
				if (_rootPopupElement.Height != rootPopupHeight)
				{
					_rootPopupElement.Height = rootPopupHeight;
				}
				_lastSetHeight = rootPopupHeight;
				DropDownHeight = rootPopupHeight + GetResizerHeight();
				if (IsControlOnNonPrimaryMonitor() && _isAboveState)
				{
					DropDownHeight -= GetRootBorderHeight();
				}
			}
			if (_rootPopupParent.DesiredSize.Height > DropDownHeight)
			{
				_rootPopupParent.Height = DropDownHeight;
			}
		}

		internal void UpdatePositionStates()
		{
			if (IsPopupJustResized && !double.IsInfinity(MaxDropDownHeight) && !double.IsNaN(MaxDropDownHeight) && DropDownHeight < MaxDropDownHeight)
			{
				return;
			}
			UpdatePosition();
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			num2 = base.ActualHeight;
			if (_isAboveState)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Above", useTransitions: false);
				double num3 = 0.0 - (DropDownHeight + num);
				if (_dropDownPopup.VerticalOffset != num3 && !double.IsNaN(num3))
				{
					_dropDownPopup.VerticalOffset = num3;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Below", useTransitions: false);
				_dropDownPopup.VerticalOffset = num2;
			}
		}

		internal virtual double GetAdditionalElementsHeight()
		{
			return 0.0;
		}

		internal FrameworkElement GetRootPopupElement()
		{
			return _rootPopupElement;
		}

		public void ScrollItemIntoView(T item, bool toTop)
		{
			if (item != null && _panel != null)
			{
				_panel.ScrollItemIntoView(item, toTop);
			}
		}

		public void ScrollItemIntoView(object dataItem, bool toTop)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				int num = dataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(dataItem);
				if (num != -1)
				{
					ScrollItemIntoView(Items[num], toTop);
				}
			}
		}

		private static object GetValueFromDataRow(string fieldName, object dataItem)
		{
			if (dataItem is DataRowView)
			{
				return ((DataRowView)dataItem)[fieldName];
			}
			if (dataItem is DataRow)
			{
				return ((DataRow)dataItem)[fieldName];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static void RegisterResources(string name, Assembly assembly)
		{
			SR.AddResource(name, assembly);
		}

		public static void UnregisterResources(string name)
		{
			SR.RemoveResource(name);
		}

		protected virtual bool OnDropDownClosing()
		{
			if (this.DropDownClosing != null)
			{
				CancelEventArgs e = new CancelEventArgs();
				this.DropDownClosing(this, e);
				return e.Cancel;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDropDownClosed()
		{
			DropDownOpenedViaTyping = false;
			IsPopupJustResized = false;
			_invalidateHeight = true;
			if (this.DropDownClosed != null)
			{
				this.DropDownClosed(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnItemAdding(T item)
		{
			if (this.ItemAdding != null)
			{
				ComboItemAddingEventArgs<T> e = new ComboItemAddingEventArgs<T>(item);
				this.ItemAdding(this, e);
				return e.Cancel;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemAdded(T item)
		{
			if (this.ItemAdded != null)
			{
				this.ItemAdded(this, new ComboItemAddedEventArgs<T>(item));
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnDropDownOpening()
		{
			if (this.DropDownOpening != null)
			{
				CancelEventArgs e = new CancelEventArgs();
				this.DropDownOpening(this, e);
				return e.Cancel;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDropDownOpened()
		{
			if (this.DropDownOpened != null)
			{
				this.DropDownOpened(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged(IList<object> previouslySelected, IList<object> newlySelected)
		{
			IList<object> list = null;
			IList<object> list2 = null;
			if (previouslySelected != null && newlySelected != null)
			{
				list = newlySelected.Except(previouslySelected).ToList();
				list2 = previouslySelected.Except(newlySelected).ToList();
			}
			else if (previouslySelected != null && newlySelected == null)
			{
				list2 = previouslySelected;
				list = new List<object>();
			}
			else if (previouslySelected == null && newlySelected != null)
			{
				list2 = new List<object>();
				list = newlySelected;
			}
			if ((list == null || list.Count == 0) && (list2 == null || list2.Count == 0))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!list2.Except(SelectedItems).Any())
			{
				list2 = new List<object>();
			}
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					Editor.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(999999999.0);
				}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
			}
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, new SelectionChangedEventArgs((IList)list, (IList)list2));
			}
			if (Panel != null)
			{
				Panel.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataObjectRequested(HandleableObjectGenerationEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsBoundToPrimitiveTypeData == true)
			{
				e.NewObject = _newPrimitiveObjectToCreate;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (this.DataObjectRequested != null)
			{
				this.DataObjectRequested(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void DataManager_NewObjectGeneration(object sender, HandleableObjectGenerationEventArgs e)
		{
			OnDataObjectRequested(e);
		}

		private bool IsControlOnNonPrimaryMonitor()
		{
			_ = _currentWorkAreaRect;
			return _currentWorkAreaRect.Y > 0.0;
		}

		private void DataManager_DataUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateItems();
			if (_dropDownPopup != null)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.Height = double.NaN;
			}
			if (Items.Count > 0 && !_willFilterBeSetAgain)
			{
				InvalidateDropDownPosition(adjustHeight: true);
			}
		}

		private void DataManager_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				DetachPropertyChanged();
			}
			if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
			{
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				foreach (object selectedItem in SelectedItems)
				{
					bool ignoreSortedFilterDataSource = false;
					if (TextEditor != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(TextEditor.Text) && TextEditor.Text.Last() == MultiSelectValueDelimiter)
					{
						ignoreSortedFilterDataSource = true;
					}
					if (DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(selectedItem, ignoreSortedFilterDataSource) == -1)
					{
						list.Add(selectedItem);
					}
				}
				foreach (object item in list)
				{
					SelectedItems.Remove(item);
				}
			}
			if (e.OldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (object oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
					{
						UpdateSelectedItems(oldItem, includeInCollection: false);
					}
					AttachDetachPropertyChanged(oldItem as INotifyPropertyChanged, attach: false);
				}
			}
			if (e.NewItems != null)
			{
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
					{
						UpdateSelectedItems(newItem, includeInCollection: true);
					}
					AttachDetachPropertyChanged(newItem as INotifyPropertyChanged, attach: true);
				}
			}
			_lastSetHeight = double.NaN;
			_hasElementBeenResized = false;
			InvalidateDropDownPosition(adjustHeight: true);
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedItems(object item, bool includeInCollection)
		{
			if (item != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved) && bool.Parse(item.GetType().GetProperty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved).GetValue(item, null)
				.ToString()))
			{
				if (includeInCollection)
				{
					SelectItem(item, clearRestOfSelection: false);
				}
				else
				{
					UnselectItem(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RootVisual_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject;
			while (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (dependencyObject == this)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
				if (dependencyObject is Run)
				{
					dependencyObject = ((Run)dependencyObject).Parent;
				}
				if (!(dependencyObject is Visual))
				{
					break;
				}
				DependencyObject parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
				if (parent == null && dependencyObject is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
				{
					parent = frameworkElement.Parent;
				}
				dependencyObject = parent;
			}
			if (!flag && IsDropDownOpen && !_skipDropDownClosingOnMouseClick)
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
			_skipDropDownClosingOnMouseClick = false;
		}

		{
			if (IsDropDownOpen && _rootPopupElement != null && !AllowDropDownResizingResolved)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.Width = e.NewSize.Width;
			}
		}

		}

		private void XamWebComboEditor_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_isLoaded)
			{
				_isLoaded = true;
			}
			IEnumerable enumerable = (IEnumerable)GetValue(ItemsSourceProperty);
			bool flag = false;
			if (enumerable != null)
			{
				if (ItemFilters.Count > 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (_dataManager == null)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (_dataManager != null && _dataManager.OriginalDataSource != enumerable)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				ApplyItemSource(enumerable);
			}
			CheckDisplayMemberPath();
			EnableCloseUpTriggerTrackers();
		}

		private void XamComboEditor_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_isLoaded = false;
			_isLoaded = Utilities.HasLoadedAncestor(this);
			IsDropDownOpen = false;
			DisableCloseUpTriggerTrackers();
		}

		}
