			_panel.InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private void DelayTracker_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: true);
			_delayTracker.Stop();
		}

		private void TimeoutTracker_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_searchedText = string.Empty;
			_timeoutTracker.Stop();
		}

		private void ItemsSource_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_dataManager = DataManagerBase.CreateDataManager(ItemsSource, null, unwrapCollectionView: false, preferEnumerableItemType: false, allowObjectItemType: false);
			if (_dataManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_equalsDataManager = DataManagerBase.CreateDataManager(ItemsSource, null, unwrapCollectionView: false, preferEnumerableItemType: false, allowObjectItemType: false);
			_dataManager.AllowCollectionViewOverrides = false;
			_equalsDataManager.AllowCollectionViewOverrides = false;
			if (ItemsSource is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
			}
			_dataManager.NewObjectGeneration += DataManager_NewObjectGeneration;
			_dataManager.DataUpdated += DataManager_DataUpdated;
			InitializeData();
			FillSelectedItemsBySelectedMemberPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add)
			{
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					AttachDetachPropertyChanged(newItem as INotifyPropertyChanged, attach: true);
				}
			}
			if (e.Action != NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				return;
			}
			DetachPropertyChanged();
			foreach (T item in _items)
			{
				AttachDetachPropertyChanged(item as INotifyPropertyChanged, attach: true);
			}
		}

		private void xamWebComboEditor_MouseWheel(object sender, MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			Point position = e.GetPosition(_rootPopupElement);
			bool flag = position.X >= 0.0 && position.Y >= 0.0 && position.X < _rootPopupElement.ActualWidth && position.Y < _rootPopupElement.ActualHeight;
			if (!IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				int num = ((e.Delta < 0) ? 1 : (-1));
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null && VerticalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					VerticalScrollBar.Value += VerticalScrollBar.SmallChange * (double)num;
				}
				else if (HorizontalScrollBar != null && HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					HorizontalScrollBar.Value += HorizontalScrollBar.SmallChange * (double)num;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false);
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void Editor_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_mouseButtonDownAfterGotFocus = true;
		}

		private void Editor_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_isTextCommitted = false;
			if (IsEditableResolved && !_delayTracker.IsEnabled)
			{
				_delayTracker.Start();
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(MultiColumnComboEditorClearSelectionCommand));
		}

		private void Editor_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			OnEditorKeyDown(e);
			if (!IsEditableResolved)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (e.Key)
			{
			case Key.Tab:
				_isTextCommitted = false;
				if (Editor.Text.Length > 0 && IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
				{
					if (Editor.SelectedText.Length > 0)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, SelectedItems.Count == 0);
					EnsureCaretPositionOnLostFocus();
				}
				break;
			case Key.Space:
				_isTextCommitted = false;
				if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource && Editor.SelectedText.Length > 0 && !Editor.SelectedText.Contains(" "))
				{
					CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Back:
			{
				_isTextCommitted = false;
				if (!IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
				{
					break;
				}
				int selectionStart2 = Editor.SelectionStart;
				if (Editor.Text == _previousText && _previousText.Length > 0 && selectionStart2 != 0 && AutoCompleteResolved)
				{
					SetEditorText(Editor.Text.Remove(Editor.SelectionStart - 1, Editor.SelectionLength + 1), silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
				}
				if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore)
				{
					if (Editor.Text.Length > selectionStart2)
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = selectionStart2;
					}
					else
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
					}
				}
				StartsWithItems.Clear();
				ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: true, attemptAutoComplete: false);
				if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Ignore && !AutoCompleteResolved)
				{
					if (Editor.Text.Length > selectionStart2)
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = selectionStart2;
					}
					else
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case Key.Delete:
				_isTextCommitted = false;
				if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
				{
					int selectionStart = Editor.SelectionStart;
					ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: true);
					if (Editor.Text.Length > selectionStart)
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = selectionStart;
					}
					else
					{
						Editor.SelectionStart = Editor.Text.Length;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void Editor_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_focusItem != null && ItemFilters != null && ItemFilters.Count > 0 && _isFocusLostDuringTabNavigationWithOpenedDropDown && !_focusItem.IsSelected)
			{
				_isFocusLostDuringTabNavigationWithOpenedDropDown = false;
				if (_focusItem.IsEnabled)
				{
					SelectItem(_focusItem, clearRestOfSelection: true);
					EnsureCaretPositionOnLostFocus();
					return;
				}
			}
			if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Add && !AutoCompleteResolved)
			{
				EnsureCaretPositionOnLostFocus();
				EnsureVisualStates();
				return;
			}
			bool isDropDownOpen = IsDropDownOpen;
			ProcessEditorText(allowDropDown: false);
			if (AutoCompleteResolved)
			{
				_isTextCommitted = false;
				CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: false, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: false, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
			}
			else
			{
				CommitTextAsSelected(!isDropDownOpen, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: false, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
			}
			RestoreIsTabStopValue();
			EnsureCaretPositionOnLostFocus();
			ClearFilters();
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void Editor_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			EnsureVisualStates();
			if (Mouse.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				EnsureCaretPositionOnGotFocus();
			}
		}

		}

			}
		}

		{
			if (DropDownButtonDisplayMode == DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Focused)
			{
				_toggleButton.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private void XamWebComboEditor_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				SetFocusToControlOrEditor();
				e.Handled = true;
				return;
			}
			Rect rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, base.ActualWidth, base.ActualHeight);
			Rect rect2 = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, ((FrameworkElement)_dropDownPopup.Child).ActualWidth, ((FrameworkElement)_dropDownPopup.Child).ActualHeight);
			Point position = e.GetPosition(this);
			Point position2 = e.GetPosition(_dropDownPopup.Child);
			if (!rect.Contains(position) && !rect2.Contains(position2))
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void XamWebComboEditor_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				SetFocusToControlOrEditor();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTextInput(e);
			if (!IsEditableResolved && !PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Alt))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (e.Handled || !base.IsEnabled || IsEditableResolved || (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText) && _searchedText[_searchedText.Length - 1].Equals(e.Text)) || (!IsDropDownOpen && !OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved) || !AllowFilteringResolved)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText))
			{
				_searchedText += e.Text;
			}
			else if (!_searchedText[_searchedText.Length - 1].Equals(e.Text) && e.Text.Length == 1)
			{
				_searchedText = _searchedText.Remove(_searchedText.Length - 1) + e.Text;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText))
			{
				SearchAndFilterItemsByText(_searchedText, performAutoComplete: false);
				if (Items.Count > 0)
				{
					if (_timeoutTracker.IsEnabled)
					{
						_timeoutTracker.Stop();
					}
					_timeoutTracker.Start();
				}
				else
				{
					_searchedText = string.Empty;
					ClearFilters();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ClearFilters();
			}
			_hasJustOpened = OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved && Items.Count > 0 && !IsDropDownOpen && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText);
			if (_hasJustOpened)
			{
				DropDownOpenedViaTyping = true;
				IsDropDownOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private void XamWebComboEditor_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			DropDownOpenedViaTyping = true;
			int num = 1;
			if (base.IsEnabled)
			{
				switch (e.Key)
				{
				case Key.F4:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
					}
					break;
				case Key.Right:
				case Key.Down:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						T focusItem2 = _focusItem;
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
						if (focusItem2 != null)
						{
							SetFocusedItem(focusItem2, scrollToTop: false);
						}
						num = 0;
					}
					if (IsEditableResolved && e.Key == Key.Right)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (StartsWithItems.Count > 0 && AutoCompleteResolved)
					{
						NavigateAutoCompleteOptions(increment: true, usePrevText: true, lookToCommit: false, ignoreFocusedItem: false);
					}
					else
					{
						if (_focusItem != null)
						{
							int num6 = Items.IndexOf(_focusItem);
							if (num6 != -1 && num6 + 1 != Items.Count)
							{
								SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(Items[num6 + num], forward: true, scrollToTop: false);
							}
							else if (num6 == -1 && Items.Count > 0)
							{
								SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(Items[0], forward: true, scrollToTop: false);
							}
						}
						else if (Items.Count > 0)
						{
							SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(Items[0], forward: true, scrollToTop: false);
						}
						if (_focusItem != null)
						{
							SetFocusedItem(_focusItem, scrollToTop: false);
						}
					}
					e.Handled = true;
					break;
				case Key.Left:
				case Key.Up:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						T focusItem = _focusItem;
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
						if (focusItem != null)
						{
							SetFocusedItem(focusItem, scrollToTop: false);
						}
						num = 0;
					}
					if (IsEditableResolved && e.Key == Key.Left)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (StartsWithItems.Count > 0 && AutoCompleteResolved)
					{
						NavigateAutoCompleteOptions(increment: false, usePrevText: true, lookToCommit: false, ignoreFocusedItem: false);
					}
					else if (_focusItem != null)
					{
						int num5 = Items.IndexOf(_focusItem);
						if (num5 != -1 && num5 - 1 != -1)
						{
							SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(Items[num5 - num], forward: false, scrollToTop: false);
						}
					}
					if (_focusItem != null)
					{
						SetFocusedItem(_focusItem, scrollToTop: false);
					}
					e.Handled = true;
					break;
				case Key.Next:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen && !IsEditableResolved)
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					else
					{
						if (Panel == null || Panel.VisibleItems.Count <= 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						T val2 = Panel.VisibleItems.Last();
						int num4 = Items.IndexOf(val2);
						if (_focusItem == val2)
						{
							num4++;
							num4 = ((num4 >= Items.Count) ? (Items.Count - 1) : num4);
							if (Items.Count - Items.IndexOf(val2) < Panel.VisibleItems.Count - 1)
							{
								num4 = Items.IndexOf(Items.Last());
							}
						}
						bool scrollToTop = _focusItem == val2 || Items.IndexOf(val2) - Items.IndexOf(_focusItem) <= 1;
						SetFocusedItem(Items[num4], scrollToTop);
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					break;
				case Key.Prior:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen && !IsEditableResolved)
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					else
					{
						if (Panel == null || Panel.VisibleItems.Count <= 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						T val = Panel.VisibleItems.First();
						bool flag = Panel.IsFirstItemFullyVisible();
						int num2 = Items.IndexOf(val);
						if (_focusItem == val)
						{
							double num3 = VerticalScrollBar.LargeChange;
							if (!flag)
							{
								num3 -= 1.0;
							}
							num2 -= (int)num3;
							num2 = ((num2 >= 0) ? num2 : 0);
						}
						SetFocusedItem(Items[num2], !flag);
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!e.Handled && base.IsEnabled)
			{
				switch (e.Key)
				{
				case Key.Space:
					if (!IsDropDownOpen || IsEditableResolved || AllowFilteringResolved || _focusItem == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (!_focusItem.IsSelected)
					{
						if (_focusItem.IsEnabled)
						{
							SelectItem(_focusItem, clearRestOfSelection: true);
							IsDropDownOpen = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = false;
					}
					e.Handled = true;
					break;
				case Key.Return:
					if (IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						if (_focusItem != null)
						{
							if (AllowMultipleSelection)
							{
								bool flag2 = (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control;
								if (_focusItem.IsSelected && flag2)
								{
									UnselectItem(_focusItem);
								}
								else if (_focusItem.IsEnabled)
								{
									bool flag3 = false;
									if (this is XamComboEditor xamComboEditor)
									{
										flag3 = !xamComboEditor.IsEditable;
									}
									SelectItem(_focusItem, clearRestOfSelection: false);
									if (!flag3)
									{
										string text = Editor.Text;
										ValidateSelection(text);
									}
								}
								if (!flag2)
								{
									IsDropDownOpen = false;
								}
							}
							else if (!_focusItem.IsSelected)
							{
								if (_focusItem.IsEnabled)
								{
									SelectItem(_focusItem, clearRestOfSelection: true);
									IsDropDownOpen = false;
								}
							}
							else
							{
								IsDropDownOpen = false;
							}
						}
						else if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
						{
							CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: false);
						}
						else
						{
							IsDropDownOpen = false;
						}
					}
					else if (CustomValueEnteredAction == CustomValueEnteredActions.Add)
					{
						string text2 = Editor.Text;
						if (AutoCompleteResolved)
						{
							text2 = text2.Remove(Editor.SelectionStart, Editor.SelectionLength);
						}
						SetEditorText(text2, silently: true, moveSelectionToEnd: true);
						CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: false);
					}
					else if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
					{
						CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: false, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
					}
					else
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
					}
					break;
				case Key.Escape:
				{
					bool isDropDownOpen = IsDropDownOpen;
					if (IsEditorTextValidInItemSource)
					{
						CommitTextAsSelected(closeDropDown: true, cancelSelection: true, moveSelectionToTheEnd: true, allowUseOfFocusedItem: true);
					}
					if (isDropDownOpen)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
						IsDropDownOpen = false;
					}
					break;
				}
				case Key.Tab:
					if ((Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) == ModifierKeys.Shift)
					{
						_isTabingBackwards = true;
						_originalIsTabStopValue = base.IsTabStop;
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, Control.IsTabStopProperty, false);
					}
					if (IsDropDownOpen)
					{
						_isFocusLostDuringTabNavigationWithOpenedDropDown = true;
					}
					break;
				}
				if (!IsEditableResolved && !e.Handled && (IsDropDownOpen || OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved) && AllowFilteringResolved)
				{
					string text3 = string.Empty;
					if (e.Key == Key.Back)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText))
						{
							_searchedText = _searchedText.Substring(0, _searchedText.Length - 1);
						}
						else
						{
							ClearFilters();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						string text4 = KeyHelper.GetCharFromKey(e.Key).ToString();
						if (e.Key == Key.Space)
						{
							text3 = " ";
						}
						else if (text4.Length == 1 && char.IsLetter(text4[0]))
						{
							text3 = text4[0].ToString();
						}
						else if (text4.Length == 2 && text4[0] == 'D' && char.IsNumber(text4[1]))
						{
							text3 = text4[1].ToString();
						}
						else if (text4.StartsWith("NumPad", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && text4.Length == 7)
						{
							text3 = text4.Substring(6, 1);
						}
						else if (text4.Length == 1 && char.IsDigit(text4[0]))
						{
							text3 = text4[0].ToString();
						}
						_searchedText += text3;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_searchedText))
					{
						SearchAndFilterItemsByText(_searchedText, performAutoComplete: false);
						if (Items.Count > 0)
						{
							if (_timeoutTracker.IsEnabled)
							{
								_timeoutTracker.Stop();
							}
							_timeoutTracker.Start();
						}
						else
						{
							_searchedText = string.Empty;
							ClearFilters();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ClearFilters();
					}
					if (OpenDropDownOnTypingResolved && Items.Count > 0 && !IsDropDownOpen && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						IsDropDownOpen = true;
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		private void SetFocusToNextEnabledItem(T startAtItem, bool forward, bool scrollToTop)
		{
			if (startAtItem.IsEnabled)
			{
				SetFocusedItem(startAtItem, scrollToTop);
				return;
			}
			T val = startAtItem;
			int num = Items.IndexOf(startAtItem);
			int num2 = (forward ? 1 : (-1));
			num += num2;
			for (int num3 = (forward ? (Items.Count - 1) : 0); (forward && num <= num3) || (!forward && num >= num3); num += num2)
			{
				T val2 = Items[num];
				if (val2.IsEnabled)
				{
					SetFocusedItem(val2, scrollToTop);
					return;
				}
				val = val2;
			}
			if (val != null && IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				ScrollItemIntoView(val, scrollToTop);
			}
		}

		private void SelectedItems_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_updatingSelectedItems || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				SelectedItemsChangedPostProcess(e.Action, e.NewItems, e.OldItems, e.NewStartingIndex, e.OldStartingIndex);
			}
		}

		private void SelectedItemsChangedPostProcess(NotifyCollectionChangedAction collectionChangedAction, IList newItems, IList oldItems, int newStartingIndex = -1, int oldStartingIndex = -1)
		{
			_deferClearingSelectionOnItemsSourceChanged = false;
			List<object> previouslySelected = ((!_supressSelectionChangedEvent) ? _backupSelectedItems.ToList() : null);
			if (collectionChangedAction == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				foreach (object backupSelectedItem in _backupSelectedItems)
				{
					if (_cachedRows.ContainsKey(backupSelectedItem))
					{
						_cachedRows[backupSelectedItem].SetSelected(isSelected: false);
						UpdateItemSelectedMemberPathValue(backupSelectedItem, isSelectedValue: false);
					}
				}
				_backupSelectedItems.Clear();
			}
			ObservableCollection<object> selectedItems = SelectedItems;
			if (!AllowMultipleSelection && selectedItems != null && selectedItems.Count > 1)
			{
				throw new Exception(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidMultipleSelectionException"));
			}
			if (oldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (object oldItem in oldItems)
				{
					if (_cachedRows.ContainsKey(oldItem))
					{
						_cachedRows[oldItem].SetSelected(isSelected: false);
					}
					if (_backupSelectedItems.Contains(oldItem))
					{
						_backupSelectedItems.Remove(oldItem);
					}
					if (oldItem is DataRowView)
					{
						DataRowView dataRowView = oldItem as DataRowView;
						if (dataRowView.Row.RowState == DataRowState.Detached || dataRowView.Row.RowState == DataRowState.Deleted)
						{
							continue;
						}
					}
					UpdateItemSelectedMemberPathValue(oldItem, isSelectedValue: false);
				}
			}
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (newItems != null && dataManager != null)
			{
				int num = newStartingIndex;
				foreach (object newItem in newItems)
				{
					if (_cachedRows.ContainsKey(newItem))
					{
						_cachedRows[newItem].SetSelected(isSelected: true);
					}
					else
					{
						int num2 = dataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(newItem);
						if (num2 != -1)
						{
							Items[num2].SetSelected(isSelected: true);
						}
						else if (CustomValueEnteredAction != CustomValueEnteredActions.Allow)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!_backupSelectedItems.Contains(newItem))
					{
						_backupSelectedItems.Insert(num, newItem);
						UpdateItemSelectedMemberPathValue(newItem, isSelectedValue: true);
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!_supressSelectionChangedEvent)
			{
				_ignoreSelectionChanging = true;
				if (selectedItems != null && selectedItems.Count > 0 && dataManager != null)
				{
					object obj = null;
					obj = ((newItems == null || newItems.Count <= 0) ? selectedItems[selectedItems.Count - 1] : newItems[newItems.Count - 1]);
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedIndexProperty, dataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(obj));
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, obj);
					if (SelectedValuePathResolved != null)
					{
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValueProperty, DataManagerBase.ResolveValueFromPropertyPath(SelectedValuePathResolved, SelectedItem));
						List<object> list = new List<object>();
						foreach (object item2 in selectedItems)
						{
							object item = DataManagerBase.ResolveValueFromPropertyPath(SelectedValuePathResolved, item2);
							list.Add(item);
						}
						DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, list.ToArray());
					}
				}
				else
				{
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedIndexProperty, -1);
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedItemProperty, null);
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValueProperty, null);
					DependencyPropertyUtilities.SetCurrentValue(this, SelectedValuesProperty, null);
				}
				_ignoreSelectionChanging = false;
				OnSelectionChanged(previouslySelected, SelectedItems);
			}
			InvalidateSelectedContent();
			EnsureVisualStates();
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(MultiColumnComboEditorClearSelectionCommand));
		}

		private void SyncFocusedItem(T item = null)
		{
			object obj = ((item != null) ? item.Data : SelectedItem);
			if (obj != null)
			{
				if (_focusItem != null)
				{
					if (_focusItem.Data != obj && DataManager != null)
					{
						int index = DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(obj);
						SetFocusedItem(Items[index], scrollToTop: false, !IsDropDownOpen);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int index2 = DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(obj);
					SetFocusedItem(Items[index2], scrollToTop: false);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				SetFocusedItem(null, scrollToTop: false);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateItemSelectedMemberPathValue(object item, bool isSelectedValue)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager == null || !_reflectChangesOverModel || string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved) || DataManagerBase.ResolvePropertyTypeFromPropertyName(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved, dataManager.CachedTypedInfo) != typeof(bool))
			{
				return;
			}
			PropertyInfo property = item.GetType().GetProperty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved);
			bool flag = ((!(property == null)) ? bool.Parse(property.GetValue(item, null).ToString()) : bool.Parse(_dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[IsSelectedMemberPathResolved].GetValue(item).ToString()));
			if (flag != isSelectedValue)
			{
				_supressPropertyChangedEvent = true;
				if (property == null)
				{
					_dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[IsSelectedMemberPathResolved].SetValue(item, isSelectedValue);
				}
				else
				{
					property.SetValue(item, isSelectedValue, null);
				}
				_supressPropertyChangedEvent = false;
			}
		}

		private void ComboEditorBase_SizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_rootPopupElement != null)
			{
				_rootPopupElement.MinWidth = ((double.IsInfinity(MinDropDownWidth) || double.IsNaN(MinDropDownWidth)) ? base.ActualWidth : MinDropDownWidth);
			}
		}

		private void ComboEditorBase_LayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.VerticalAlignment != _lastVerticalAlignment)
			{
				_invalidateHeight = true;
				_lastVerticalAlignment = base.VerticalAlignment;
			}
		}

		private void _xamWebComboEditor_LostMouseCapture(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsDropDownOpen || Mouse.Captured == this)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Mouse.Captured is DependencyObject && !Utilities.IsDescendantOf(this, Mouse.Captured as DependencyObject))
			{
				IsDropDownOpen = false;
				return;
			}
			_elementThatStoleMouseCapture = Mouse.Captured as UIElement;
			if (_elementThatStoleMouseCapture != null)
			{
				_elementThatStoleMouseCapture.LostMouseCapture += _elementThatStoleMouseCapture_LostMouseCapture;
			}
		}

		private void dropdownPopupChildElement_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					Mouse.Capture(this, CaptureMode.SubTree);
					base.LostMouseCapture += _xamWebComboEditor_LostMouseCapture;
				}, DispatcherPriority.Input);
			}
		}

		private void _elementThatStoleMouseCapture_LostMouseCapture(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_elementThatStoleMouseCapture != null)
			{
				_elementThatStoleMouseCapture.LostMouseCapture -= _elementThatStoleMouseCapture_LostMouseCapture;
				_elementThatStoleMouseCapture = null;
			}
			if (IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				Mouse.Capture(this, CaptureMode.SubTree);
			}
		}

		private void _rootPopupElement_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ReleaseMouseCapture();
		}

		private void Item_PropertyChanged(object item, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_supressPropertyChangedEvent && (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName) || e.PropertyName == IsSelectedMemberPathResolved))
			{
				UpdateSelectedItemsPerItem(item);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedItemsPerItem(object item)
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved) && !(DataManagerBase.ResolvePropertyTypeFromPropertyName(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved, dataManager.CachedTypedInfo) != typeof(bool)))
			{
				PropertyInfo property = item.GetType().GetProperty(IsSelectedMemberPathResolved);
				if ((!(property == null)) ? bool.Parse(property.GetValue(item, null).ToString()) : ((bool)_dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors[IsSelectedMemberPathResolved].GetValue(item)))
				{
					SelectItem(item, clearRestOfSelection: false);
				}
				else
				{
					UnselectItem(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void FlowDirectionChanged()
		{
			_heightRestrictionsChanged = true;
		}

		T IProvideDataItems<T>.GetDataItem(int index)
		{
			return GetDataItem(index);
		}

		T IProvideDataItems<T>.CreateItem()
		{
			return CreateItem();
		}

		T IProvideDataItems<T>.CreateItem(object dataItem)
		{
			return CreateItem(dataItem);
		}

		void IProvideDataItems<T>.AddItem(T addedObject)
		{
			if (!OnItemAdding(addedObject))
			{
				AddItem(addedObject);
				OnItemAdded(addedObject);
			}
		}

		bool IProvideDataItems<T>.RemoveItem(T removedObject)
		{
			return RemoveItem(removedObject);
		}

		void IProvideDataItems<T>.RemoveRange(IList<T> itemsToRemove)
		{
			RemoveRange(itemsToRemove);
		}

		void IProvideDataItems<T>.InsertItem(int index, T insertedObject)
		{
			if (!OnItemAdding(insertedObject))
			{
				InsertItem(index, insertedObject);
				OnItemAdded(insertedObject);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void FinishedLoadingPersistence()
		{
		}

		void IProvidePropertyPersistenceSettings.FinishedLoadingPersistence()
		{
			FinishedLoadingPersistence();
		}

			if (Panel != null && Panel.VisibleItems.Count > 0)
			{
				return Panel.VisibleItems[0].Control.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		}

		private static object ParseEnum(string text, Type typeOfTheEnum)
		{
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				return Enum.Parse(typeOfTheEnum, text, ignoreCase: true);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				try
				{
					return Enum.Parse(typeOfTheEnum, text, ignoreCase: false);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsComposing(TextBoxBase editor)
		{
			if (editor != null && _isComposingMethod != null)
			{
				return true.Equals(_isComposingMethod.Invoke(null, new object[1] { editor }));
			}
			return false;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void BringImeToFront()
		{
			if (IsComposing(Editor))
			{
				IntPtr intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.FindWindow("mscandui40.candidate", null);
				if (intPtr != IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					NativeWindowMethods.SetWindowPosApi(intPtr, NativeWindowMethods.HWND_TOPMOST, 0, 0, 0, 0, 19);
				}
			}
		}

		private void DropDownPopup_Opened(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				BringImeToFront();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

	internal static class KeyHelper
	{
		public enum MapType : uint
		{
			MAPVK_VK_TO_VSC,
			MAPVK_VSC_TO_VK,
			MAPVK_VK_TO_CHAR,
			MAPVK_VSC_TO_VK_EX
		}

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		public static extern int ToUnicode(uint wVirtKey, uint wScanCode, byte[] lpKeyState, [Out][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPWStr)] StringBuilder pwszBuff, int cchBuff, uint wFlags);

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		public static extern bool GetKeyboardState(byte[] lpKeyState);

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		public static extern uint MapVirtualKey(uint uCode, MapType uMapType);

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		public static char GetCharFromKey(Key key)
		{
			char result = ' ';
			int num = KeyInterop.VirtualKeyFromKey(key);
			byte[] lpKeyState = new byte[256];
			GetKeyboardState(lpKeyState);
			uint wScanCode = MapVirtualKey((uint)num, MapType.MAPVK_VK_TO_VSC);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(2);
			switch (ToUnicode((uint)num, wScanCode, lpKeyState, stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Capacity, 0u))
			{
			case 1:
				result = stringBuilder[0];
				break;
			default:
				result = stringBuilder[0];
				break;
			case -1:
			case 0:
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}

		static XamMultiColumnComboEditor()
		{
			AutoGenerateColumnsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoGenerateColumns", typeof(bool), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(true, AutoGenerateColumnsChanged));
			FilterModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FilterMode", typeof(FilterMode), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(FilterMode.FilterOnAllColumns, FilterModeChanged));
			FooterProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Footer", typeof(object), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, OnFooterChanged));
			FooterTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("FooterTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, OnFooterTemplateChanged));
			SelectedItemsResetButtonVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedItemsResetButtonVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox, SelectedItemsResetButtonVisibilityChanged));
			ColumnWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColumnWidth", typeof(ComboColumnWidth), typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new PropertyMetadata(ComboColumnWidth.Auto, ColumnWidthChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor)));
			ThemeManager.RegisterControl(typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor));
			ControlTemplate controlTemplate = new ControlTemplate();
			controlTemplate.Seal();
			Validation.ErrorTemplateProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(controlTemplate));
		}

		public XamMultiColumnComboEditor()
		{
			_license = Utilities.ValidateLicense(typeof(XamMultiColumnComboEditor), this);
			base.SupportsAlternateFilters = true;
		}

		private static void AutoGenerateColumnsChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamMultiColumnComboEditor = obj as XamMultiColumnComboEditor;
			if (xamMultiColumnComboEditor.ItemsSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!(bool)e.NewValue && xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns != null)
			{
				ObservableCollection<ComboColumn> columns = xamMultiColumnComboEditor.Columns;
				for (int num = columns.Count - 1; num > 0; num--)
				{
					if (columns[num].IsAutoGenerated)
					{
						columns.RemoveAt(num);
					}
				}
			}
			xamMultiColumnComboEditor.UnhookDataManager();
			xamMultiColumnComboEditor.ApplyItemSource(xamMultiColumnComboEditor.ItemsSource);
		}

		private static void FilterModeChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamMultiColumnComboEditor obj2 = obj as XamMultiColumnComboEditor;
			obj2.FilterModeResolved = (FilterMode)e.NewValue;
			obj2.SupportsAlternateFilters = obj2.FilterModeResolved == FilterMode.FilterOnAllColumns;
			obj2.AutoCompleteResolved = obj2.FilterModeResolved == FilterMode.FilterOnPrimaryColumnOnly;
			obj2.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition: false, resetItems: true);
		}

		protected virtual ComboColumn GenerateColumnForField(DataField field)
		{
			ComboColumnTypeMappingsCollection columnTypeMappings = ColumnTypeMappings;
			ComboColumn comboColumn = null;
			foreach (ComboColumnTypeMapping item in columnTypeMappings)
			{
				if (item.DataType != null && item.ColumnType != null && (item.DataType == field.FieldType || (item.DataType.IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType) && !typeof(string).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType) && field.FieldType.GetInterface("IDictionary", ignoreCase: false) == null)))
				{
					bool num = item.DataType.Name.Contains("Nullable`1");
					bool flag = field.FieldType.Name.Contains("Nullable`1");
					if (num == flag)
					{
						comboColumn = item.ColumnType.GetConstructor(new Type[0]).Invoke(null) as ComboColumn;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (comboColumn == null)
			{
				comboColumn = new TextComboColumn();
			}
			return comboColumn;
		}

		private void InvalidateHeaderRows()
		{
			if (base.Panel is MultiColumnComboItemsPanel multiColumnComboItemsPanel)
			{
				if (_headerRow != null)
				{
					multiColumnComboItemsPanel.UnregisterFixedRowTop(_headerRow);
				}
				multiColumnComboItemsPanel.RegisterFixedRowTop(HeaderRow);
			}
		}

		internal void RegisterCellTextBlock(HighlightingTextBlock highlightingTextBlock)
		{
			if (!CellTextBlocks.Contains(highlightingTextBlock))
			{
				_cellTextBlocks.Add(highlightingTextBlock);
				highlightingTextBlock.ComboEditor = this;
			}
		}

		internal static void ValidateColumn(ComboColumn column, Dictionary<string, DataField> dataKeys, DataManagerBase manager, PropertyDescriptorCollection pdcs)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Key))
			{
				throw new Exception(SRCombo.GetString("EmpyKeyException"));
			}
			if (column.Key.Contains("[") && column.Key.Contains("]"))
			{
				try
				{
					System.Linq.Expressions.Expression expression = DataManagerBase.BuildPropertyExpressionFromPropertyName(column.Key, System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Parameter(manager.CachedType, "param"));
					if (expression != null)
					{
						column.DataType = expression.Type;
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if (pdcs != null)
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = pdcs[column.Key];
				if (propertyDescriptor != null)
				{
					column.DataType = propertyDescriptor.PropertyType;
					if (dataKeys.ContainsKey(column.Key))
					{
						DataField dataField = (column.DataField = dataKeys[column.Key]);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.HeaderText))
						{
							column.HeaderText = dataField.DisplayName;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			string[] array = column.Key.Split('.');
			if (array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!dataKeys.ContainsKey(array[0]))
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidKeyException"), column.Key));
			}
			DataField dataField3 = dataKeys[array[0]];
			Type type = manager.CachedType;
			bool flag = true;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string name in array2)
			{
				PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty(name);
				if (property == null)
				{
					throw new Exception(string.Format(SRCombo.GetString("InvalidKeyException"), column.Key));
				}
				type = property.PropertyType;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				column.DataType = type;
				column.DataField = dataField3;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.HeaderText))
				{
					column.HeaderText = dataField3.DisplayName;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			FooterContentArea = GetTemplateChild("FooterContentArea") as ContentControl;
		}

		protected override ComboRow GenerateNewObject(object data)
		{
			return new ComboRow(data, this);
		}

		internal override RecordFilterCollection GetAlternateFilters(string text)
		{
			if (FilterMode == FilterMode.FilterOnPrimaryColumnOnly)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager != null)
			{
				RecordFilterCollection recordFilterCollection = new RecordFilterCollection
				{
					LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or
				};
				Expression<Func<string, string, bool>> filterExpression = (string s, string f) => s != null && s.IndexOf(f, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
				{
					foreach (ComboColumn column in Columns)
					{
						if (!(column is CheckboxComboColumn) && !(column is ImageComboColumn))
						{
							ComboItemFilter comboItemFilter = new ComboItemFilter
							{
								ObjectType = dataManager.CachedType,
								FieldName = column.Key,
								Field = column.DataField,
								ObjectTypedInfo = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo
							};
							comboItemFilter.Conditions.LogicalOperator = LogicalOperator.Or;
							if (dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors != null && dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors.Count > 0)
							{
								CustomComparisonCondition item = CreateComplexPropertyCondition(dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.CachedType, column.Key, text, filterExpression, dataManager.CachedTypedInfo);
								comboItemFilter.Conditions.Add(item);
							}
							else
							{
								CustomComparisonCondition item2 = CreateComplexPropertyCondition(dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.CachedType, column.Key, text, filterExpression, null);
								comboItemFilter.Conditions.Add(item2);
							}
							recordFilterCollection.Add(comboItemFilter);
						}
					}
					return recordFilterCollection;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal override void ClearDataProperties()
		{
			base.ClearDataProperties();
			if (!AutoGenerateColumns)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int num = Columns.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				ComboColumn comboColumn = Columns[num];
				if (comboColumn.IsAutoGenerated)
				{
					Columns.Remove(comboColumn);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void InitializeData()
		{
			DataManagerBase dataManager = DataManager;
			if (dataManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Columns.IsInitialized = false;
			IEnumerable<DataField> dataManagerDataFields = GetDataManagerDataFields();
			Dictionary<string, DataField> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, DataField>();
			_ = Columns.Count;
			foreach (DataField item in dataManagerDataFields)
			{
				dictionary.Add(item.Name, item);
			}
			DataKeys = dictionary;
			if (AutoGenerateColumns)
			{
				Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
				foreach (ComboColumn column in Columns)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Key))
					{
						collection.Add(column.Key);
						column.ComboEditor = this;
					}
				}
				foreach (DataField item2 in dataManagerDataFields)
				{
					if (item2.AutoGenerate && !collection.Contains(item2.Name))
					{
						ComboColumn comboColumn = GenerateColumnForField(item2);
						if (comboColumn != null)
						{
							comboColumn.DataField = item2;
							comboColumn.IsAutoGenerated = true;
							comboColumn.Key = item2.Name;
							Columns.Add(comboColumn);
							comboColumn.DataType = item2.FieldType;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptors = dataManager.CachedTypedInfo.PropertyDescriptors;
			foreach (ComboColumn column2 in Columns)
			{
				ValidateColumn(column2, dictionary, dataManager, propertyDescriptors);
			}
			Columns.IsInitialized = true;
			InvalidateHeaderRows();
		}

		internal override void OnCurrentSearchTextChanged(string currentSearchText)
		{
			foreach (HighlightingTextBlock cellTextBlock in CellTextBlocks)
			{
				cellTextBlock.HighlightText(currentSearchText);
			}
		}

		protected override bool OnDropDownOpening()
		{
			InvalidateHeaderRows();
			return base.OnDropDownOpening();
		}

		protected override void OnEditorKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		internal override void InvalidateScrollPanel(bool resetScrollPosition, bool resetItems)
		{
			if (resetScrollPosition)
			{
				OverrideHorizontalMax = -1.0;
			}
			base.InvalidateScrollPanel(resetScrollPosition, resetItems);
		}

		internal override void OnComboEditorItemClicked(ComboRow comboEditorItem)
		{
			base.OnComboEditorItemClicked(comboEditorItem);
			if (comboEditorItem != null && comboEditorItem.Control != null)
			{
				comboEditorItem.Control.InternalEnsureAllCellVisualStates();
			}
		}

		internal override double GetAdditionalElementsHeight()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (Footer != null || FooterTemplate != null)
			{
				num = FooterContentArea.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			return base.GetAdditionalElementsHeight() + num;
		}

		private void Columns_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateHeaderRows();
		}

		object ICommandTarget.GetParameter(CommandSource source)
		{
			if (source.Command is MultiColumnComboEditorCommandBase)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		bool ICommandTarget.SupportsCommand(ICommand command)
		{
			if (command is CommandBase { CommandSource: not null } commandBase && commandBase.CommandSource.Parameter != null)
			{
				return commandBase.CommandSource.Parameter == this;
			}
			return command is MultiColumnComboEditorCommandBase;
		}
	}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			double left = 0.0;
			double num = 0.0;
			InLayoutPhase = false;
			MeasureCalled = false;
			if (ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				left = 0.0 - ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Value;
			}
			_clipRG.Rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, finalSize.Width, finalSize.Height);
			foreach (FrameworkElement availableElement in RecyclingManager.Manager.GetAvailableElements(this))
			{
				availableElement.Arrange(new Rect(-1000.0, -1000.0, 0.0, 0.0));
			}
			foreach (T item in _lastItemsDetached)
			{
				if (item.Control != null && !VisibleItems.Contains(item))
				{
					item.Control.Arrange(new Rect(-1000.0, -1000.0, 0.0, 0.0));
				}
			}
			int count = ComboEditor.Items.Count;
			UpdateScrollInfo(count, finalSize);
			if (FixedRowsTop != null)
			{
				foreach (ComboRowBase item2 in FixedRowsTop)
				{
					if (item2.Control != null)
					{
						ArrangeItem(item2.Control, left, num, item2.Control.DesiredSize.Width);
						Panel.SetZIndex(item2.Control, 10003);
						num += item2.Control.DesiredSize.Height;
					}
				}
			}
			if (count != 0)
			{
				ScrollBar verticalScrollBar = ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar;
				if (verticalScrollBar != null && verticalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && VisibleItems.Count > 0)
				{
					if (_measureScrollBarValue != verticalScrollBar.Maximum)
					{
						double num2 = _measureScrollBarValue - (double)(int)_measureScrollBarValue;
						double num3 = VisibleItems[0].Control.DesiredSize.Height * num2;
						double measureScrollBarValue = _measureScrollBarValue;
						double overrideVerticalMax = _overrideVerticalMax;
						num = ((!(measureScrollBarValue >= overrideVerticalMax) || !(overrideVerticalMax > 0.0)) ? (num + (0.0 - num3)) : (num - _overflowAdjustment));
					}
					else
					{
						num -= _overflowAdjustment;
					}
					verticalScrollBar.SmallChange = (double)VisibleItems.Count / 10.0;
				}
				double num4 = ((ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar != null && ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible) ? ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Value : 0.0);
				double width = finalSize.Width + num4;
				foreach (T visibleItem in VisibleItems)

					}

					}
