					{
						InvalidateMeasure();
					}
					ScrollItemIntoViewInProgress = false;
					_scrollIntoViewItem = null;
				}
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		private void ReleaseItem(ISupportRecycling comboEditorItem)
		{
			RecyclingManager.Manager.ReleaseElement(comboEditorItem, this);
		}

		private void ThrowoutUnusedItems(List<T> previousVisibleItems)
		{
			foreach (T previousVisibleItem in previousVisibleItems)
			{
				if (!VisibleItems.Contains(previousVisibleItem) && previousVisibleItem.Control != null)
				{
					ReleaseItem(previousVisibleItem);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScrollInfo(int totalRowCount, Size finalSize)
		{
			ScrollBar verticalScrollBar = ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar;
			if (verticalScrollBar != null)
			{
				_ = verticalScrollBar.Value;
				if (_overrideVerticalMax >= 0.0)
				{
					verticalScrollBar.Maximum = _overrideVerticalMax;
				}
				verticalScrollBar.ViewportSize = VisibleItems.Count;
				verticalScrollBar.LargeChange = verticalScrollBar.ViewportSize;
				verticalScrollBar.SmallChange = verticalScrollBar.ViewportSize / 10.0;
				Visibility visibility = verticalScrollBar.Visibility;
				verticalScrollBar.Visibility = (((verticalScrollBar.ViewportSize == (double)totalRowCount && _overrideVerticalMax < 0.1) || totalRowCount == 0) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				if (verticalScrollBar.Visibility != visibility)
				{
					if (verticalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
					{
						verticalScrollBar.Value = 0.0;
					}
					else if (ComboEditor.SelectedItem != null && ComboEditor.HasJustOpened)
					{
						int num = ComboEditor.DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(ComboEditor.SelectedItem);
						if (num > -1 && !VisibleItems.Contains(ComboEditor.Items[num]))
						{
							ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ScrollBar horizontalScrollBar = ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar;
			if (horizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				Visibility visibility2 = horizontalScrollBar.Visibility;
				_isUpdatingHorizontalScrollBar = true;
				UpdateHorizontalScrollBar(horizontalScrollBar, finalSize);
				_isUpdatingHorizontalScrollBar = false;
				HorizontalScrollBarJustMadeVisible = visibility2 != horizontalScrollBar.Visibility && horizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private double GetHeightRelatedProperties(FrameworkElement elem)
		{
			if (elem == null)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			if (elem is Border border)
			{
				num += border.BorderThickness.Bottom + border.BorderThickness.Top;
			}
			return elem.Margin.Bottom + elem.Margin.Top + num;
		}

		private double ReduceWidthWithRootBorderThickness(double width)
		{
			double rootBorderWidth = ComboEditor.GetRootBorderWidth();
			return width - rootBorderWidth;
		}

		protected virtual Size RenderItem<T2, TControl2>(T2 comboEditorItem, Size availableSize) where T2 : ComboEditorItemBase<TControl2> where TControl2 : FrameworkElement
		{
			Size result = default(Size);
			if (comboEditorItem.Control == null)
			{
				RecyclingManager.Manager.AttachElement(comboEditorItem, this);
			}
			comboEditorItem.MeasureRaised = false;
			double width = double.PositiveInfinity;
			if (UseAvailableWidthToMeasureItem)
			{
				width = availableSize.Width;
			}
			if (comboEditorItem.Control != null)
			{
				OnControlAttachedToItem(comboEditorItem.Control as TControl);
				comboEditorItem.Control.Measure(new Size(width, double.PositiveInfinity));
				comboEditorItem.EnsureVisualStates();
				result = comboEditorItem.Control.DesiredSize;
			}
			if (!comboEditorItem.MeasureRaised)
			{
				comboEditorItem.Control.Measure(new Size(1.0, 1.0));
				comboEditorItem.Control.Measure(new Size(width, double.PositiveInfinity));
				result = comboEditorItem.Control.DesiredSize;
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal Size RenderItem(T comboEditorItem, Size availableSize)
		{
			return RenderItem<T, TControl>(comboEditorItem, availableSize);
		}

		protected virtual void ArrangeItem(FrameworkElement elem, double left, double top, double width)
		{
			elem.Arrange(new Rect(left, top, Math.Max(elem.DesiredSize.Width, width), elem.DesiredSize.Height));
		}

		protected virtual void OnControlAttachedToItem(TControl cntrl)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateHorizontalScrollBar(ScrollBar horizBar, Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = ReduceWidthWithRootBorderThickness(_actualWidth);
			if ((finalSize.Width < num) | (ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				bool flag = false;
				horizBar.LargeChange = horizBar.ViewportSize;
				horizBar.SmallChange = horizBar.ViewportSize / 10.0;
				horizBar.ViewportSize = finalSize.Width;
				horizBar.Maximum = num - finalSize.Width;
				if (horizBar.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed && horizBar.ViewportSize > 0.0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					horizBar.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (finalSize.Width == num && horizBar.Value == 0.0 && !HorizontalScrollBarJustMadeVisible)
				{
					horizBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					if (!_isUpdatingHorizontalScrollBar)
					{
						ComboEditor.InvalidateDropDownPosition(adjustHeight: false);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				horizBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				horizBar.Value = 0.0;
			}
			_lastHeight = finalSize.Height;
		}

		internal void ScrollItemIntoView(T item, bool scrollToTop)
		{
			if (ComboEditor == null || ComboEditor.DataManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = ComboEditor.DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(item.Data);
			bool flag = false;
			Rect rect = Rect.Empty;
			if (item.Control != null)
			{
				rect = LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(item.Control);
				if (rect.Top > -1000.0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				Rect layoutSlot = LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(this);
				if (scrollToTop)
				{
					ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
				}
				else if (rect.Height + rect.Top > layoutSlot.Height)
				{
					_reverseItemStartIndex = num;
					_reverseMeasure = true;
				}
				else if (rect.Top < 0.0)
				{
					ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
				}
			}
			else if (VisibleItems.Count > 0)
			{
				if (scrollToTop)
				{
					ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
				}
				else
				{
					int num2 = ComboEditor.DataManager.ResolveIndexForRecord(VisibleItems[VisibleItems.Count - 1].Data);
					if (num2 != -1)
					{
						if (num > num2)
						{
							_reverseItemStartIndex = num;
							_reverseMeasure = true;
						}
						else
						{
							ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
				if (ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value != (double)num)
				{
					ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Maximum = num + 1;
					ComboEditor.VerticalScrollBar.Value = num;
				}
			}
			_scrollIntoViewItem = item;
			ScrollItemIntoViewInProgress = true;
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		internal void ResetItems()
		{
			_isCurrentlyReleasingItems = true;
			foreach (T visibleItem in VisibleItems)
			{
				ReleaseItem(visibleItem);
			}
			foreach (T item in _lastItemsDetached)
			{
				ReleaseItem(item);
			}
			RecyclingManager.Manager.ReleaseAll(this, typeof(TControl));
			VisibleItems.Clear();
			_lastItemsDetached.Clear();
			_overrideVerticalMax = -1.0;
			_overflowAdjustment = -1.0;
			_actualWidth = 0.0;
			_isCurrentlyReleasingItems = false;
		}

		internal void DropDownClosed()
		{
			_reverseMeasure = false;
		}

		internal bool IsFirstItemFullyVisible()
		{
			if (VisibleItems == null || VisibleItems.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Rect layoutSlot = LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(VisibleItems.First().Control);
			double num = 0.0;
			if (FixedRowsTop != null)
			{
				num = FixedRowsTop.Sum((ComboRowBase item) => LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(item.Control).Height);
			}
			return layoutSlot.Top - num >= 0.0;
		}

		internal double UpdateHeightWithParentSizeProperties(double height)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = this;
			FrameworkElement rootPopupElement = ComboEditor.GetRootPopupElement();
			do
			{
				if (dependencyObject is FrameworkElement)
				{
					height += GetHeightRelatedProperties(dependencyObject as FrameworkElement);
				}
				if (dependencyObject == rootPopupElement)
				{
					break;
				}
				dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
			}
			while (dependencyObject != null);
			return height;
		}

		private void ItemsPanelBase_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}
	}
	public class ComboCellsCollection : CollectionBase<ComboCellBase>
	{
		private ComboRowBase _row;

		private ComboColumnCollection _columns;

		private Dictionary<ComboColumn, ComboCellBase> _auxColumns;

		public ComboCellBase this[ComboColumn column]
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (ComboCellBase item in Items)
				{
					if (item.Column == column)
					{
						return item;
					}
				}
				if (_auxColumns.ContainsKey(column))
				{
					return _auxColumns[column];
				}
				ComboCellBase comboCellBase = column.GenerateCell(_row);
				Items.Add(comboCellBase);
				_auxColumns.Add(column, comboCellBase);
				return comboCellBase;
			}
		}

		public ComboCellBase this[string key] => this[_columns[key]];

		protected ComboRowBase Row => _row;

		public ComboCellsCollection(ComboColumnCollection columns, ComboRowBase row)
		{
			_row = row;
			_columns = columns;
			_auxColumns = new Dictionary<ComboColumn, ComboCellBase>();
		}

		protected override int GetCount()
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (_row != null)
			{
				result = _columns.Count;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override int IndexOf(ComboCellBase item)
		{
			return _columns.IndexOf(item.Column);
		}

		protected override ComboCellBase GetItem(int index)
		{
			return this[_columns[index]];
		}
	}
	public class CheckboxComboColumnContentProvider : ComboColumnContentProviderBase
	{
		private CheckBox _cb;

		public CheckboxComboColumnContentProvider()
		{
			_cb = new CheckBox();
		}

		public override void AdjustDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			if (cell != null && cell.Control != null && cell.Column.CellStyle == null)
			{
				cell.Control.HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
			}
		}

		public override FrameworkElement ResolveDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell, Binding cellBinding)
		{
			_cb.IsThreeState = cell.Column.DataType == typeof(bool?);
			_cb.IsEnabled = false;
			_cb.IsHitTestVisible = false;
			if (cellBinding != null)
			{
				_cb.SetBinding(ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, cellBinding);
			}
			return _cb;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ComboColumnContentProviderBase
	{
		public abstract FrameworkElement ResolveDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell, Binding cellBinding);

		public virtual void AdjustDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
		}

		public virtual object ApplyFormatting(object value, ComboColumn column, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value;
		}

		protected internal virtual Binding ResolveBinding(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			Binding result = null;
			if (cell != null)
			{
				result = ResolveBindingInternal(cell);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static Binding ResolveBindingInternal(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			Binding binding = null;
			if (cell.Column.Key != null && cell.Column.DataType != null)
			{
				binding = new Binding(cell.Column.Key);
				binding.ConverterCulture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				binding.Mode = cell.BindingMode;
				binding.ConverterParameter = cell.Control;
				binding.Converter = cell.CreateCellBindingConverter();
			}
			return binding;
		}
	}
	public class DateComboColumnContentProvider : TextComboColumnContentProvider
	{
		public override object ApplyFormatting(object value, ComboColumn column, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			TextComboColumn textComboColumn = (TextComboColumn)column;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textComboColumn.FormatString))
			{
				return string.Format(culture, textComboColumn.FormatString, new object[1] { value });
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textComboColumn.DataField.FormatString))
			{
				return string.Format(culture, textComboColumn.DataField.FormatString, new object[1] { value });
			}
			return string.Format(culture, "{0:d}", new object[1] { value });
		}
	}
	public class ImageComboColumnContentProvider : ComboColumnContentProviderBase
	{
		private Image _image;

		private bool _heightSet;

		private bool _widthSet;

		public ImageComboColumnContentProvider()
		{
			_image = new Image();
		}

		public override void AdjustDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			ImageComboColumn imageComboColumn = (ImageComboColumn)cell.Column;
			if (imageComboColumn.Key == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_image.Style != imageComboColumn.ImageStyle)
			{
				_image.Style = imageComboColumn.ImageStyle;
			}
			if (_image.Source == null && cell.Control != null && _image.DesiredSize.Width == 0.0 && _image.DesiredSize.Height == 0.0)
			{
				cell.Control.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
				cell.Control.Measure(new Size(1.0, 1.0));
			}
			if (_image.Source != null)
			{
				double imageHeight = imageComboColumn.ImageHeight;
				if (imageHeight != 0.0 && _image.Height != imageHeight)
				{
					_image.Height = imageHeight;
					_heightSet = true;
				}
				imageHeight = imageComboColumn.ImageWidth;
				if (imageHeight != 0.0)
				{
					_image.Width = imageHeight;
					_widthSet = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (_heightSet)
				{
					_heightSet = false;
					_image.Height = double.NaN;
				}
				if (_widthSet)
				{
					_widthSet = false;
					_image.Width = double.NaN;
				}
			}
			_image.Measure(new Size(1.0, 1.0));
		}

		public override FrameworkElement ResolveDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell, Binding cellBinding)
		{
			_image.IsHitTestVisible = false;
			if (cellBinding != null)
			{
				_image.SetBinding(Image.SourceProperty, cellBinding);
			}
			ImageComboColumn imageComboColumn = (ImageComboColumn)cell.Column;
			if (imageComboColumn.Key != null && _image.Source != null)
			{
				double imageHeight = imageComboColumn.ImageHeight;
				if (imageHeight != 0.0 && _image.Height != imageHeight)
				{
					_image.Height = imageHeight;
					_heightSet = true;
				}
				if (imageComboColumn.ImageWidth != 0.0)
				{
					_image.Width = imageComboColumn.ImageWidth;
					_widthSet = true;
				}
			}
			return _image;
		}
	}
	public class TextComboColumnContentProvider : ComboColumnContentProviderBase
	{
		private HighlightingTextBlock _tb;

		public TextComboColumnContentProvider()
		{
			_tb = new HighlightingTextBlock();
		}

		public override FrameworkElement ResolveDisplayElement(ComboCellBase cell, Binding cellBinding)
		{
			TextComboColumn textComboColumn = (TextComboColumn)cell.Column;
			_tb.TextBlockTextWrapping = textComboColumn.TextWrapping;
			_tb.TextBlockStyle = textComboColumn.TextBlockStyle;
			_tb.SetBinding(HighlightingTextBlock.TextProperty, cellBinding);
			XamMultiColumnComboEditor comboEditor = textComboColumn.ComboEditor;
			_tb.TextNeedsHighlighting = comboEditor.FilterMode != FilterMode.FilterOnPrimaryColumnOnly || textComboColumn.Key.Equals(comboEditor.DisplayMemberPathResolved);
			if (cell.Column.DataType == typeof(string) && cell.Row.RowType == RowType.DataRow)
			{
				cell.Row.ComboEditor.RegisterCellTextBlock(_tb);
			}
			return _tb;
		}

		public override object ApplyFormatting(object value, ComboColumn column, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			TextComboColumn textComboColumn = (TextComboColumn)column;
			if (textComboColumn != null && textComboColumn.DataField != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textComboColumn.FormatString))
				{
					return string.Format(culture, textComboColumn.FormatString, new object[1] { value });
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textComboColumn.DataField.FormatString))
				{
					return string.Format(culture, textComboColumn.DataField.FormatString, new object[1] { value });
				}
			}
			_tb.HighlightText();
			return base.ApplyFormatting(value, column, culture);
		}
	}
	public abstract class MultiColumnComboEditorCommandBase : CommandBase
	{
		public override bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public override void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			ExecuteCommand(parameter);
			base.CommandSource.Handled = true;
			base.Execute(parameter);
		}

		protected abstract void ExecuteCommand(object parameter);
	}
	public class MultiColumnComboEditorCommandSource : CommandSource
	{
		public MultiColumnComboEditorCommand CommandType { get; set; }

		protected override ICommand ResolveCommand()
		{
			if (CommandType == MultiColumnComboEditorCommand.ClearSelection)
			{
				return new MultiColumnComboEditorClearSelectionCommand();
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class MultiColumnComboEditorClearSelectionCommand : MultiColumnComboEditorCommandBase
	{
		public override bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (!(parameter is XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamMultiColumnComboEditor))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (xamMultiColumnComboEditor.SelectedItems != null && xamMultiColumnComboEditor.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			TextBox textEditor = xamMultiColumnComboEditor.TextEditor;
			if (textEditor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(textEditor.Text);
		}

		protected override void ExecuteCommand(object parameter)
		{
			if (parameter is XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamMultiColumnComboEditor)
			{
				xamMultiColumnComboEditor.ClearCurrentSelection();
			}
		}
	}
	public class HighlightingTextBlock : Control
	{
		private string _lastTextToHighlight;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TextProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HighlightProperty;

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(TextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Brush Highlight
		{
			get
			{
				return (Brush)GetValue(HighlightProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HighlightProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal XamMultiColumnComboEditor ComboEditor { get; set; }

		internal TextBlock TextBlock { get; private set; }

		internal TextWrapping TextBlockTextWrapping { get; set; }

		internal Style TextBlockStyle { get; set; }

		internal bool TextNeedsHighlighting { get; set; }

		private Brush HighlightResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (Highlight != null)
				{
					return Highlight;
				}
				return Brushes.LightGray;
			}
		}

		static HighlightingTextBlock()
		{
			TextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Text", typeof(string), typeof(HighlightingTextBlock), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, TextChanged));
			HighlightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Highlight", typeof(Brush), typeof(HighlightingTextBlock), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnHighlightChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(HighlightingTextBlock), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(HighlightingTextBlock)));
		}

		internal HighlightingTextBlock()
		{
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			HighlightText();
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			TextBlock = GetTemplateChild("TextBlock") as TextBlock;
			if (TextBlock != null)
			{
				TextBlock.TextWrapping = TextBlockTextWrapping;
				TextBlock.Style = TextBlockStyle;
				HighlightText();
			}
		}

		private static void TextChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			HighlightingTextBlock highlightingTextBlock = obj as HighlightingTextBlock;
			if (highlightingTextBlock.TextBlock != null)
			{
				highlightingTextBlock.HighlightText();
			}
		}

		private static void OnHighlightChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((HighlightingTextBlock)d).OnHighlightChanged((Brush)e.OldValue, (Brush)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnHighlightChanged(Brush oldBrush, Brush newBrush)
		{
			if (TextBlock != null)
			{
				HighlightText();
			}
		}

		internal void HighlightText()
		{
			if (ComboEditor != null)
			{
				HighlightText(ComboEditor.CurrentSearchText);
			}
			else
			{
				HighlightText(_lastTextToHighlight);
			}
		}

		internal void HighlightText(string textToHighlight)
		{
			_lastTextToHighlight = textToHighlight;
			if (TextBlock == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			TextBlock.Inlines.Clear();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(textToHighlight))
			{
				TextBlock.Text = Text;
			}
			else
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Text))
				{
					return;
				}
				string text = Text;
				int num = text.ToUpper().IndexOf(textToHighlight.ToUpper());
				if (num < 0)
				{
					TextBlock.Text = text;
					return;
				}
				Run run;
				if (num > 0)
				{
					run = new Run();
					run.Text = text.Substring(0, num);
					TextBlock.Inlines.Add(run);
				}
				run = new Run();
				run.Text = text.Substring(num, textToHighlight.Length);
				if (TextNeedsHighlighting)
				{
					run.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
					run.Background = HighlightResolved;
				}
				TextBlock.Inlines.Add(run);
				int num2 = num + textToHighlight.Length;
				if (num2 < text.Length)
				{
					run = new Run();
					run.Text = text.Substring(num2);
					TextBlock.Inlines.Add(run);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class MultiColumnComboItemsPanel : ItemsPanelBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>
	{
		private List<ComboRowBase> _fixedRowsTop;

		public override List<ComboRowBase> FixedRowsTop
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fixedRowsTop == null)
				{
					_fixedRowsTop = new List<ComboRowBase>();
				}
				return _fixedRowsTop;
			}
		}

		protected override bool UseAvailableWidthToMeasureItem => true;

		protected override void OnControlAttachedToItem(ComboCellsPanel cntrl)
		{
			cntrl.Owner = this;
			base.OnControlAttachedToItem(cntrl);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (base.ComboEditor != null)
			{
				XamMultiColumnComboEditor xamMultiColumnComboEditor = (XamMultiColumnComboEditor)base.ComboEditor;
				xamMultiColumnComboEditor.IsFirstRowRenderingInThisLayoutCycle = true;
				xamMultiColumnComboEditor.OverflowAdjustment = 0.0;
				xamMultiColumnComboEditor.RowWidth = 0.0;
				if (!ComboEditorBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>.IsDPIScaled)
				{
					xamMultiColumnComboEditor.IndexOfFirstColumnRendered = 0;
				}
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected override void UpdateHorizontalScrollBar(ScrollBar horizBar, Size finalSize)
		{
			XamMultiColumnComboEditor obj = (XamMultiColumnComboEditor)base.ComboEditor;
			double overrideHorizontalMax = obj.OverrideHorizontalMax;
			_ = obj.ScrollableCellCount;
			double largeChange = obj.VisibleCellCount;
			if (overrideHorizontalMax != -1.0 && !double.IsPositiveInfinity(overrideHorizontalMax) && !double.IsNaN(overrideHorizontalMax))
			{
				if (horizBar.Value > overrideHorizontalMax)
				{
					horizBar.Value = overrideHorizontalMax;
				}
				horizBar.Maximum = overrideHorizontalMax;
				horizBar.ViewportSize = 1.0;
			}
			horizBar.LargeChange = largeChange;
			horizBar.SmallChange = 1.0;
			bool flag = !NeedsHorizontalScrollbar(finalSize);
			if (horizBar.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				horizBar.Visibility = (flag ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				horizBar.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			else if (flag && horizBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				horizBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (horizBar.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				horizBar.Value = 0.0;
			}
		}

		private bool NeedsHorizontalScrollbar(Size finalSize)
		{
			XamMultiColumnComboEditor obj = (XamMultiColumnComboEditor)base.ComboEditor;
			_ = finalSize.Width;
			new List<ComboColumn>();
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (ComboColumn column in obj.Columns)
			{
				if (column.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && !(column is FillerComboColumn) && column.IsFixed == ComboColumnFixedState.NotFixed)
				{
					num += column.ActualWidth;
				}
			}
			return num > finalSize.Width;
		}

		protected override void ArrangeItem(FrameworkElement elem, double left, double top, double width)
		{
			elem.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, top, elem.DesiredSize.Width, elem.DesiredSize.Height));
		}

		internal void RegisterFixedRowTop(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			if (!FixedRowsTop.Contains(row))
			{
				FixedRowsTop.Add(row);
			}
		}

		internal void UnregisterFixedRowTop(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			if (FixedRowsTop.Contains(row))
			{
				FixedRowsTop.Remove(row);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ComboCellsPanel : Panel
	{
		private ComboRowBase _row;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _visibleCells;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _visibleFixedLeftCells;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _visibleFixedRightCells;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _additionalCells;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _lastCellsDetached;

		private Collection<ComboCellBase> _nonReleasedCells;

		private MultiColumnComboItemsPanel _owner;

		private XamMultiColumnComboEditor _comboEditor;

		private RectangleGeometry _clipRG;

		private double _prevAvailableWidth = -1.0;

		private Rect _hideCellRect = new Rect(-1000.0, -1000.0, 0.0, 0.0);

		public ComboRowBase Row => _row;

		public MultiColumnComboItemsPanel Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return _owner;
			}
			set
			{
				_owner = value;
				if (value != null)
				{
					_comboEditor = (XamMultiColumnComboEditor)_owner.ComboEditor;
				}
				else
				{
					_comboEditor = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal Collection<ComboCellBase> VisibleCells => _visibleCells;

		protected internal Collection<ComboCellBase> VisibleFixedLeftCells => _visibleFixedLeftCells;

		protected internal Collection<ComboCellBase> VisibleFixedRightCells => _visibleFixedRightCells;

		internal double PreviousInvalidateHeight { get; set; }

		internal bool ArrangeRaised { get; set; }

		internal bool MeasureRaised { get; set; }

		internal RectangleGeometry ClipRect => _clipRG;

		public ComboCellsPanel()
		{
			_visibleCells = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			_visibleFixedLeftCells = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			_visibleFixedRightCells = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			_additionalCells = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			_clipRG = new RectangleGeometry();
			base.Clip = _clipRG;
			_lastCellsDetached = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			_nonReleasedCells = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnAttached(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			_row = row;
			base.DataContext = _row.Data;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnReleased(ComboRowBase row)
		{
			_row = null;
			base.DataContext = null;
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedLeftCell in _visibleFixedLeftCells)
			{
				ReleaseCell(visibleFixedLeftCell);
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleCell in _visibleCells)
			{
				ReleaseCell(visibleCell);
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedRightCell in _visibleFixedRightCells)
			{
				ReleaseCell(visibleFixedRightCell);
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase additionalCell in _additionalCells)
			{
				ReleaseCell(additionalCell);
			}
			_additionalCells.Clear();
			_visibleCells.Clear();
			_visibleFixedLeftCells.Clear();
			_visibleFixedRightCells.Clear();
			_lastCellsDetached.Clear();
		}

		protected virtual Size RenderCells(double availableWidth)
		{
			Size result;
			if (_comboEditor.IsFirstRowRenderingInThisLayoutCycle)
			{
				result = RenderCellsComplex(availableWidth);
				_comboEditor.IsFirstRowRenderingInThisLayoutCycle = false;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_comboEditor.InvalidateOverrideHorizontalMax)
				{
					_comboEditor.InvalidateOverrideHorizontalMax = false;
					_comboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = -1.0;
				}
				bool isInfinite = double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableWidth);
				ReadOnlyCollection<ComboColumn> visibleColumns = _comboEditor.Columns.VisibleColumns;
				double maxHeight = 0.0;
				Collection<ComboCellBase> starColumns = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
				double num = 0.0;
				foreach (ComboColumn fixedAdornerColumn in _comboEditor.Columns.FixedAdornerColumns)
				{
					double actualWidth = fixedAdornerColumn.ActualWidth;
					RenderCell(fixedAdornerColumn, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedLeftCells, isInfinite);
					if (actualWidth != fixedAdornerColumn.ActualWidth)
					{
						VisibleFixedLeftCells.Clear();
						return RenderCellsComplex(availableWidth);
					}
					if (fixedAdornerColumn.WidthResolved.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
					{
						num += actualWidth;
					}
				}
				FixedComboColumnsCollection fixedColumnsLeft = _comboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsLeft;
				if (fixedColumnsLeft.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (ComboColumn item in fixedColumnsLeft)
					{
						if (item.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							double actualWidth2 = item.ActualWidth;
							RenderCell(item, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedLeftCells, isInfinite);
							if (actualWidth2 != item.ActualWidth)
							{
								ReleaseCells(VisibleFixedLeftCells);
								return RenderCellsComplex(availableWidth);
							}
							if (item.WidthResolved.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
							{
								num += actualWidth2;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (ResolveScrollLeft() == -1.0 && Row.RowType == RowType.DataRow)
				{
					InvalidateHorizontalMax(availableWidth - num, ref maxHeight, isInfinite, includeStarCols: false);
					ResolveScrollLeft();
					Owner.MeasureCalled = false;
					Owner.InvalidateMeasure();
				}
				fixedColumnsLeft = _comboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsRight;
				if (fixedColumnsLeft.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (ComboColumn item2 in fixedColumnsLeft)
					{
						if (item2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							double actualWidth3 = item2.ActualWidth;
							RenderCell(item2, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedRightCells, isInfinite);
							if (actualWidth3 != item2.ActualWidth)
							{
								ReleaseCells(VisibleFixedLeftCells);
								ReleaseCells(VisibleFixedRightCells);
								return RenderCellsComplex(availableWidth);
							}
							if (item2.WidthResolved.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
							{
								num += actualWidth3;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				int num2 = _comboEditor.TotalColumnsRendered + _comboEditor.IndexOfFirstColumnRendered;
				if (num2 > visibleColumns.Count)
				{
					num2 = visibleColumns.Count;
				}
				if (ComboEditorBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>.IsDPIScaled)
				{
					num2 = visibleColumns.Count;
				}
				int num3 = ((!ComboEditorBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>.IsDPIScaled) ? _comboEditor.IndexOfFirstColumnRendered : 0);
				_ = _comboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Value;
				for (int i = num3; i < num2; i++)
				{
					ComboColumn comboColumn = visibleColumns[i];
					double actualWidth4 = comboColumn.ActualWidth;
					RenderCell(comboColumn, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleCells, isInfinite);
					if (actualWidth4 != comboColumn.ActualWidth)
					{
						ReleaseCells(VisibleFixedLeftCells);
						ReleaseCells(VisibleFixedRightCells);
						ReleaseCells(VisibleCells);
						return RenderCellsComplex(availableWidth);
					}
					if (comboColumn.WidthResolved.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
					{
						num += actualWidth4;
					}
				}
				_comboEditor.RowWidth = num;
				SetupStarCells(availableWidth, _comboEditor.RowWidth, ref maxHeight, starColumns);
				result = new Size(_comboEditor.RowWidth, maxHeight);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual double RenderCell(ComboColumn column, Collection<ComboCellBase> starColumns, ref double maxHeight, bool insert, Collection<ComboCellBase> visibleCells, bool isInfinite)
		{
			double actualWidth = column.ActualWidth;
			ComboColumnWidth widthResolved = column.WidthResolved;
			ComboColumnWidthType comboColumnWidthType = widthResolved.WidthType;
			double width = double.PositiveInfinity;
			if (comboColumnWidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Numeric)
			{
				width = widthResolved.Value;
			}
			else if (isInfinite && comboColumnWidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
			{
				comboColumnWidthType = ComboColumnWidthType.Auto;
			}
			else if (comboColumnWidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.InitialAuto && column.IsInitialAutoSet && column.ActualWidth != 0.0)
			{
				width = column.ActualWidth;
			}
			double height = double.PositiveInfinity;
			if (column.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			if (isInfinite && column is FillerComboColumn)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			ComboCellBase comboCellBase = Row.ResolveCell(column);
			if (comboCellBase != null)
			{
				if (!insert)
				{
					visibleCells.Add(comboCellBase);
				}
				else
				{
					visibleCells.Insert(0, comboCellBase);
				}
				Size empty = Size.Empty;
				if (comboCellBase.Control == null)
				{
					RecyclingManager.Manager.AttachElement(comboCellBase, this);
					comboCellBase.Control.EnsureContent();
					comboCellBase.EnsureCurrentState();
					comboCellBase.MeasuringSize = Size.Empty;
					empty = new Size(width, height);
				}
				else
				{
					comboCellBase.ApplyStyle();
					comboCellBase.Control.EnsureContent();
					comboCellBase.EnsureCurrentState();
					empty = ((!comboCellBase.MeasuringSize.IsEmpty) ? comboCellBase.MeasuringSize : new Size(width, height));
				}
				if (comboColumnWidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
				{
					comboCellBase.Control.IsMeasureRaised = false;
					comboCellBase.Control.Measure(empty);
					if (!comboCellBase.Control.IsMeasureRaised && comboCellBase.Control.Content is FrameworkElement { DesiredSize: { Width: var width2 } } frameworkElement && (width2 > comboCellBase.Control.DesiredSize.Width || frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Width < 10.0))
					{
						comboCellBase.Control.Measure(new Size(1.0, 1.0));
						comboCellBase.Control.Measure(new Size(width, height));
					}
				}
				ComboCellControlBase control = comboCellBase.Control;
				double num = control.DesiredSize.Width;
				if (isInfinite && comboColumnWidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
				{
					comboColumnWidthType = ComboColumnWidthType.Auto;
					if (num < control.ActualWidth)
					{
						num = control.ActualWidth;
					}
				}
				switch (comboColumnWidthType)
				{
				case ComboColumnWidthType.InitialAuto:
					if (column.ActualWidth == 0.0)
					{
						column.IsInitialAutoSet = false;
					}
					if (!column.IsInitialAutoSet && column.ActualWidth < num)
					{
						column.ActualWidth = num;
					}
					break;
				case ComboColumnWidthType.Auto:
					if (column.ActualWidth < num)
					{
						column.ActualWidth = num;
					}
					break;
				case ComboColumnWidthType.Numeric:
					column.ActualWidth = comboCellBase.Column.WidthResolved.Value;
					break;
				case ComboColumnWidthType.SizeToHeader:
					if (Row.RowType == RowType.HeaderRow && column.ActualWidth < num)
					{
						column.ActualWidth = num;
					}
					break;
				case ComboColumnWidthType.SizeToCells:
					if (Row.RowType != RowType.HeaderRow && column.ActualWidth < num)
					{
						column.ActualWidth = num;
					}
					break;
				case ComboColumnWidthType.Star:
					starColumns?.Add(comboCellBase);
					return 0.0;
				}
				if (column.ActualWidth < column.MinimumWidth)
				{
					column.ActualWidth = column.MinimumWidth;
				}
				if (column.ActualWidth > column.MaximumWidth)
				{
					column.ActualWidth = column.MaximumWidth;
				}
				if (actualWidth != column.ActualWidth)
				{
					_comboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = -1.0;
				}
				maxHeight = Math.Max(maxHeight, comboCellBase.Control.DesiredSize.Height);
				return column.ActualWidth;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellMouseOver()
		{
			InternalEnsureAllCellVisualStates();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellMouseLeave()
		{
			InternalEnsureAllCellVisualStates();
		}

		protected double SetupStarCells(double availableWidth, double currentWidth, ref double maxHeight, Collection<ComboCellBase> starColumns)
		{
			double num = availableWidth - currentWidth;
			double num2 = num;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			if (num > 0.0)
			{
				if (starColumns.Count > 0)
				{
					double num4 = 0.0;
					foreach (ComboCellBase starColumn in starColumns)
					{
						num4 += starColumn.Column.WidthResolved.Value;
					}
					if (num4 != 0.0)
					{
						double num5 = num / num4;
						bool flag = false;
						List<ComboCellBase> list = new List<ComboCellBase>(starColumns);
						do
						{
							flag = false;
							List<ComboCellBase> list2 = new List<ComboCellBase>();
							foreach (ComboCellBase item2 in list)
							{
								if ((double)(int)num5 * item2.Column.WidthResolved.Value < item2.Column.MinimumWidth)
								{
									num -= item2.Column.MinimumWidth;
									num4 -= item2.Column.WidthResolved.Value;
									item2.Column.ActualWidth = item2.Column.MinimumWidth;
									if (!Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(item2.Column))
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.Add(item2.Column, item2.Column.MinimumWidth);
									}
									else
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[item2.Column] = item2.Column.MinimumWidth;
									}
									flag = true;
								}
								else
								{
									list2.Add(item2);
								}
							}
							list = list2;
							num5 = num / num4;
						}
						while (flag);
						if (num > 0.0)
						{
							if (list.Count > 0)
							{
								foreach (ComboCellBase item3 in list)
								{
									item3.Column.ActualWidth = (double)(int)num5 * item3.Column.WidthResolved.Value;
									if (!Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(item3.Column))
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.Add(item3.Column, item3.Column.ActualWidth);
									}
									else
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[item3.Column] = item3.Column.ActualWidth;
									}
									num3 += num5 - (double)(int)num5;
								}
								int num6 = 0;
								while (num3 > 1.0)
								{
									ComboCellBase comboCellBase = starColumns[num6];
									comboCellBase.Column.ActualWidth += 1.0;
									if (!Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(comboCellBase.Column))
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.Add(comboCellBase.Column, comboCellBase.Column.ActualWidth);
									}
									else
									{
										Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[comboCellBase.Column] = comboCellBase.Column.ActualWidth;
									}
									num6++;
									num3 -= 1.0;
								}
								list[list.Count - 1].Column.ActualWidth += num3;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num2 = 0.0;
							foreach (ComboCellBase item4 in list)
							{
								item4.Column.ActualWidth = item4.Column.MinimumWidth;
								if (!Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(item4.Column))
								{
									Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.Add(item4.Column, item4.Column.ActualWidth);
								}
								else
								{
									Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[item4.Column] = item4.Column.ActualWidth;
								}
							}
						}
						foreach (ComboCellBase starColumn2 in starColumns)
						{
							double num7 = 0.0;
							if (Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(starColumn2.Column))
							{
								num7 = Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[starColumn2.Column];
							}
							InvalidateMeasureOfElementAndDescendants(starColumn2.Control);
							starColumn2.Control.Measure(new Size(num7, double.PositiveInfinity));
							num2 -= num7;
							maxHeight = Math.Max(maxHeight, starColumn2.Control.DesiredSize.Height);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ReadOnlyCollection<ComboColumn> starColumns2 = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.StarColumns;
				if (starColumns2.Count > starColumns.Count)
				{
					foreach (ComboColumn item5 in starColumns2)
					{
						ComboCellBase item = Row.Cells[item5];
						if (!VisibleCells.Contains(item))
						{
							bool num8 = starColumns.Contains(item);
							RenderCell(item5, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleCells, isInfinite: false);
							VisibleCells.Remove(item);
							if (!num8)
							{
								starColumns.Remove(item);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				num2 = 0.0;
				foreach (ComboCellBase starColumn3 in starColumns)
				{
					starColumn3.Column.ActualWidth = starColumn3.Column.MinimumWidth;
					if (!Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(starColumn3.Column))
					{
						Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.Add(starColumn3.Column, starColumn3.Column.ActualWidth);
					}
					else
					{
						Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[starColumn3.Column] = starColumn3.Column.ActualWidth;
					}
					starColumn3.Control.Measure(new Size(starColumn3.Column.ActualWidth, double.PositiveInfinity));
					maxHeight = Math.Max(maxHeight, starColumn3.Control.DesiredSize.Height);
					num2 -= starColumn3.Column.ActualWidth;
				}
				Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FillerColumn.ActualWidth = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(num2))
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			return currentWidth + Math.Abs(num2);
		}

		private Size RenderCellsComplex(double availableWidth)
		{
			double maxHeight = 0.0;
			ReadOnlyCollection<ComboColumn> visibleColumns = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.VisibleColumns;
			bool flag = double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableWidth);
			int num = visibleColumns.Count;
			Collection<ComboCellBase> starColumns = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			foreach (ComboColumn fixedAdornerColumn in Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FixedAdornerColumns)
			{
				if (num2 >= availableWidth)
				{
					break;
				}
				num2 += RenderCell(fixedAdornerColumn, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedLeftCells, flag);
			}
			FixedComboColumnsCollection fixedColumnsLeft = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsLeft;
			if (fixedColumnsLeft.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (ComboColumn item in fixedColumnsLeft)
				{
					if (item.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						if (num2 >= availableWidth)
						{
							break;
						}
						num2 += RenderCell(item, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedLeftCells, flag);
					}
				}
			}
			fixedColumnsLeft = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FixedColumnsRight;
			if (fixedColumnsLeft.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (ComboColumn item2 in fixedColumnsLeft)
				{
					if (item2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						if (num2 >= availableWidth)
						{
							break;
						}
						num2 += RenderCell(item2, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleFixedRightCells, flag);
					}
				}
			}
			num3 = num2;
			int num4 = 0;
			int num5 = 0;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			double num7 = 0.0;
			double num8 = ResolveScrollLeft();
			if (num8 == -1.0)
			{
				if (Row.RowType == RowType.DataRow)
				{
					InvalidateHorizontalMax(availableWidth - num3, ref maxHeight, flag, includeStarCols: false);
					num8 = ResolveScrollLeft();
					Owner.MeasureCalled = false;
					Owner.InvalidateMeasure();
				}
				else
				{
					InvalidateHorizontalMax(availableWidth - num3, ref maxHeight, flag, includeStarCols: false);
					num8 = ResolveScrollLeft();
					if (num8 != 0.0)
					{
						_comboEditor.InvalidateOverrideHorizontalMax = true;
					}
				}
			}
			num4 = (int)num8;
			if (num4 < visibleColumns.Count)
			{
				ComboColumn column = visibleColumns[num4];
				num7 = RenderCell(column, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleCells, flag);
				num2 += num7;
				double num9 = num8 - (double)(int)num8;
				num6 = num7 * num9;
				num2 -= num6;
				double num10 = 0.0;
				for (num5 = num4 + 1; num5 < num; num5++)
				{
					if (num2 >= availableWidth)
					{
						break;
					}
					ComboColumn column2 = visibleColumns[num5];
					num10 = RenderCell(column2, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, VisibleCells, flag);
					num2 += num10;
				}
				num2 += num6;
			}
			num4--;
			num2 = SetupStarCells(availableWidth, num2, ref maxHeight, starColumns);
			if (num2 < availableWidth && _visibleCells.Count < num)
			{
				for (num5 = num4; num5 >= 0; num5--)
				{
					ComboColumn comboColumn = visibleColumns[num5];
					if (comboColumn is FillerComboColumn)
					{
						num4--;
					}
					num7 = RenderCell(comboColumn, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: true, VisibleCells, flag);
					num2 += num7;
				}
			}
			InvalidateHorizontalMax(availableWidth - num3, ref maxHeight, flag, includeStarCols: true);
			_comboEditor.OverflowAdjustment = Math.Max(_comboEditor.OverflowAdjustment, num2 - availableWidth);
			if (flag)
			{
				num--;
			}
			_comboEditor.ScrollableCellCount = num;
			_comboEditor.VisibleCellCount = _visibleCells.Count;
			_comboEditor.CurrentVisibleWidth = num2 - num3;
			if (!ComboEditorBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>.IsDPIScaled)
			{
				_comboEditor.IndexOfFirstColumnRendered = num4 + 1;
			}
			_comboEditor.TotalColumnsRendered = VisibleCells.Count;
			_comboEditor.RowWidth = num2;
			return new Size(num2, maxHeight);
		}

		internal void ReleaseCell(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			if (cell.Control != null && cell.Control.Parent == this && !RecyclingManager.Manager.ReleaseElement(cell, this))
			{
				_nonReleasedCells.Add(cell);
			}
		}

		private void ReleaseCells(Collection<ComboCellBase> cells)
		{
			foreach (ComboCellBase cell in cells)
			{
				ReleaseCell(cell);
			}
			cells.Clear();
		}

		private double ResolveScrollLeft()
		{
			return _comboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar?.Value ?? 0.0;
		}

		internal void SetScrollLeft(double scrollLeft)
		{
			ScrollBar horizontalScrollBar = _comboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar;
			if (horizontalScrollBar != null && Row.ComboEditor.ScrollableCellCount - 1 > 0)
			{
				horizontalScrollBar.Value = scrollLeft;
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateHorizontalMax(double availWidth, ref double maxHeight, bool isInfinite, bool includeStarCols)
		{
			if (_prevAvailableWidth != availWidth)
			{
				Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = -1.0;
			}
			_prevAvailableWidth = availWidth;
			if (Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax != -1.0 || isInfinite)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ComboCellBase> list = new List<ComboCellBase>(_lastCellsDetached);
			_lastCellsDetached.Clear();
			foreach (ComboColumn visibleColumn in Row.ComboEditor.Columns.VisibleColumns)
			{
				if (!(visibleColumn is FillerComboColumn) && visibleColumn.WidthResolved.WidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Star && visibleColumn.ActualWidth > visibleColumn.MinimumWidth && Row.Cells[visibleColumn].Control != null)
				{
					Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = -1.0;
					return;
				}
			}
			Collection<ComboCellBase> starColumns = new Collection<ComboCellBase>();
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			int num3 = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.VisibleColumns.Count - 1;
			while (num2 < availWidth && num3 >= 0)
			{
				ComboColumn comboColumn = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.VisibleColumns[num3];
				if (comboColumn.WidthResolved.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star)
				{
					num = RenderCell(comboColumn, starColumns, ref maxHeight, insert: false, _lastCellsDetached, isInfinite);
				}
				else if (includeStarCols && !(comboColumn is FillerComboColumn))
				{
					num = comboColumn.ActualWidth;
				}
				num2 += num;
				num3--;
			}
			for (int num4 = _lastCellsDetached.Count - 1; num4 >= 0; num4--)
			{
				ComboCellBase comboCellBase = _lastCellsDetached[num4];
				if (VisibleCells.Contains(comboCellBase))
				{
					_lastCellsDetached.Remove(comboCellBase);
				}
				else
				{
					ReleaseCell(comboCellBase);
				}
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase item in list)
			{
				if (!VisibleCells.Contains(item) && !VisibleFixedLeftCells.Contains(item) && !VisibleFixedRightCells.Contains(item) && item.Control != null)
				{
					ReleaseCell(item);
				}
			}
			num3++;
			double num5 = (num2 - availWidth) / num;
			Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = (double)num3 + num5;
			if (Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax < 0.0)
			{
				Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax = 0.0;
			}
		}

		private double CalculateActualHeight(double calculateRowSize)
		{
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleCell in VisibleCells)
			{
				calculateRowSize = Math.Max(calculateRowSize, visibleCell.Control.DesiredSize.Height);
			}
			return calculateRowSize;
		}

		private static void InvalidateMeasureOfElementAndDescendants(DependencyObject v)
		{
			if (v is UIElement uIElement)
			{
				uIElement.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			int i = 0;
			for (int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(v); i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				InvalidateMeasureOfElementAndDescendants(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(v, i));
			}
		}

		internal void InternalCellMouseEnter()
		{
			OnCellMouseOver();
		}

		internal void InternalCellMouseLeave()
		{
			OnCellMouseLeave();
		}

		internal void RenderCell(ComboCellBase cell)
		{
			double maxHeight = 0.0;
			RenderCell(cell.Column, new Collection<ComboCellBase>(), ref maxHeight, insert: false, _visibleCells, isInfinite: false);
		}

		internal void InternalEnsureAllCellVisualStates()
		{
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedLeftCell in _visibleFixedLeftCells)
			{
				visibleFixedLeftCell.EnsureCurrentState();
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleCell in _visibleCells)
			{
				visibleCell.EnsureCurrentState();
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedRightCell in _visibleFixedRightCells)
			{
				visibleFixedRightCell.EnsureCurrentState();
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			MeasureRaised = true;
			if (Row == null || (availableSize.Height == 1.0 && availableSize.Width == 1.0))
			{
				return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			}
			PreviousInvalidateHeight = availableSize.Height;
			if (Owner != null && !Owner.InLayoutPhase)
			{
				Owner.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			bool flag = double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableSize.Width);
			List<ComboCellBase> list = new List<ComboCellBase>(_visibleCells);
			list.AddRange(_visibleFixedLeftCells);
			list.AddRange(_additionalCells);
			list.AddRange(_visibleFixedRightCells);
			_additionalCells.Clear();
			_visibleCells.Clear();
			_visibleFixedLeftCells.Clear();
			_visibleFixedRightCells.Clear();
			Size size = RenderCells(availableSize.Width);
			Row.ActualHeight = CalculateActualHeight(size.Height);
			foreach (ComboCellBase item in list)
			{
				if (!_visibleCells.Contains(item) && !_visibleFixedLeftCells.Contains(item) && !_visibleFixedRightCells.Contains(item) && !_additionalCells.Contains(item))
				{
					ReleaseCell(item);
				}
			}
			double width = availableSize.Width;
			if (flag)
			{
				width = size.Width;
			}
			return new Size(width, Row.ActualHeight);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			ArrangeRaised = true;
			if (Row == null)
			{
				return finalSize;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement availableElement in RecyclingManager.Manager.GetAvailableElements(this))
			{
				availableElement.Arrange(_hideCellRect);
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase nonReleasedCell in _nonReleasedCells)
			{
				if (nonReleasedCell.Control != null)
				{
					nonReleasedCell.Control.Arrange(_hideCellRect);
				}
			}
			_nonReleasedCells.Clear();
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = finalSize.Width - num;
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			_clipRG.Rect = new Rect(num, 0.0, num2, finalSize.Height);
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedLeftCell in _visibleFixedLeftCells)
			{
				ComboColumn column = visibleFixedLeftCell.Column;
				FrameworkElement control = visibleFixedLeftCell.Control;
				double actualWidth = column.ActualWidth;
				double height = Math.Max(finalSize.Height, control.DesiredSize.Height);
				double x = num;
				control.Arrange(new Rect(x, 0.0, actualWidth, height));
				num += actualWidth;
				Panel.SetZIndex(control, 1);
			}
			double num3 = finalSize.Width;
			double actualWidth2 = Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FillerColumn.ActualWidth;
			if (actualWidth2 > 0.0)
			{
				num3 -= actualWidth2;
			}
			foreach (ComboCellBase visibleFixedRightCell in _visibleFixedRightCells)
			{
				_ = visibleFixedRightCell.Column;
				FrameworkElement control2 = visibleFixedRightCell.Control;
				double actualWidth3 = visibleFixedRightCell.Column.ActualWidth;
				double num4 = num3;
				num3 -= actualWidth3;
				num4 -= actualWidth3;
				control2.Arrange(new Rect(num4, 0.0, actualWidth3, Math.Max(finalSize.Height, control2.DesiredSize.Height)));
				Panel.SetZIndex(control2, 1);
			}
			if (Row.CanScrollHorizontally)
			{
				ScrollBar horizontalScrollBar = _comboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar;
				if (horizontalScrollBar != null && horizontalScrollBar.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					if (Row.ComboEditor.OverrideHorizontalMax > 0.0)
					{
						Row.ComboEditor.Columns.FillerColumn.ActualWidth = 0.0;
					}
					if (horizontalScrollBar.Value != horizontalScrollBar.Maximum)
					{
						if (_visibleCells.Count > 0)
						{
							double num5 = ResolveScrollLeft();
							double num6 = num5 - (double)(int)num5;
							double num7 = _visibleCells[0].Column.ActualWidth * num6;
							if (Row.ComboEditor.CurrentVisibleWidth + num > num3)
							{
								num += 0.0 - num7;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (Row.ComboEditor.CurrentVisibleWidth + num > num3)
					{
						num -= Row.ComboEditor.OverflowAdjustment;
					}
				}
			}
			if (ComboEditorBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel>.IsDPIScaled)
			{
				num += Row.ComboEditor.HorizontalScrollBar.Value * -1.0;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _visibleCells.Count; i++)
			{
				ComboCellBase comboCellBase = _visibleCells[i];
				ComboColumn column2 = comboCellBase.Column;
				if (!column2.IsInitialAutoSet && !Owner.FixedRowsTop.Contains(Row) && comboCellBase.Control.IsCellLoaded)
				{
					column2.IsInitialAutoSet = true;
				}
				ComboCellControlBase control3 = comboCellBase.Control;
				double num8 = comboCellBase.Column.ActualWidth;
				if (comboCellBase.Column.WidthResolved.WidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Star && Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths.ContainsKey(comboCellBase.Column))
				{
					num8 = Row.ComboEditor.StarColumnWidths[comboCellBase.Column];
				}
				if (column2 is FillerComboColumn)
				{
					num8 = Math.Max(finalSize.Width - num, 0.0);
				}
				control3.Arrange(new Rect(num, 0.0, num8, Math.Max(finalSize.Height, control3.DesiredSize.Height)));
				num += num8;
				Panel.SetZIndex(control3, 0);
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new ComboCellsPanelAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class SpecializedTextBox : TextBox
	{
		private bool _keyPressed;

		private bool _allowTextChanges;

		private bool _isCompositionUsed;

		private ScrollViewer _scrollViewer;

		internal bool AllowTextChanges
		{
			get
			{
				if (_keyPressed)
				{
					return _allowTextChanges;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsComposition => _isCompositionUsed;

		internal ScrollViewer ScrollViewer => _scrollViewer;

		public SpecializedTextBox()
		{
			_keyPressed = false;
			_allowTextChanges = false;
			_isCompositionUsed = false;
			base.Focusable = true;
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			_allowTextChanges = !_isCompositionUsed;
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTextInput(e);
			_allowTextChanges = true;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			_isCompositionUsed = e.Key == Key.ImeProcessed;
			_allowTextChanges = !_isCompositionUsed;
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
			if (e.Key == Key.ImeProcessed)
			{
				_keyPressed = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_keyPressed = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTextChanged(TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_allowTextChanges = false;
			base.OnTextChanged(e);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_scrollViewer = GetTemplateChild("PART_ContentHost") as ScrollViewer;
		}

		internal void ScrollToRightEnd()
		{
			if (ScrollViewer != null)
			{
				ScrollViewer.ScrollToRightEnd();
			}
		}
	}
	public class ComboColumnWidthTypeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			return sourceType == typeof(string);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			if (value is string)
			{
				string text = value.ToString().ToUpper(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				double result = -1.0;
				if (double.TryParse(text, out result))
				{
					return new ComboColumnWidth(result, isStar: false);
				}
				if (text.IndexOf('*') != -1)
				{
					text = text.Replace('*', ' ');
					if (double.TryParse(text, out result))
					{
						return new ComboColumnWidth(result, isStar: true);
					}
					return ComboColumnWidth.Star;
				}
				switch (text)
				{
				case "AUTO":
					return ComboColumnWidth.Auto;
				case "SIZETOCELLS":
					return ComboColumnWidth.SizeToCells;
				case "SIZETOHEADER":
					return ComboColumnWidth.SizeToHeader;
				case "INITIALAUTO":
					return ComboColumnWidth.InitialAuto;
				case "STAR":
					return ComboColumnWidth.Star;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			return destinationType == typeof(string);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			string result = "";
			if (value != null)
			{
				ComboColumnWidth comboColumnWidth = (ComboColumnWidth)value;
				result = ((comboColumnWidth.WidthType == ComboColumnWidthType.Numeric) ? comboColumnWidth.Value.ToString() : ((comboColumnWidth.WidthType != ComboColumnWidthType.Star) ? comboColumnWidth.WidthType.ToString() : (comboColumnWidth.Value + "*")));
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.AutomationPeers
{
	public class ComboCellsPanelAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, IValueProvider, ISelectionItemProvider
	{
		private ComboCellsPanel OwningComboCellsPanel => (ComboCellsPanel)base.Owner;

		private ItemsPanelBase<ComboRow, ComboCellsPanel> Panel => OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.ComboEditor.Panel;

		public bool IsReadOnly => true;

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningComboCellsPanel != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.Data != null)
				{
					return OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.Data.ToString();
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningComboCellsPanel != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null)
				{
					return OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.IsSelected;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple SelectionContainer
		{
			get
			{
				if (Panel != null)
				{
					AutomationPeer peer = UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(Panel) ?? UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(Panel);
					return ProviderFromPeer(peer);
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public ComboCellsPanelAutomationPeer(ComboCellsPanel owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
			if (OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null)
			{
				OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.PropertyChanged += Row_PropertyChanged;
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return OwningComboCellsPanel.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			string nameCore = base.GetNameCore();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameCore))
			{
				return Value;
			}
			return nameCore;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			return patternInterface switch
			{
				PatternInterface.SelectionItem => this, 
				PatternInterface.Value => this, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		internal void RaiseIsSelectedPropertyChanged(bool isSelected)
		{
			if (AutomationPeer.ListenerExists(AutomationEvents.PropertyChanged))
			{
				RaisePropertyChangedEvent(SelectionItemPatternIdentifiers.IsSelectedProperty, !isSelected, isSelected);
			}
		}

		private void Row_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "IsSelected")
			{
				RaiseIsSelectedPropertyChanged(IsSelected);
			}
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
		}

		public void AddToSelection()
		{
			if (OwningComboCellsPanel != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null)
			{
				OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.IsSelected = true;
			}
		}

		public void RemoveFromSelection()
		{
			if (OwningComboCellsPanel != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null)
			{
				OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.IsSelected = false;
			}
		}

		public void Select()
		{
			if (OwningComboCellsPanel != null && OwningComboCellsPanel.Row != null)
			{
				OwningComboCellsPanel.Row.IsSelected = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class XamComboEditorAutomationPeer<T, TControl> : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, ISelectionProvider, IExpandCollapseProvider, IValueProvider where T : ComboEditorItemBase<TControl> where TControl : FrameworkElement
	{
		private ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> OwningComboEditor => (ComboEditorBase<T, TControl>)base.Owner;

		public bool CanSelectMultiple => OwningComboEditor.AllowMultipleSelection;

		public bool IsSelectionRequired => false;

		public ExpandCollapseState ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningComboEditor.IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningComboEditor.IsEnabled)
				{
					return !OwningComboEditor.IsEditableResolved;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (OwningComboEditor.IsEditableResolved)
				{
					text = OwningComboEditor.TextEditor.Text;
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (object child in OwningComboEditor.MultiSelectPanel.Children)
					{
						if (child.GetType().Equals(typeof(ContentControl)))
						{
							text += (child as ContentControl).Content.ToString();
						}
						else if (child.GetType().Equals(typeof(TextBlock)))
						{
							text += (child as TextBlock).Text;
						}
					}
				}
				return text;
			}
		}

		public XamComboEditorAutomationPeer(ComboEditorBase<T, TControl> owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
			OwningComboEditor.DropDownOpened += OwningComboEditor_DropDownOpened;
			OwningComboEditor.DropDownClosed += OwningComboEditor_DropDownClosed;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return OwningComboEditor.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			string nameCore = base.GetNameCore();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameCore))
			{
				return OwningComboEditor.Name;
			}
			return nameCore;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			return patternInterface switch
			{
				PatternInterface.Selection => this, 
				PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse => this, 
				PatternInterface.Value => this, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		internal void RaiseExpandCollapseStatePropertyChangedEvent(ExpandCollapseState oldValue, ExpandCollapseState newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != newValue && AutomationPeer.ListenerExists(AutomationEvents.PropertyChanged))
			{
				RaisePropertyChangedEvent(ExpandCollapsePatternIdentifiers.ExpandCollapseStateProperty, oldValue, newValue);
			}
		}

		private void OwningComboEditor_DropDownOpened(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RaiseExpandCollapseStatePropertyChangedEvent(ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed, ExpandCollapseState.Expanded);
		}

		private void OwningComboEditor_DropDownClosed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RaiseExpandCollapseStatePropertyChangedEvent(ExpandCollapseState.Expanded, ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed);
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetSelection()
		{
			List<IRawElementProviderSimple> list = new List<IRawElementProviderSimple>();
			if (OwningComboEditor.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < OwningComboEditor.SelectedItems.Count; i++)
				{
					UIElement control = OwningComboEditor.CachedRows[OwningComboEditor.SelectedItems[i]].Control;
					AutomationPeer peer = UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(control) ?? UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(control);
					list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(peer));
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public void Collapse()
		{
			OwningComboEditor.IsDropDownOpen = false;
		}

		public void Expand()
		{
			OwningComboEditor.IsDropDownOpen = true;
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			if (OwningComboEditor.IsEditableResolved)
			{
				OwningComboEditor.TextEditor.Text = value;
			}
		}
	}
